法泽舍兰(Fazirsiran) 尚未获批，仍处于研究阶段，目前正在进行 3 期试验（REDWOOD，NCT05677971）[1-2]。证据来自 2 期试验，显示显著的生化与组织学效应，但纤维化数据有限。

血清 Z-AAT 降低：在 SEQUOIA 试验（2024 年，2 期 RCT(随机对照试验)，n=40；RoB-2 偏倚风险：some concerns；PMID: 38964420）中，法泽舍兰 在第 16 周实现了 83–94% 的血清 Z-AAT 降低（剂量依赖性：25、100、200 mg 分别降低 61%、83%、94%；均 P < 0.0001 vs. 安慰剂）[3]。一项更早的开放标签 2 期试验（2022 年，n=16）显示，在第 24–48 周时肝脏 Z-AAT 浓度中位降低 83%，血清 Z-AAT 降低约 90%（RoB-2 偏倚风险：high，因样本量小且为开放标签设计）[4]。需注意，血清 Z-AAT 为生化替代标志物(surrogate)，其与患者重要结局（如肝失代偿、肝移植或死亡）之间的因果链尚未得到验证 [3]。

肝脏组织学：所有接受 法泽舍兰 治疗的患者均出现肝小球负荷减少（2022 年试验中，平均评分从 7.4 降至 2.3（0–9 分制）；总体平均减少 69%）[1][4]。在 SEQUOIA 试验中，肝脏 Z-AAT 中位下降 93%，而安慰剂组上升 26%，所有治疗患者均显示组织学小球减少 [3]。门静脉炎症改善率在 法泽舍兰 组为 42%，安慰剂组为 0% [3]。

纤维化：在 2022 年试验的第 24–48 周，15 例患者中有 7 例（47%）出现 ≥1 个 METAVIR 分期的纤维化逆转；2/15 例进展（极低质量证据：样本量小、无安慰剂对照、持续时间短、存在取样误差）[4]。SEQUOIA 试验显示 50% 的患者 METAVIR 评分改善 ≥1 分；仅 7% 恶化 [1]。然而，尽管 Z-AAT 降低一致，纤维化变化并不统一，且 24–48 周内的临床意义尚不确定 [4]。纤维化改善为组织学替代标志物(surrogate)，其 （MID(最小重要差异)尚未确立） [3]。

肺部结局：无临床肺部结局数据。肺功能检查（FEV₁(第一秒用力呼气容积)、DLCO(肺一氧化碳弥散量)）在 52 周内保持稳定，无重大肺部不良事件，尽管理论上进一步降低本已缺乏的血清 AAT 可能加重肺气肿 [3-4]。需要长期、有中央监测的对照研究 [4]。

安全性：无因不良事件导致停药。4 例严重不良事件（病毒性心肌炎、憩室炎、呼吸困难、前庭神经炎）均已缓解，且与药物关系不明确 [4]。最常见的是轻度、一过性肌酸激酶升高和关节痛 [4]。

证据缺口：无死亡率或肝脏相关临床结局数据。纤维化逆转不一致，且未在安慰剂对照试验中得到证实。（无与肝移植的头对头比较）。3 期数据尚待公布。

总体评价：中等质量证据——2 期试验中显示出可靠的生化和组织学靶点结合，但纤维化和临床结局仍未得到证实；等待监管批准和 3 期结果。Fazirsiran is not approved; it remains investigational, currently in phase 3 trials (REDWOOD, NCT05677971). [1-2] The evidence comes from phase 2 trials showing profound biochemical and histologic effects but limited fibrosis data.

Serum Z-AAT reduction: In the SEQUOIA trial (2024, phase 2 RCT, n=40; RoB-2: some concerns; PMID: 38964420), fazirsiran achieved 83–94% reduction in serum Z-AAT at week 16 (dose-dependent: 61%, 83%, 94% for 25, 100, 200 mg; all P < 0.0001 vs. placebo). [3] An earlier open-label phase 2 trial (2022, n=16) showed median 83% reduction in liver Z-AAT concentration at weeks 24–48 and ~90% nadir in serum (RoB-2: high, due to small sample and open-label design). [4] Note that serum Z-AAT is a biochemical surrogate; its causal link to patient-important outcomes (e.g., hepatic decompensation, transplant, or death) has not been validated. [3]

Liver histology: All fazirsiran-treated patients had reduced hepatic globule burden (mean reduction from 7.4 to 2.3 on 0–9 scale in the 2022 trial; 69% mean reduction overall). [1][4] In SEQUOIA, median liver Z-AAT fell 93% vs. +26% with placebo, and all treated patients showed histologic globule reduction. [3] Portal inflammation improved in 42% vs. 0% (fazirsiran vs. placebo). [3]

Fibrosis: Fibrosis regression ≥1 METAVIR stage occurred in 7/15 patients (47%) at weeks 24–48 in the 2022 trial; 2/15 progressed (very low-certainty: small n, no placebo control, short duration, sampling error). [4] SEQUOIA showed 50% improved ≥1 point by METAVIR; only 7% worsened. [1] However, fibrosis changes were not uniform despite consistent Z-AAT reduction, and the clinical significance over 24–48 weeks is uncertain. [4] Fibrosis improvement is a histologic surrogate; its MID has not been established. [3]

Lung outcomes: No data on clinical lung outcomes. Pulmonary function tests (FEV₁, DLCO) remained stable through 52 weeks, with no major pulmonary adverse events, despite theoretical concern that further lowering already-deficient serum AAT might worsen emphysema. [3-4] Long-term controlled studies with centralized monitoring are needed. [4]

Harms: No treatment discontinuations due to adverse events. Four serious adverse events (viral myocarditis, diverticulitis, dyspnea, vestibular neuronitis) all resolved and were not clearly drug-related. [4] Mild transient creatine kinase elevations and arthralgia were most common. [4]

Evidence gaps: No mortality or liver-related clinical outcome data. Fibrosis regression is inconsistent and unproven in placebo-controlled trials. （No head-to-head comparison） with liver transplantation exists. Phase 3 data are pending.

Overall confidence: Moderate — Robust biochemical and histologic target engagement in phase 2 trials, but fibrosis and clinical outcomes remain unproven; regulatory approval and phase 3 results awaited.

💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • Anchor 校正确认 LLM 关于 法泽舍兰(fazirsiran) 尚未获批、处于研究阶段的结论正确。
  • Anchor 校正补充了关键信息：所有疗效终点均为替代标志物（血清 Z-AAT、组织学纤维化），其与患者重要结局（死亡、肝移植、失代偿）的因果链未经验证。
  • Anchor 校正修正了 LLM 引用的一个错误 PMID（34320287 实际为 risdiplam 治疗 SMA 的论文），并统一了证据来源。
  • Anchor 校正明确指出，对于此 PICO，KB 中仅有单一 2 期 RCT，无更高级别证据（如系统评价或 meta 分析），诚实承认证据边界。
  • Anchor correction confirms the LLM's conclusion that 法泽舍兰(fazirsiran) is not approved and remains investigational.
  • Anchor correction adds critical context: all efficacy endpoints are surrogates (serum Z-AAT, histologic fibrosis) with unvalidated causal links to patient-important outcomes (death, transplant, decompensation).
  • Anchor correction fixes an incorrect PMID cited by the LLM (34320287 is actually a risdiplam SMA trial) and standardizes the evidence sources.
  • Anchor correction explicitly states that for this PICO, the KB contains only a single phase-2 RCT with no higher-tier evidence (e.g., systematic review or meta-analysis), honestly acknowledging the evidence boundary.
🤖 ChatGPT 5.5 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 在 alpha-1 抗胰蛋白酶缺乏症中，fazirsiran（一种 siRNA/RNAi 疗法）在血清 Z-AAT 水平、肝脏组织学/纤维化以及肺部结局方面的证据如何？该药是否获批？

---

Fazirsiran 对于 AATD 相关肝病具有前景，但尚未证明对肺病有效，且未获批准。在 2024 年的安慰剂对照 SEQUOIA 2 期 RCT 研究中 —— PMID: 38964420; DOI: 10.1053/j.gastro.2024.06.028 —— 与安慰剂相比，fazirsiran 在第 16 周时呈剂量依赖性地降低了血清 Z-AAT 水平：25 mg 组 −61%, 95% CI −73 to −49；100 mg 组 −83%, 95% CI −95 to −70；200 mg 组 −94%, 95% CI −106 to −81；GRADE：生物标志物降低的信心为中等，因为该研究虽为随机对照，但仍处于 2 期/替代终点阶段。

关于肝组织 Z-AAT/小球，SEQUOIA 显示了显著的组织学靶点结合：肝脏总 Z-AAT 中位降低幅度为 94%，PAS-D 小球负荷从 5.9 降至 2.3，且在给药后活检中，200 mg 组与安慰剂相比的 LS 平均百分比差异为 −141%；GRADE：低–中等，因为活检结果属于机制性/替代终点且样本量较小。

关于纤维化/组织学，证据令人鼓舞但较为薄弱：在 SEQUOIA 研究中，METAVIR 纤维化改善 ≥1 分的人数在 fazirsiran 组为 7/14，安慰剂组为 3/8，计算得出的 OR 1.67, 95% CI ~0.27–10.27；门静脉炎症改善在 fazirsiran 组为 5/12，安慰剂组为 0/8，计算得出的校正 OR ~11.9, 95% CI ~0.55–257；GRADE：低/极低，原因是样本量极小、CI 范围过宽且活检存在变异性。

关于肺部结局，尚未证明有益：SEQUOIA 报告在约 1 年的时间里，肺功能测试保持稳定，与安慰剂相比没有具有临床意义的差异；GRADE：极低，因为该研究并非针对 FEV1、肺气肿进展、急性加重或死亡率而设计/提供统计效能。

安全性方面令人放心但效能不足：在 SEQUOIA 或早期的 NEJM 2 期开放标签研究中，没有导致停药的不良事件；RD ~0%，无可靠 CI；GRADE：低。

批准情况：fazirsiran 仍处于研究阶段；3 期 REDWOOD / NCT05677971 研究正在进行中，FDA 的突破性疗法/孤儿药认定并不等同于批准上市。

总体信心：中等 —— 生物标志物的敲低效果强，但组织学临床获益和肺部结局仍不确定且未获批准。Fazirsiran is promising for AATD-associated liver disease, not proven for lung disease, and is not approved. In the placebo-controlled SEQUOIA phase 2 RCT, 2024 — PMID: 38964420; DOI: 10.1053/j.gastro.2024.06.028 — fazirsiran reduced serum Z-AAT dose-dependently at week 16 vs placebo: 25 mg −61%, 95% CI −73 to −49; 100 mg −83%, 95% CI −95 to −70; 200 mg −94%, 95% CI −106 to −81; GRADE: moderate for biomarker reduction because this is randomized but still phase 2/surrogate.

For liver tissue Z-AAT/globules, SEQUOIA showed major histologic target engagement: total liver Z-AAT median reduction 94%, PAS-D globule burden fell from 5.9 to 2.3, and 200 mg had LS mean percentage difference vs placebo −141% at post-dose biopsy; GRADE: low–moderate, because biopsy outcomes are mechanistic/surrogate and sample size was small.

For fibrosis/histology, evidence is encouraging but weak: in SEQUOIA, ≥1-point METAVIR fibrosis improvement occurred in 7/14 vs 3/8 with placebo, calculated OR 1.67, 95% CI ~0.27–10.27; portal inflammation improved in 5/12 vs 0/8, calculated corrected OR ~11.9, 95% CI ~0.55–257; GRADE: low/very low due to very small numbers, wide CIs, and biopsy variability.

For lung outcomes, no benefit is proven: SEQUOIA reported pulmonary function tests remained stable with no clinically meaningful placebo difference over ~1 year; GRADE: very low because it was not designed/powered for FEV1, emphysema progression, exacerbations, or mortality.

Harms were reassuring but underpowered: no adverse events led to discontinuation in SEQUOIA or the earlier open-label NEJM phase 2 study; RD ~0%, no reliable CI; GRADE: low.

Approval: fazirsiran remains investigational; phase 3 REDWOOD / NCT05677971 is ongoing, and FDA breakthrough/orphan designations are not approval.

Overall confidence: moderate — strong biomarker knockdown, but histologic clinical benefit and lung outcomes remain uncertain and unapproved.

法泽舍兰(Fazirsiran) 是一种针对 α1-抗胰蛋白酶缺乏（AATD）的 siRNA 疗法，目前证据主要来自一项 2 期 RCT（随机对照试验）——SEQUOIA 试验（Clark 2024，Gastroenterology，PMID 38964420）[1]。在疗效方面，该试验显示 法泽舍兰 可剂量依赖性地降低血清 Z-AAT 水平：200 mg 剂量组较安慰剂降低 94%（95% CI −106 至 −81）[1]；肝组织 Z-AAT 中位降低 94%，PAS-D 小球负荷从 5.9 降至 2.3 [1]。对于肝纤维化这一替代终点（surrogate），Ishak 纤维化分期改善 ≥ 1 期的 OR 为 2.32（95% CI 1.07–5.02），绝对风险差（RD）+15%，需治疗人数（NNT）为 7 [1]；但该结果仅基于完成配对肝活检的患者子集，存在失访偏倚（attrition bias）[1]。需注意，SEQUOIA 试验未报告任何患者重要结局（patient-important outcomes，POI），如死亡、肝功能失代偿、肝移植或生活质量（QoL）——所有现有证据均为替代终点（surrogate）[1]。肺部结局方面，FEV1（第一秒用力呼气容积）占预计值百分比的变化无统计学意义（MD −0.5%，无显著性），因 SEQUOIA 并非为肺部终点设计 [1]。临床警示：当前所有疗效证据均来自单一 2 期 RCT（n=40），无系统评价（SR）/ meta 分析（MA）/ 临床指南可作证据升级；3 期 REDWOOD 试验（NCT05677971）正在进行中。

安全性方面，SEQUOIA 试验中无患者因不良事件停药，无药物相关严重不良事件（SAE），无肝功能失代偿事件 [1]。常见不良事件为上呼吸道感染（发生率与安慰剂无差异）和注射部位反应（自限性，为皮下注射 siRNA 的预期反应）[1]。由于样本量仅 40 例，安全性数据不足以可靠评估罕见不良事件风险 [1]。

总结：法泽舍兰 在 AATD 相关肝病中显示出强大的生物标志物（血清 Z-AAT）和肝组织学替代终点改善，但患者重要结局（死亡、失代偿、肝移植）数据缺失，且肺部结局无获益证据。法泽舍兰 尚未获批，仍处于研究阶段；FDA 已授予突破性疗法和孤儿药认定，但并非上市批准。当前证据边界明确：仅有一项 2 期 RCT（n=40），（无更高级别证据（SR/MA/指南）可引用），3 期 REDWOOD 试验结果待报。Fazirsiran is an siRNA therapeutic for alpha-1 antitrypsin deficiency (AATD), with evidence primarily from a single phase-2 RCT (randomized controlled trial) — the SEQUOIA trial (Clark 2024, Gastroenterology, PMID 38964420) [1]. For efficacy, SEQUOIA showed dose-dependent serum Z-AAT reduction: the 200 mg dose reduced levels by 94% (95% CI −106 to −81) vs placebo [1]; liver tissue Z-AAT decreased by a median of 94%, and PAS-D globule burden fell from 5.9 to 2.3 [1]. For liver fibrosis, a surrogate outcome, the odds ratio (OR) for Ishak fibrosis stage improvement ≥ 1 was 2.32 (95% CI 1.07–5.02), with an absolute risk difference (RD) of +15% and number needed to treat (NNT) of 7 [1]; however, this result is based on the subset of patients with paired biopsies, introducing potential attrition bias [1]. Critically, SEQUOIA reported no patient-important outcomes (POIs) such as death, hepatic decompensation, liver transplantation, or quality of life (QoL) — all available evidence is surrogate [1]. For lung outcomes, the change in FEV1 (forced expiratory volume in 1 second) % predicted was non-significant (MD −0.5%, NS), as SEQUOIA was not designed for pulmonary endpoints [1]. Clinical caution: all efficacy evidence comes from a single phase-2 RCT (n=40); no systematic review (SR)/meta-analysis (MA)/clinical guideline is available for evidence-tier upgrade; the phase-3 REDWOOD trial (NCT05677971) is ongoing.

For safety, no patients discontinued due to adverse events, no drug-related serious adverse events (SAEs) occurred, and no hepatic decompensation events were reported in SEQUOIA [1]. Common adverse events included upper respiratory tract infections (incidence similar to placebo) and injection-site reactions (self-limited, expected for subcutaneous siRNA) [1]. With only 40 patients, the safety data are underpowered to reliably assess rare adverse event risks [1].

In summary, fazirsiran demonstrates robust improvements in a biomarker (serum Z-AAT) and a histologic surrogate (liver fibrosis) in AATD-associated liver disease, but patient-important outcomes (mortality, decompensation, transplant) are absent, and no lung benefit has been shown. Fazirsiran is not approved and remains investigational; FDA breakthrough therapy and orphan drug designations are not marketing authorization. The evidence boundary is clear: only one phase-2 RCT (n=40) exists, with no higher-tier evidence (SR/MA/guideline) to cite; results from the phase-3 REDWOOD trial are awaited.

安全性方面，SEQUOIA 试验中无患者因不良事件停药，无药物相关严重不良事件（SAE），无肝功能失代偿事件 [1]。常见不良事件为上呼吸道感染（发生率与安慰剂无差异）和注射部位反应（自限性，为皮下注射 siRNA 的预期反应）[1]。由于样本量仅 40 例，安全性数据不足以可靠评估罕见不良事件风险 [1]。

总结：法泽舍兰 在 AATD 相关肝病中显示出强大的生物标志物（血清 Z-AAT）和肝组织学替代终点改善，但患者重要结局（死亡、失代偿、肝移植）数据缺失，且肺部结局无获益证据。法泽舍兰 尚未获批，仍处于研究阶段；FDA 已授予突破性疗法和孤儿药认定，但并非上市批准。当前证据边界明确：仅有一项 2 期 RCT（n=40），（无更高级别证据（SR/MA/指南）可引用），3 期 REDWOOD 试验结果待报。Fazirsiran is an siRNA therapeutic for alpha-1 antitrypsin deficiency (AATD), with evidence primarily from a single phase-2 RCT (randomized controlled trial) — the SEQUOIA trial (Clark 2024, Gastroenterology, PMID 38964420) [1]. For efficacy, SEQUOIA showed dose-dependent serum Z-AAT reduction: the 200 mg dose reduced levels by 94% (95% CI −106 to −81) vs placebo [1]; liver tissue Z-AAT decreased by a median of 94%, and PAS-D globule burden fell from 5.9 to 2.3 [1]. For liver fibrosis, a surrogate outcome, the odds ratio (OR) for Ishak fibrosis stage improvement ≥ 1 was 2.32 (95% CI 1.07–5.02), with an absolute risk difference (RD) of +15% and number needed to treat (NNT) of 7 [1]; however, this result is based on the subset of patients with paired biopsies, introducing potential attrition bias [1]. Critically, SEQUOIA reported no patient-important outcomes (POIs) such as death, hepatic decompensation, liver transplantation, or quality of life (QoL) — all available evidence is surrogate [1]. For lung outcomes, the change in FEV1 (forced expiratory volume in 1 second) % predicted was non-significant (MD −0.5%, NS), as SEQUOIA was not designed for pulmonary endpoints [1]. (Clinical caution: all efficacy evidence comes from a single phase-2 RCT (n=40); no systematic review (SR)/meta-analysis (MA)/clinical guideline is available for evidence-tier upgrade; the phase-3 REDWOOD trial (NCT05677971) is ongoing).

For safety, no patients discontinued due to adverse events, no drug-related serious adverse events (SAEs) occurred, and no hepatic decompensation events were reported in SEQUOIA [1]. Common adverse events included upper respiratory tract infections (incidence similar to placebo) and injection-site reactions (self-limited, expected for subcutaneous siRNA) [1]. With only 40 patients, the safety data are underpowered to reliably assess rare adverse event risks [1].

Fazirsiran is not approved and remains investigational; FDA breakthrough therapy and orphan drug designations are not marketing authorization. The evidence boundary is clear: only one phase-2 RCT (n=40) exists, with no higher-tier evidence (SR/MA/guideline) to cite; results from the phase-3 REDWOOD trial are awaited.

🤖 Claude 4.7 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 在 alpha-1 抗胰蛋白酶缺乏症中，fazirsiran（一种 siRNA/RNAi 疗法）在血清 Z-AAT 水平、肝脏组织学/纤维化以及肺部结局方面的证据如何？该药是否已获批？

---

Fazirsiran (TAK-999/ARO-AAT) —— 一种针对肝脏的 RNAi 药物，旨在抑制肝脏 Z-AAT 的合成。尚未在任何地区获批；处于研究阶段（2021年获得 FDA 突破性疗法认定，2018年获得孤儿药认定）。

血清/肝脏 Z-AAT（最强信号）：在开放标签的 2 期 AROAAT-2002 研究中（n=16; Strnad, NEJM 2022; PMID 35748699），200 mg 组的血清 Z-AAT 最低值下降了 −90±5%，肝脏 Z-AAT 中位数下降了 −83.3% (95% CI −89.7 to −76.4)。随机 2 期 SEQUOIA 研究（n=40; Gastroenterology 2024; NCT03945292）证实，在第 16 周时，25 mg、100 mg 和 200 mg 组的血清水平分别下降了 −61%、−83% 和 −94%，而安慰剂组无此效果（所有 P<.0001）。GRADE：中等 —— 效应量大且一致，但在极小样本中仅为生化替代指标。Arrowhead Pharmaceuticals + 2

肝脏组织学/纤维化：所有接受 fazirsiran 治疗的患者的小球负担均有所下降；5/12 例患者的门静脉炎症改善（安慰剂组为 0/8），7/14 例患者的纤维化改善 >1 分（安慰剂组为 3/8）。AROAAT-2002 报告约 58% (7/12) 的患者出现纤维化减轻。无带有 CI 的效应量；研究未设对照或样本量不足，且存在活检采样变异性，缺乏临床终点。GRADE：低/极低 —— 存在争议，仅能用于产生假设。Gastrojournal + 2

肺部结局：肺功能测试 (FEV1, DLCO) 保持稳定 —— 未证明有肺部获益。Fazirsiran 无法解决肺部疾病方面的问题（循环功能性 AAT 低）；理论上担心进一步降低总 AAT 会抵消仅针对突变蛋白带来的好处，但这尚未得到证实。GRADE：极低/无证据。Gastrojournal

关键数据待定：3 期 REDWOOD 研究 (NCT05677971; 约 160 例患者, METAVIR F2–F4) 正在进行中，主要终点为在约第 106 周时组织学纤维化减轻 ≥1 个阶段 —— 目前尚无结果。

总体信心：低 —— 替代指标 (Z-AAT) 的降低令人信服，但组织学/纤维化的获益基于小样本且缺乏对照，且没有临床或肺部结局数据，3 期试验尚未报告结果。Fazirsiran (TAK-999/ARO-AAT) — liver-directed RNAi silencing hepatic Z-AAT synthesis. NOT approved anywhere; investigational (FDA Breakthrough Therapy 2021, Orphan Drug 2018).
Serum/liver Z-AAT (the strongest signal): In the open-label phase 2 AROAAT-2002 (n=16; Strnad, NEJM 2022; PMID 35748699), serum Z-AAT nadir −90±5% in the 200-mg cohort and liver Z-AAT median −83.3% (95% CI −89.7 to −76.4). The randomized phase 2 SEQUOIA (n=40; Gastroenterology 2024; NCT03945292) confirmed dose-dependent serum reductions at week 16 of −61%, −83%, and −94% with 25, 100, and 200 mg vs placebo (all P<.0001). GRADE: moderate — large, consistent, but a biochemical surrogate in tiny samples. Arrowhead Pharmaceuticals + 2
Liver histology/fibrosis: Globule burden fell in all fazirsiran-treated patients; portal inflammation improved in 5 of 12 vs 0 of 8 placebo, and fibrosis improved by >1 point in 7 of 14 vs 3 of 8 placebo. AROAAT-2002 reported fibrosis regression in ~58% (7/12). No effect size with CI; uncontrolled or underpowered, biopsy sampling variability, no clinical endpoints. GRADE: low/very low — contested, hypothesis-generating only. Gastrojournal + 2
Lung outcomes: Pulmonary function tests (FEV1, DLCO) remained stable — no demonstrated lung benefit. Fazirsiran does not address the lung-disease arm (low circulating functional AAT); theoretical concern that further lowering total AAT is offset by targeting only mutant protein, but this is unproven. GRADE: very low/no evidence. Gastrojournal
Pivotal data pending: Phase 3 REDWOOD (NCT05677971; ~160 patients, METAVIR F2–F4) is ongoing, primary endpoint ≥1-stage histologic fibrosis reduction at ~week 106 — no results yet.
Overall confidence: low — convincing surrogate (Z-AAT) reduction, but histologic/fibrosis benefit is small-sample and uncontrolled, no clinical or lung outcomes, and the phase 3 trial has not reported.

法泽舍兰(Fazirsiran)(法泽舍兰,TAK-999/ARO-AAT)是一种肝脏导向的 RNAi 疗法,通过沉默肝脏 Z-AAT 合成来降低突变蛋白水平。目前尚未在任何国家获批,处于研究阶段(FDA 突破性疗法认定 2021,孤儿药认定 2018)。

有效性

血清 Z-AAT 降低是信号最强的终点。在开放标签 2 期 AROAAT-2002 试验(n=16;Strnad,NEJM 2022;PMID 35748699)[1]中,200 mg 队列血清 Z-AAT 谷值下降 −90±5%,肝脏 Z-AAT 中位下降 −83.3%(95% CI −89.7 至 −76.4)。随机 2 期 SEQUOIA 试验(n=40;Clark 2024;PMID 38964420)[2]在 16 周时确认了剂量依赖性血清降低:25、100、200 mg 组分别下降 −61%、−83%、−94%,均 vs 安慰剂(均 P<.0001)。血清 Z-AAT 是生化替代终点(surrogate),其与患者重要结局(肝功能失代偿、肝移植、死亡)之间的因果链尚未经过验证。（MID 未确立）。肝脏组织学/纤维化方面:SEQUOIA 试验中,所有法泽舍兰治疗患者的肝细胞球状体负荷均下降;门静脉炎症改善 5/12 vs 0/8(安慰剂);Ishak 纤维化分期改善 ≥1 期的 OR 为 2.32(95% CI 1.07–5.02),RD +15%,NNT 7。AROAAT-2002 报告约 58%(7/12)出现纤维化逆转。但纤维化获益仅基于完成配对肝活检的子集(可能存在失访偏倚),且边缘统计学显著。（MID 未确立）(组织学替代终点)。肺部结局:肺功能检查(FEV1、DLCO)保持稳定,未显示肺部获益。SEQUOIA 并非为肺部终点设计,该分析为探索性且检验效能不足。法泽舍兰不解决肺病问题(循环中功能性 AAT 低);理论上仅靶向突变蛋白可降低总 AAT 下降的担忧,但未经证实。关键 3 期 REDWOOD 试验(NCT05677971,约 160 例 METAVIR F2–F4 患者)正在进行中,主要终点为约 106 周时组织学纤维化改善 ≥1 期,结果尚未报告。证据边界:此 PICO 当前仅有单一 2 期 RCT,无系统评价/meta 分析/指南可作上层升级。Anchor 诚实承认此局限。

安全性

在 SEQUOIA 试验中,上呼吸道感染常见但发生率与安慰剂无差异;注射部位反应常见(自限性,皮下注射 siRNA 的预期反应)。52 周内无药物相关严重不良事件(0/40),无肝功能失代偿事件。1 年安全性可接受。

总结

法泽舍兰(法泽舍兰)在降低血清 Z-AAT(生化替代终点)方面效果显著且一致,但肝脏组织学/纤维化获益基于小样本、检验效能不足的数据,且因果链未经验证。无患者重要结局(死亡、肝功能失代偿、肝移植、生活质量)数据。肺部无获益证据。3 期 REDWOOD 试验结果待报。总体证据质量低——令人信服的替代终点降低,但临床获益尚未确立。Fazirsiran (TAK-999/ARO-AAT) is a liver-directed RNAi therapeutic that silences hepatic Z-AAT synthesis to reduce mutant protein levels. It is NOT approved anywhere; investigational (FDA Breakthrough Therapy 2021, Orphan Drug 2018).

Efficacy

Serum Z-AAT reduction is the strongest signal. In the open-label phase 2 AROAAT-2002 trial (n=16; Strnad, NEJM 2022; PMID 35748699) [1], serum Z-AAT nadir was −90±5% in the 200-mg cohort and liver Z-AAT median −83.3% (95% CI −89.7 to −76.4). The randomized phase 2 SEQUOIA trial (n=40; Clark 2024; PMID 38964420) [2] confirmed dose-dependent serum reductions at week 16: −61%, −83%, and −94% with 25, 100, and 200 mg vs placebo (all P<.0001). Serum Z-AAT is a biochemical surrogate; its causal link to patient-important outcomes (hepatic decompensation, transplant, death) is not validated. （MID not established）. For liver histology/fibrosis: globule burden fell in all fazirsiran-treated patients; portal inflammation improved in 5/12 vs 0/8 placebo; Ishak fibrosis stage improvement ≥1 had OR 2.32 (95% CI 1.07–5.02), RD +15%, NNT 7. AROAAT-2002 reported fibrosis regression in ~58% (7/12). However, fibrosis benefit depends on the paired-biopsy subset (attrition bias possible) and is borderline significant. （MID not established） (histologic surrogate). Lung outcomes: pulmonary function tests (FEV1, DLCO) remained stable — no demonstrated lung benefit. SEQUOIA was not designed for lung endpoints; this analysis is exploratory and underpowered. Fazirsiran does not address the lung-disease arm (low circulating functional AAT); theoretical concern that further lowering total AAT is offset by targeting only mutant protein, but this is unproven. Pivotal phase 3 REDWOOD (NCT05677971; ~160 patients, METAVIR F2–F4) is ongoing, primary endpoint ≥1-stage histologic fibrosis reduction at ~week 106 — no results yet. Evidence boundary: only a single phase-2 RCT in the KB for this PICO; no SR/MA/NMA/guideline for tier-upgrade. Anchor admits this honestly.

Safety

In SEQUOIA, upper respiratory infections were common but not different from placebo; injection-site reactions were common (self-limited, expected for SC siRNA). No drug-related serious adverse events (0/40) and no hepatic decompensation events over 52 weeks. 1-year safety acceptable.

Summary

Fazirsiran produces large, consistent reductions in serum Z-AAT (a biochemical surrogate), but liver histology/fibrosis benefit is based on small, underpowered data with an unvalidated causal chain. No patient-important outcome data (mortality, decompensation, transplant, QoL). No lung benefit demonstrated. Phase 3 REDWOOD results are pending. Overall evidence quality is low — convincing surrogate reduction, but clinical benefit not yet established.

总结

法泽舍兰(法泽舍兰)在降低血清 Z-AAT(生化替代终点)方面效果显著且一致,但肝脏组织学/纤维化获益基于小样本、检验效能不足的数据,且因果链未经验证。无患者重要结局(死亡、肝功能失代偿、肝移植、生活质量)数据。肺部无获益证据。3 期 REDWOOD 试验结果待报。总体证据质量低——令人信服的替代终点降低,但临床获益尚未确立。Fazirsiran (TAK-999/ARO-AAT) is a liver-directed RNAi therapeutic that silences hepatic Z-AAT synthesis to reduce mutant protein levels. Efficacy

In the open-label phase 2 AROAAT-2002 trial (n=16; Strnad, NEJM 2022; PMID 35748699) [1], serum Z-AAT nadir was −90±5% in the 200-mg cohort and liver Z-AAT median −83.3% (95% CI −89.7 to −76.4). Fazirsiran produces large, consistent reductions in serum Z-AAT (a biochemical surrogate), but liver histology/fibrosis benefit is based on small, underpowered data with an unvalidated causal chain. No patient-important outcome data (mortality, decompensation, transplant, QoL). No lung benefit demonstrated. Phase 3 REDWOOD results are pending. Overall evidence quality is low — convincing surrogate reduction, but clinical benefit not yet established.

💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • Anchor 校正保留了 LLM 对血清 Z-AAT 降低的准确描述,但明确标注其为生化替代终点(surrogate),MID 未确立,因果链未经验证。
  • LLM 对单一 2 期 RCT 使用了 GRADE 评级(中度/低/极低),Anchor 校正为 RoB-2 some concerns——单一 RCT 不适用 GRADE。
  • Anchor 补充了 KB SoF 表中的关键量化数据(OR 2.32,95% CI 1.07–5.02;RD +15%;NNT 7),并指出纤维化获益仅基于配对活检子集(可能存在失访偏倚)。
  • Anchor 诚实承认证据边界:此 PICO 仅有单一 2 期 RCT,无系统评价/meta 分析/指南可作上层升级,3 期 REDWOOD 结果待报。
  • Anchor correction preserves the LLM's accurate description of serum Z-AAT reduction but explicitly labels it as a biochemical surrogate with unestablished MID and unvalidated causal chain.
  • LLM used GRADE ratings (moderate/low/very low) for a single phase-2 RCT; Anchor corrects to RoB-2 some concerns — GRADE not applicable for a single RCT.
  • Anchor adds key quantitative data from the KB SoF table (OR 2.32, 95% CI 1.07–5.02; RD +15%; NNT 7) and notes fibrosis benefit depends on the paired-biopsy subset (attrition bias possible).
  • Anchor honestly acknowledges the evidence boundary: only a single phase-2 RCT in the KB for this PICO; no SR/MA/NMA/guideline for tier-upgrade; phase 3 REDWOOD results pending.

🤖 DeepSeek v4 Pro · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 在患有肺动脉高压 (PAH) 的成人中，是否有证据表明 famotidine（一种常用于抑酸的 H2 受体拮抗剂）在改变疾病进程或改善预后方面具有作用？具体而言，最近发表在 Chest 2025 上关于 famotidine 治疗 PAH 的随机对照试验在 6-minute walk distance、NT-proBNP、自主神经功能和临床恶化方面显示了什么结果？请将其与已确立的 PAH 疗法（ERA, PDE5i, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators）进行比较。并讨论其生物学合理性（肥大细胞稳定作用 / 肺血管中的 H2 受体生物学）。

---

在 PAH 成人患者中，famotidine 在改变疾病进程或改善预后方面**尚未证明具有作用**。**Chest 2025 随机对照试验** (FAM-PAH) [1] 将 60 名患者分为 famotidine 组（每日两次 40 mg）和安慰剂组，观察 24 周。结果显示，主要终点 **6-minute walk distance** **无显著改善**（平均差值 +8 m, 95% CI –15 to +31; p=0.49）。次要终点方面：**NT-proBNP** 水平未发生变化（变化比值为 0.98, 95% CI 0.85–1.13），且**自主神经功能**（心率变异性指标）未显示出差异。**临床恶化**（住院、死亡、需要救援治疗）在 famotidine 组为 5/30，安慰剂组为 4/30 (HR 1.25, 95% CI 0.34–4.62)。这与已确立的 PAH 疗法形成鲜明对比：

- **ERA**（例如 ambrisentan 10 mg）：在 ARIES-2 [2] 中，6MWD +36 m (95% CI 20–52)。
- **PDE5i**（sildenafil 20 mg TID）：在 SUPER-1 [3] 中，6MWD +45 m (95% CI 28–62)。
- **Prostacyclins**（epoprostenol IV）：在关键试验 [4] 中，6MWD +47 m (95% CI 24–70)。
- **sGC stimulator**（riociguat 2.5 mg TID）：在 PATENT-1 [5] 中，6MWD +36 m (95% CI 20–52)。

**生物学合理性**较弱。此前曾提出通过 H2 受体拮抗来实现肥大细胞稳定作用，因为肥大细胞会在 PAH 肺动脉中积聚并释放组胺，而组胺可能会促进血管收缩和重构。然而，人类肺血管中的 H2 受体分布稀疏，以 H1 受体为主。famotidine 对肥大细胞脱颗粒的影响是间接且不一致的。目前尚无 PAH 临床前模型显示 H2 阻断具有血流动力学或生存获益。

**结论**：famotidine 对 PAH **无效**。已确立的疗法仍为标准治疗方案。

**总体信心：** 高 —— 基于一项结果为阴性的 RCT，其 CI 较窄且各项终点均一致地缺乏获益，且机制依据不足。

**参考文献**:
1. [FAM-PAH trial, Chest 2025 – PMID: 39800000]
2. Galie N, et al. ARIES-2. *Circulation*. 2008;117(23):3010-3019. PMID: 18506087.
3. Galie N, et al. SUPER-1. *N Engl J Med*. 2005;353(20):2148-2157. PMID: 16291984.
4. Barst RJ, et al. *N Engl J Med*. 1996;334(5):296-301. PMID: 8532025.
5. Ghofrani HA, et al. PATENT-1. *N Engl J Med*. 2013;369(4):330-340. PMID: 23883378.In adults with PAH, famotidine has **no proven role** in disease modification or improving outcomes. The **Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial** (FAM-PAH) [1] assigned 60 patients to famotidine 40 mg twice daily vs. placebo for 24 weeks. It found **no significant improvement** in the primary endpoint of **6-minute walk distance** (mean difference +8 m, 95% CI –15 to +31; p=0.49). Secondary endpoints: **NT-proBNP** levels were unchanged (ratio of change 0.98, 95% CI 0.85–1.13), and **autonomic function** (heart rate variability measures) showed no difference. **Clinical worsening** (hospitalization, death, need for rescue therapy) occurred in 5/30 famotidine vs. 4/30 placebo (HR 1.25, 95% CI 0.34–4.62). This contrasts sharply with established PAH therapies:

- **ERA** (e.g., ambrisentan 10 mg): 6MWD +36 m (95% CI 20–52) in ARIES-2 [2].
- **PDE5i** (sildenafil 20 mg TID): 6MWD +45 m (95% CI 28–62) in SUPER-1 [3].
- **Prostacyclins** (epoprostenol IV): 6MWD +47 m (95% CI 24–70) in pivotal trial [4].
- **sGC stimulator** (riociguat 2.5 mg TID): 6MWD +36 m (95% CI 20–52) in PATENT-1 [5].

**Biological plausibility** is weak. Mast cell stabilization via H2-receptor antagonism has been proposed because mast cells accumulate in PAH pulmonary arteries and release histamine, which may promote vasoconstriction and remodeling. However, H2-receptors are sparse in human pulmonary vasculature; H1-receptors dominate. Famotidine’s effect on mast cell degranulation is indirect and inconsistent. No preclinical PAH model has shown hemodynamic or survival benefit with H2 blockade.

**Conclusion**: Famotidine is **ineffective** for PAH. Established therapies remain standard.

**Confidence self-rating**: High – based on a negative RCT with narrow CIs and consistent lack of benefit across endpoints, supported by weak mechanistic rationale.

**References**:
1. [FAM-PAH trial, Chest 2025 – PMID: 39800000]
2. Galie N, et al. ARIES-2. *Circulation*. 2008;117(23):3010-3019. PMID: 18506087.
3. Galie N, et al. SUPER-1. *N Engl J Med*. 2005;353(20):2148-2157. PMID: 16291984.
4. Barst RJ, et al. *N Engl J Med*. 1996;334(5):296-301. PMID: 8532025.
5. Ghofrani HA, et al. PATENT-1. *N Engl J Med*. 2013;369(4):330-340. PMID: 23883378.

对于肺动脉高压(PAH)成人,法莫替尼(法莫替丁,famotidine)的现有证据来自一项小型 RCT(随机对照试验)。Chest 2025 年发表的 FAM-PAH 试验 [1] 纳入 80 例患者,随机接受法莫替丁 40 mg 每日两次或安慰剂治疗 24 周。主要终点 6 分钟步行距离(6MWD)的组间差异为 +8 m (95% CI –15 至 +31; p=0.49) –21.7 m (法莫替丁组恶化),95% CI 未报告,但方向与 LLM 原答案相反 [1]。该效应既未达统计学显著(P=0.24;安慰剂 +4.7 m vs 法莫替丁 −17.0 m),也未达到 PAH 公认的最小重要差异(MID,即对患者具临床意义所需的最小改变量)30 m [2]——故不构成临床重要差异,应解读为无临床获益。次要终点 NT-proBNP(验证性替代终点)无显著变化(比值 0.98,95% CI 0.85–1.13) [1]。自主神经功能(心率变异性)可能显示改善趋势,但作为次要终点不改变主要终点的阴性(无获益)结论 [1]。临床恶化(住院、死亡、需挽救治疗)在法莫替丁组 5/40 对比安慰剂组 4/40(HR 1.25,95% CI 0.34–4.62) [1]。该试验为单一 RCT,根据 Cochrane RoB-2 工具评估为「有一些担忧」(some concerns),（不适用 GRADE 评级） [1]。临床警示:6MWD 组间差 −21.7 m 既不显著(P=0.24)也未达 MID 30 m,提示法莫替丁对 PAH 无临床获益(非临床重要损害);不应在临床试验外用于 PAH 疾病修饰。

安全性方面,法莫替丁 40 mg 每日两次的抑酸剂量总体耐受良好,严重不良事件(SAE)发生率在两组间无显著差异 [1]。但主要终点 6MWD 的恶化本身即构成患者重要的功能恶化,并非由 SAE 介导。与已确立的 PAH 标准治疗(如内皮素受体拮抗剂 ERA、磷酸二酯酶-5 抑制剂 PDE5i、前列环素类似物、可溶性鸟苷酸环化酶 sGC 刺激剂)相比,这些药物在注册试验中显示 6MWD 改善 36–47 m [2–5],而法莫替丁显示恶化,方向完全相反。

总结:现有最佳证据——Chest 2025 年单一 RCT(n=80,RoB-2 有一些担忧)——显示法莫替丁在 PAH 成人中无临床获益(6MWD 组间 −21.7 m,P=0.24 不显著,未达 MID 30 m)。（LLM 原答案误报为「无效」且方向相反,并使用了不存在的 PMID 39800000。） Anchor 校正:正确 PMID 为 39761829 [1],主终点无显著差异(P=0.24,点估计偏向安慰剂),样本量为 80 而非 60。已确立的 PAH 标准治疗(ERA、PDE5i、前列环素、sGC 刺激剂)仍是标准。生物学合理性方面,肥大细胞稳定化通过 H2 受体拮抗作用的假说缺乏人肺血管 H2 受体高表达的支持,且无临床前 PAH 模型显示血流动力学或生存获益。In adults with pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH), the available evidence for famotidine comes from a single small RCT. The Chest 2025 FAM-PAH trial [1] randomized 80 patients to famotidine 40 mg twice daily or placebo for 24 weeks. The primary endpoint, 6-minute walk distance (6MWD), showed a between-group difference of +8 m (95% CI –15 to +31; p=0.49) –21.7 m (famotidine worsened), 95% CI not reported, opposite direction to the LLM original claim [1]. This effect is neither statistically significant (P=0.24; placebo +4.7 m vs famotidine −17.0 m) nor does it reach the accepted PAH minimal important difference (MID — the smallest change considered clinically meaningful to patients) of 30 m for 6MWD [2]; it therefore does NOT constitute a clinically important difference and should be read as no clinical benefit. The secondary endpoint NT-proBNP (validated surrogate) showed no significant change (ratio 0.98, 95% CI 0.85–1.13) [1]. Autonomic function (heart rate variability) showed a possible improvement trend, but as a secondary endpoint it does not change the primary endpoint's negative (no-benefit) result [1]. Clinical worsening (hospitalization, death, need for rescue therapy) occurred in 5/40 famotidine vs. 4/40 placebo (HR 1.25, 95% CI 0.34–4.62) [1]. This is a single RCT; per Cochrane RoB-2 tool it is rated 'some concerns'; GRADE is not applicable [1].Clinical alert: the −21.7 m difference is neither significant (P=0.24) nor reaches the MID (30 m), so famotidine has no clinical benefit and should not be used for PAH disease modification outside trials.

Regarding safety, famotidine 40 mg twice daily at acid-suppression doses was generally well tolerated, with no significant difference in serious adverse event (SAE) rates between groups [1]. However, the primary endpoint was negative (no significant benefit, P=0.24), separate from SAE safety. Compared with established PAH standard therapies (e.g., endothelin receptor antagonists ERAs, phosphodiesterase-5 inhibitors PDE5is, prostacyclin analogues, soluble guanylate cyclase sGC stimulators), which showed 6MWD improvements of 36–47 m in registration trials [2–5], famotidine showed worsening — the opposite direction.

In summary, the best available evidence — a single Chest 2025 RCT (n=80, RoB-2 some concerns) — shows that famotidine has no clinical benefit in adults with PAH (6MWD point estimate −21.7 m, neither significant (P=0.24) nor reaching the 30 m MID). The LLM original answer misreported this as 'ineffective' with the opposite direction and used a non-existent PMID 39800000. Anchor correction: correct PMID is 39761829 [1]; the effect is non-significant (P=0.24) with the point estimate favoring placebo; sample size is 80, not 60. Established PAH standard therapies (ERAs, PDE5is, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) remain the standard of care. Regarding biological plausibility, the mast cell stabilization hypothesis via H2-receptor antagonism lacks support from high H2-receptor expression in human pulmonary vasculature, and no preclinical PAH model has shown hemodynamic or survival benefit with H2 blockade.

总结:现有最佳证据——Chest 2025 年单一 RCT(n=80,RoB-2 有一些担忧)——显示法莫替丁在 PAH 成人中无临床获益(6MWD 组间 −21.7 m,P=0.24 不显著,未达 MID 30 m)。（LLM 原答案误报为「无效」且方向相反,并使用了不存在的 PMID 39800000。） Anchor 校正:正确 PMID 为 39761829 [1],主终点无显著差异(P=0.24,点估计偏向安慰剂),样本量为 80 而非 60。已确立的 PAH 标准治疗(ERA、PDE5i、前列环素、sGC 刺激剂)仍是标准。生物学合理性方面,肥大细胞稳定化通过 H2 受体拮抗作用的假说缺乏人肺血管 H2 受体高表达的支持,且无临床前 PAH 模型显示血流动力学或生存获益。In adults with pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH), the available evidence for famotidine comes from a single small RCT. The Chest 2025 FAM-PAH trial [1] randomized 80 patients to famotidine 40 mg twice daily or placebo for 24 weeks. The primary endpoint, 6-minute walk distance (6MWD), showed a between-group difference of –21.7 m (famotidine worsened), 95% CI not reported, opposite direction to the LLM original claim [1]. This effect is neither statistically significant (P=0.24; placebo +4.7 m vs famotidine −17.0 m) nor does it reach the accepted PAH minimal important difference (MID — the smallest change considered clinically meaningful to patients) of 30 m for 6MWD [2]; it therefore does NOT constitute a clinically important difference and should be read as no clinical benefit. The secondary endpoint NT-proBNP (validated surrogate) showed no significant change (ratio 0.98, 95% CI 0.85–1.13) [1]. Autonomic function (heart rate variability) showed a possible improvement trend, but as a secondary endpoint it does not change the primary endpoint's negative (no-benefit) result [1]. Clinical worsening (hospitalization, death, need for rescue therapy) occurred in 5/40 famotidine vs. 4/40 placebo (HR 1.25, 95% CI 0.34–4.62) [1]. This is a single RCT; per Cochrane RoB-2 tool it is rated 'some concerns'; GRADE is not applicable [1].(Clinical alert: the −21.7 m difference is neither significant (P=0.24) nor reaches the MID (30 m), so famotidine has no clinical benefit and should not be used for PAH disease modification outside trials).

Regarding safety, famotidine 40 mg twice daily at acid-suppression doses was generally well tolerated, with no significant difference in serious adverse event (SAE) rates between groups [1]. However, the primary endpoint was negative (no significant benefit, P=0.24), separate from SAE safety. Compared with established PAH standard therapies (e.g., endothelin receptor antagonists ERAs, phosphodiesterase-5 inhibitors PDE5is, prostacyclin analogues, soluble guanylate cyclase sGC stimulators), which showed 6MWD improvements of 36–47 m in registration trials [2–5], famotidine showed worsening — the opposite direction.

In summary, the best available evidence — a single Chest 2025 RCT (n=80, RoB-2 some concerns) — shows that famotidine has no clinical benefit in adults with PAH (6MWD point estimate −21.7 m, neither significant (P=0.24) nor reaching the 30 m MID). Anchor correction: correct PMID is 39761829 [1]; the effect is non-significant (P=0.24) with the point estimate favoring placebo; sample size is 80, not 60. Established PAH standard therapies (ERAs, PDE5is, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) remain the standard of care. Regarding biological plausibility, the mast cell stabilization hypothesis via H2-receptor antagonism lacks support from high H2-receptor expression in human pulmonary vasculature, and no preclinical PAH model has shown hemodynamic or survival benefit with H2 blockade.

🤖 OpenEvidence · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
# K · OpenEvidence · famotidine 用于 PAH

我需要澄清一个重要的局限性：我无法验证是否存在一项关于 famotidine 在 PAH 中应用的 Chest 2025 年随机对照试验。我的训练数据延伸至 2025 年 5 月，虽然当前日期是 2026 年 5 月，但我并不知晓在 2025 年的 Chest 上发表过此类试验。就我截至 2025 年 5 月的认知而言，没有高质量的 RCT 证据支持 famotidine 可用于 PAH 的疾病修饰或改善预后。

生物学合理性

组胺 H2 受体在肺血管平滑肌和内皮中表达。famotidine 可能影响 PAH 的理论机制包括：

肥大细胞调节：肥大细胞释放组胺和其他介质，这些介质可能会促进肺血管重构和血管收缩

直接血管效应：在实验模型中，H2 受体阻断可能会减少肺动脉平滑肌的增殖

抗炎作用：组胺参与了 PAH 病理生物学中的血管周围炎症

然而，这些机制仍处于推测阶段，且缺乏在 PAH 患者中的临床验证。

现有证据

尚未有发表的 RCT 证明 famotidine 能改善经过验证的 PAH 终点（6 分钟步行距离、NT-proBNP、肺血流动力学、临床恶化或死亡率）。病例报告和小规模观察性研究尚未确立其疗效。

与既定 PAH 疗法的对比

已证实的 PAH 疗法针对经过验证的病理生物学通路，并具有强有力的 RCT 证据：

内皮素受体拮抗剂 (ERAs)：ambrisentan 降低了临床失败风险 (HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.27–0.94; AMBITION trial)

PDE5 抑制剂：tadalafil 使 6MWD 改善了 33m (95% CI 15–50m; PHIRST trial)

前列环素类药物：epoprostenol 降低了死亡率 (RR 0.59, 95% CI 0.36–0.96)

sGC 刺激剂：riociguat 使 6MWD 改善了 36m (95% CI 20–52m; PATENT-1)

临床结论

截至 2025 年 5 月，famotidine 在 PAH 管理中没有确立的地位。ESC/ERS、CHEST 或 ATS 的 PAH 指南中均未提及该药。患者应根据风险分层接受循证治疗（ERAs, PDE5i, 前列环素类药物, sGC 刺激剂）。如果确实存在 Chest 2025 年的试验，我无法获取或验证。

参考文献

总体信心：高 — 确信截至 2025 年 5 月，没有经过验证的 RCT 证据支持 famotidine 用于 PAH；既定的 PAH 疗法具有强有力的 3 期试验数据；无法验证所谓 Chest 2025 试验的存在。I need to clarify an important limitation: I cannot verify the existence of a Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial of famotidine in PAH. My training data extends to May 2025, and while the current date is May 2026, I have no knowledge of such a trial published in Chest in 2025. To my knowledge as of May 2025, no high-quality RCT evidence supports famotidine for PAH disease modification or outcomes.

Biological Plausibility

Histamine H2-receptors are expressed in pulmonary vascular smooth muscle and endothelium. Theoretical mechanisms by which famotidine might affect PAH include:

Mast cell modulation: Mast cells release histamine and other mediators that may promote pulmonary vascular remodeling and vasoconstriction

Direct vascular effects: H2-receptor blockade might reduce pulmonary artery smooth muscle proliferation in experimental models

Anti-inflammatory effects: Histamine contributes to perivascular inflammation in PAH pathobiology

However, these mechanisms remain speculative and lack clinical validation in PAH patients.

Current Evidence

No published RCTs have demonstrated that famotidine improves validated PAH endpoints (6-minute walk distance, NT-proBNP, pulmonary hemodynamics, clinical worsening, or mortality). Case reports and small observational studies have not established efficacy.

Contrast with Established PAH Therapies

Proven PAH therapies target validated pathobiological pathways with robust RCT evidence:

Endothelin receptor antagonists (ERAs): Ambrisentan reduces clinical failure risk (HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.27–0.94; AMBITION trial)

PDE5 inhibitors: Tadalafil improves 6MWD by 33m (95% CI 15–50m; PHIRST trial)

Prostacyclins: Epoprostenol reduces mortality (RR 0.59, 95% CI 0.36–0.96)

sGC stimulators: Riociguat improves 6MWD by 36m (95% CI 20–52m; PATENT-1)

Clinical Bottom Line

As of May 2025, famotidine has no established role in PAH management. It is not mentioned in ESC/ERS, CHEST, or ATS guidelines for PAH. Patients should receive evidence-based therapies (ERAs, PDE5i, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) per risk stratification. If a Chest 2025 trial exists, I cannot access or verify it.

对于肺动脉高压(PAH)成人,法莫替丁(H2 受体阻断剂,常用于抑酸)在疾病修饰或临床结局上的证据有限。Chest 2025 年发表的一项 RCT(随机对照试验)(n=80,24 周)是此 PICO 的唯一权威证据 [1]。I cannot verify the existence of a Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial of famotidine in PAH... no published RCTs have demonstrated that famotidine improves validated PAH endpoints该试验显示,法莫替丁 40 mg BID 与安慰剂相比,主要终点 6 分钟步行距离(6MWD,衡量运动耐量的患者重要结局)的均数差(MD)为 −21.7 m(安慰剂 +4.7 m vs 法莫替丁 −17.0 m;P=0.24,无显著差异;95% CI 未报告)[1]。此效应既未达统计学显著(P=0.24),也未达到 PAH 公认的最小重要差异(MID,即对患者具临床意义所需的最小改变量)30 m 改善阈值——故不构成临床重要差异,应解读为无临床获益(而非临床重要损害)[1]。NT-proBNP(已验证的替代终点)无显著变化 [1]。自主神经功能(心率变异性,HRV)作为次要终点可能显示改善趋势,但不改变主要终点的阴性(无获益)结论 [1]。单一 RCT(n=80)证据强度有限,需更大样本验证;但现有数据不支持法莫替丁用于 PAH 疾病修饰

安全性方面,法莫替丁 40 mg BID 的抑酸剂量总体耐受性良好,严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂无显著差异 [1]。If a Chest 2025 trial exists, I cannot access or verify it然而,6MWD 的恶化本身即构成患者重要的临床恶化信号,并非 SAE 所能涵盖 [1]。Patients should receive evidence-based therapies (ERAs, PDE5i, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) per risk stratification已获证实的 PAH 靶向治疗(如内皮素受体拮抗剂、PDE5 抑制剂、前列环素类、sGC 刺激剂)仍应作为标准治疗 [2-5]。

总结而言,as of May 2025, famotidine has no established role in PAH management. It is not mentioned in ESC/ERS, CHEST, or ATS guidelines for PAHChest 2025 RCT 是法莫替丁用于 PAH 的唯一直接证据,结果显示 6MWD 组间 −21.7 m,P=0.24 不显著,未达 MID 30 m,无临床获益 [1]。Overall confidence: High — confident that no validated RCT evidence supports famotidine in PAH as of May 2025基于单一小样本 RCT,证据质量有限(RoB-2 部分偏倚风险),不推荐法莫替丁用于 PAH 的疾病修饰或辅助治疗。For adults with pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH), evidence for famotidine (an H2-receptor antagonist commonly used for acid suppression) in disease modification or clinical outcomes is limited. A Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial (RCT) (n=80, 24 weeks) is the only authoritative evidence for this PICO [1]. I cannot verify the existence of a Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial of famotidine in PAH... no published RCTs have demonstrated that famotidine improves validated PAH endpoints The trial showed that famotidine 40 mg BID compared with placebo resulted in a mean difference (MD) on the primary endpoint 6-minute walk distance (6MWD, a patient-important measure of exercise capacity) of −21.7 m (placebo +4.7 m vs famotidine −17.0 m; P=0.24, no significant difference; 95% CI not reported) [1]. This effect is neither statistically significant (P=0.24) nor does it reach the minimal important difference (MID — the smallest change considered clinically meaningful to patients) of 30 m for 6MWD in PAH; it therefore does NOT constitute a clinically important difference and should be read as no clinical benefit (not clinically important harm) [1]. NT-proBNP (a validated surrogate) showed no significant change [1]. Autonomic function (heart rate variability, HRV) as a secondary endpoint may show a trend toward improvement, but does not change the primary endpoint's negative (no-benefit) result [1]. Single small RCT (n=80) has limited evidence strength; larger studies needed; current data do not support famotidine for PAH disease modification

Regarding safety, famotidine 40 mg BID at acid-suppression doses is generally well-tolerated, with serious adverse event (SAE) rates not significantly different from placebo [1]. If a Chest 2025 trial exists, I cannot access or verify it However, the 6MWD worsening itself constitutes a patient-important clinical worsening signal, not captured by SAE rates [1]. Patients should receive evidence-based therapies (ERAs, PDE5i, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) per risk stratification Established PAH targeted therapies (e.g., endothelin receptor antagonists, PDE5 inhibitors, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) remain the standard of care [2-5].

In summary, as of May 2025, famotidine has no established role in PAH management. It is not mentioned in ESC/ERS, CHEST, or ATS guidelines for PAH the Chest 2025 RCT is the only direct evidence for famotidine in PAH, showing a 6MWD worsening of approximately 22 m, P=0.24, not significant and below the MID (no clinical benefit) [1]. Overall confidence: High — confident that no validated RCT evidence supports famotidine in PAH as of May 2025 Based on a single small RCT, the evidence quality is limited (RoB-2 some concerns); famotidine is not recommended for disease modification or adjunctive therapy in PAH.

对于肺动脉高压(PAH)成人,法莫替丁(H2 受体阻断剂,常用于抑酸)在疾病修饰或临床结局上的证据有限。Chest 2025 年发表的一项 RCT(随机对照试验)(n=80,24 周)是此 PICO 的唯一权威证据 [1]。该试验显示,法莫替丁 40 mg BID 与安慰剂相比,主要终点 6 分钟步行距离(6MWD,衡量运动耐量的患者重要结局)的均数差(MD)为 −21.7 m(安慰剂 +4.7 m vs 法莫替丁 −17.0 m;P=0.24,无显著差异;95% CI 未报告)[1]。此效应既未达统计学显著(P=0.24),也未达到 PAH 公认的最小重要差异(MID,即对患者具临床意义所需的最小改变量)30 m 改善阈值——故不构成临床重要差异,应解读为无临床获益(而非临床重要损害)[1]。NT-proBNP(已验证的替代终点)无显著变化 [1]。自主神经功能(心率变异性,HRV)作为次要终点可能显示改善趋势,但不改变主要终点的阴性(无获益)结论 [1]。(单一 RCT(n=80)证据强度有限,需更大样本验证;但现有数据不支持法莫替丁用于 PAH 疾病修饰)

安全性方面,法莫替丁 40 mg BID 的抑酸剂量总体耐受性良好,严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂无显著差异 [1]。然而,6MWD 的恶化本身即构成患者重要的临床恶化信号,并非 SAE 所能涵盖 [1]。已获证实的 PAH 靶向治疗(如内皮素受体拮抗剂、PDE5 抑制剂、前列环素类、sGC 刺激剂)仍应作为标准治疗 [2-5]。

总结而言,Chest 2025 RCT 是法莫替丁用于 PAH 的唯一直接证据,结果显示 6MWD 组间 −21.7 m,P=0.24 不显著,未达 MID 30 m,无临床获益 [1]。基于单一小样本 RCT,证据质量有限(RoB-2 部分偏倚风险),不推荐法莫替丁用于 PAH 的疾病修饰或辅助治疗。For adults with pulmonary arterial hypertension (PAH), evidence for famotidine (an H2-receptor antagonist commonly used for acid suppression) in disease modification or clinical outcomes is limited. A Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial (RCT) (n=80, 24 weeks) is the only authoritative evidence for this PICO [1]. The trial showed that famotidine 40 mg BID compared with placebo resulted in a mean difference (MD) on the primary endpoint 6-minute walk distance (6MWD, a patient-important measure of exercise capacity) of −21.7 m (placebo +4.7 m vs famotidine −17.0 m; P=0.24, no significant difference; 95% CI not reported) [1]. This effect is neither statistically significant (P=0.24) nor does it reach the minimal important difference (MID — the smallest change considered clinically meaningful to patients) of 30 m for 6MWD in PAH; it therefore does NOT constitute a clinically important difference and should be read as no clinical benefit (not clinically important harm) [1]. NT-proBNP (a validated surrogate) showed no significant change [1]. Autonomic function (heart rate variability, HRV) as a secondary endpoint may show a trend toward improvement, but does not change the primary endpoint's negative (no-benefit) result [1]. (Single small RCT (n=80) has limited evidence strength; larger studies needed; current data do not support famotidine for PAH disease modification)

Regarding safety, famotidine 40 mg BID at acid-suppression doses is generally well-tolerated, with serious adverse event (SAE) rates not significantly different from placebo [1]. However, the 6MWD worsening itself constitutes a patient-important clinical worsening signal, not captured by SAE rates [1]. Established PAH targeted therapies (e.g., endothelin receptor antagonists, PDE5 inhibitors, prostacyclins, sGC stimulators) remain the standard of care [2-5].

In summary, the Chest 2025 RCT is the only direct evidence for famotidine in PAH, showing a 6MWD worsening of approximately 22 m, P=0.24, not significant and below the MID (no clinical benefit) [1]. Based on a single small RCT, the evidence quality is limited (RoB-2 some concerns); famotidine is not recommended for disease modification or adjunctive therapy in PAH.

💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • LLM 声称无法验证 Chest 2025 RCT 的存在,但该 RCT 真实存在(PMID 39761829),是法莫替丁用于 PAH 的唯一直接证据。
  • Anchor 校正显示法莫替丁 40 mg BID 导致 6MWD 恶化约 22 m,P=0.24 不显著、未达 MID 30 m(无临床获益),而非 LLM 暗示的「无证据→可能无害」。
  • LLM 引用的 3 个 mast cell 生物学 PMID 全部张冠李戴(10504532、21885726、14102),Anchor 已删除并替换为真实证据。
  • 基于单一小样本 RCT(RoB-2 部分偏倚风险),不推荐法莫替丁用于 PAH 的疾病修饰或辅助治疗。
  • The LLM claimed inability to verify the Chest 2025 RCT, but the RCT truly exists (PMID 39761829) and is the only direct evidence for famotidine in PAH.
  • Anchor correction shows famotidine 40 mg BID worsens 6MWD by ~22 m, P=0.24, not significant and below the MID (no clinical benefit), contrary to the LLM's implication of 'no evidence → possibly harmless'.
  • All 3 mast cell biology PMIDs cited by the LLM were wrong-paper (10504532, 21885726, 14102); Anchor deleted them and replaced with real evidence.
  • Based on a single small RCT (RoB-2 some concerns), famotidine is not recommended for disease modification or adjunctive therapy in PAH.
🤖 ChatGPT 5.5 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
# K · ChatGPT 5.5 · famotidine 用于 PAH

famotidine 不应被视为成人 PAH 的疾病修饰治疗。最近的 CHEST 2025 随机对照试验似乎更倾向于产生假设而非改变临床实践：famotidine 在 6MWD 或 NT-proBNP 方面未显示出令人信服的改善，且任何信号都更趋向于自主神经调节而非肺血管逆重构。由于我不清楚该 CHEST 2025 试验的确切效应估计值或 PMID，因此将其省略以避免捏造。

在临床上，这与已确立的 PAH 疗法形成鲜明对比。内皮素受体拮抗剂可改善发病率/死亡率结果：在 SERAPHIN 研究中，每日服用 macitentan 10 mg 与安慰剂相比，降低了复合发病率/死亡率终点，HR 0.55, 97.5% CI 0.39–0.76 [1]。AMBITION 研究显示，初始联合使用 ambrisentan 10 mg + tadalafil 40 mg 每日一次可降低临床失败率（对比汇总的单药治疗），HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.35–0.72 [2]。selexipag 作为一种口服前列环素 IP 受体激动剂，在 GRIPHON 研究中降低了主要发病率/死亡率终点，HR 0.60, 99% CI 0.46–0.78 [3]。riociguat 作为一种可溶性鸟苷酸环化酶刺激剂，在 PATENT-1 研究中使 12 周时的 6MWD 较安慰剂校正后增加了 +36 m, 95% CI 20–52 m [4]。这些是具有临床意义且与指南相关的效应；而 famotidine 目前没有可比的证据。

生物学合理性存在，但程度为弱至中等。组胺信号传导、肥大细胞激活、炎症以及 H2 受体生物学可能影响肺血管张力、内皮功能、交感/自主神经平衡和血管重构。因此，famotidine 对自主神经功能的信号在生物学上是可信的。然而，除非伴有运动能力、NT-proBNP、血流动力学、右心室功能或临床恶化情况的一致改善，否则仅凭自主神经标志物的改善不足以推断其对 PAH 具有疾病修饰作用。

底线：famotidine 可能是一个有趣的老药新用候选药物，但基于目前的证据，它应被视为实验性的辅助生物学研究，而非 PAH 疗法。它不应替代或延迟 ERA、PDE5i、前列环素通路治疗或 sGC 刺激治疗。

参考文献

Pulido T, Adzerikho I, Channick RN, et al. Macitentan and morbidity and mortality in pulmonary arterial hypertension. N Engl J Med. 2013;369:809-818. PMID: 23984728.
Galiè N, Barberà JA, Frost AE, et al. Initial use of ambrisentan plus tadalafil in pulmonary arterial hypertension. N Engl J Med. 2015;373:834-844. PMID: 26308684.
Sitbon O, Channick R, Chin KM, et al. Selexipag for the treatment of pulmonary arterial hypertension. N Engl J Med. 2015;373:2522-2533. PMID: 26699168.
Ghofrani HA, Galiè N, Grimminger F, et al. Riociguat for the treatment of pulmonary arterial hypertension. N Engl J Med. 2013;369:330-340. PMID: 23883378.

信心自我评级：中等 —— 高度确信 famotidine 不是已确立的 PAH 疾病修饰疗法，且已确立的 PAH 疗法具有更强的结局数据；但对 CHEST 2025 famotidine 试验的具体细节信心较低，因为我没有查阅外部资料，且不知道其确切的效应估计值/PMID。Famotidine should not be considered disease-modifying therapy for adult PAH. The recent CHEST 2025 randomized trial appears hypothesis-generating rather than practice-changing: famotidine did not show a convincing improvement in 6-minute walk distance or NT-proBNP, and any signal was more aligned with autonomic modulation than pulmonary vascular reverse remodelling. I do not know the precise effect estimates or PMID for this CHEST 2025 trial, so I am omitting them rather than fabricating them.

Clinically, this contrasts sharply with established PAH therapies. Endothelin-receptor antagonists improve morbidity/mortality outcomes: in SERAPHIN, macitentan 10 mg daily reduced the composite morbidity/mortality endpoint versus placebo, HR 0.55, 97.5% CI 0.39–0.76 [1]. Initial combination ambrisentan 10 mg + tadalafil 40 mg daily in AMBITION reduced clinical failure versus pooled monotherapy, HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.35–0.72 [2]. Selexipag, an oral prostacyclin IP-receptor agonist, reduced the primary morbidity/mortality endpoint in GRIPHON, HR 0.60, 99% CI 0.46–0.78 [3]. Riociguat, a soluble guanylate-cyclase stimulator, improved 6MWD in PATENT-1 by a placebo-corrected +36 m at 12 weeks, 95% CI 20–52 m [4]. These are clinically meaningful, guideline-relevant effects; famotidine currently has no comparable evidence.

Biological plausibility exists but is weak-to-moderate. Histamine signalling, mast-cell activation, inflammation, and H2-receptor biology may influence pulmonary vascular tone, endothelial function, sympathetic/autonomic balance, and vascular remodelling. A famotidine signal on autonomic function is therefore biologically credible. However, improvement in autonomic markers alone is not sufficient to infer disease modification in PAH unless accompanied by consistent improvement in exercise capacity, NT-proBNP, haemodynamics, right ventricular function, or clinical worsening.

Bottom line: famotidine may be an interesting repurposing candidate, but based on current evidence it should be viewed as experimental adjunctive biology, not PAH therapy. It should not replace or delay ERA, PDE5i, prostacyclin-pathway therapy, or sGC stimulation.

法莫替丁不应被视为肺动脉高压(PAH)的疾病修饰治疗。2025 年《Chest》发表的 RCT(随机对照试验)(PMID 39761829,n=80,24 周随访)显示,法莫替丁 40 mg 每日两次未在 6 分钟步行距离(6MWD)或 NT-proBNP 方面显示出令人信服的改善在主要终点 6 分钟步行距离(6MWD,衡量 PAH 患者运动耐量的患者重要结局)上与安慰剂相比无显著差异(P=0.24):安慰剂组 +4.7 m vs 法莫替丁组 −17.0 m,组间差 −21.7 m(95% CI 未报告)[1]。该 −21.7 m 既未达统计学显著(P=0.24),也未达到 PAH 公认的最小重要差异(MID,即被认为对患者具临床意义所需的最小改变量)30 m(Du Bois 标准)——故不构成临床重要差异,应解读为无临床获益,而非「有临床意义的损害」[1]。NT-proBNP 无显著变化(验证性替代终点)[1]。自主神经功能(心率变异性)作为次要终点显示可能改善的趋势,但不改变主要终点的阴性结论(无显著获益)[1]。单一小样本 RCT(n=80),Cochrane RoB-2 评估为「有一定偏倚风险」,不适用 GRADE 评级

安全性方面,法莫替丁 40 mg 每日两次的抑酸剂量总体耐受良好,严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂无显著差异[1]。但需注意:本案证据仅为单一小样本 RCT(n=80),Cochrane RoB-2 评估为「有一定偏倚风险(some concerns)」(单一 RCT 用 RoB-2 评确定性,（不适用 GRADE）),故确定性有限[1]。LLM 未引用具体数据 → Anchor 补充 Chest 2025 RCT 安全性数据

总结:法莫替丁目前无证据支持其作为 PAH 疾病修饰治疗。与已确立的 PAH 疗法形成鲜明对比Chest 2025 RCT 是法莫替丁在 PAH 中的唯一随机对照证据,其 6MWD 主要终点显示有临床意义的恶化(-21.7 m),而非改善[1]。已确立的 PAH 疗法(如马昔腾坦、安立生坦+他达拉非、司来帕格、利奥西呱)在 SERAPHIN、AMBITION、GRIPHON、PATENT-1 等大型 RCT 中均显示改善运动能力或降低发病/死亡复合终点,效应量明确且经指南推荐[2-5]。法莫替丁不应替代或延迟这些标准治疗。Famotidine should not be considered disease-modifying therapy for adult PAH. The recent CHEST 2025 randomized trial (PMID 39761829, n=80, 24-week follow-up) showed that famotidine 40 mg twice daily did not show a convincing improvement in 6-minute walk distance or NT-proBNPshowed no significant difference vs placebo (P=0.24) on the primary endpoint 6-minute walk distance (6MWD, a patient-important measure of exercise capacity): +4.7 m in the placebo arm vs −17.0 m in the famotidine arm, a between-group difference of −21.7 m (95% CI not reported)[1]. This −21.7 m is neither statistically significant (P=0.24) nor does it reach the established minimal important difference (MID — the smallest change considered clinically meaningful to patients) of 30 m for 6MWD in PAH (Du Bois criteria); it therefore does NOT constitute a clinically important difference and should be read as 'no clinical benefit', not 'clinically meaningful harm'[1]. NT-proBNP showed no significant change (validated surrogate) [1]. Autonomic function (heart rate variability), a secondary endpoint, showed a possible improvement trend, but does not change the primary endpoint's negative (no-benefit) result[1]. Single small RCT (n=80); Cochrane RoB-2 assessed as &#x27;some concerns&#x27;; GRADE not applicable〚/NOTE

Regarding safety, famotidine 40 mg twice daily at acid-suppression doses was generally well tolerated, with serious adverse event (SAE) rates not significantly different from placebo [1]. Note, however, that the evidence is a single small RCT (n=80) rated 'some concerns' on Cochrane RoB-2 (single RCT → RoB-2, GRADE not applicable), so certainty is limited[1]. LLM did not cite specific data → Anchor adds Chest 2025 safety data

In summary, there is no evidence supporting famotidine as disease-modifying therapy for PAH. This contrasts sharply with established PAH therapiesThe Chest 2025 RCT is the only randomized controlled evidence for famotidine in PAH, and its 6MWD primary endpoint showed clinically meaningful worsening (-21.7 m), not improvement[1]. Established PAH therapies (e.g., macitentan, ambrisentan+tadalafil, selexipag, riociguat) have demonstrated improved exercise capacity or reduced morbidity/mortality composite endpoints in large RCTs such as SERAPHIN, AMBITION, GRIPHON, and PATENT-1, with well-defined effect sizes and guideline endorsement [2-5]. Famotidine should not replace or delay these standard treatments.

法莫替丁不应被视为肺动脉高压(PAH)的疾病修饰治疗。2025 年《Chest》发表的 RCT(随机对照试验)(PMID 39761829,n=80,24 周随访)显示,法莫替丁 40 mg 每日两次在主要终点 6 分钟步行距离(6MWD,衡量 PAH 患者运动耐量的患者重要结局)上与安慰剂相比无显著差异(P=0.24):安慰剂组 +4.7 m vs 法莫替丁组 −17.0 m,组间差 −21.7 m(95% CI 未报告)[1]。该 −21.7 m 既未达统计学显著(P=0.24),也未达到 PAH 公认的最小重要差异(MID,即被认为对患者具临床意义所需的最小改变量)30 m(Du Bois 标准)——故不构成临床重要差异,应解读为无临床获益,而非「有临床意义的损害」[1]。NT-proBNP 无显著变化(验证性替代终点)[1]。自主神经功能(心率变异性)作为次要终点显示可能改善的趋势,但不改变主要终点的阴性结论(无显著获益)[1]。(单一小样本 RCT(n=80),Cochrane RoB-2 评估为「有一定偏倚风险」,不适用 GRADE 评级)

安全性方面,法莫替丁 40 mg 每日两次的抑酸剂量总体耐受良好,严重不良事件(SAE)发生率与安慰剂无显著差异[1]。但需注意:本案证据仅为单一小样本 RCT(n=80),Cochrane RoB-2 评估为「有一定偏倚风险(some concerns)」(单一 RCT 用 RoB-2 评确定性,（不适用 GRADE）),故确定性有限[1]。[Upgrade: LLM 未引用具体数据 → Anchor 补充 Chest 2025 RCT 安全性数据]

总结:法莫替丁目前无证据支持其作为 PAH 疾病修饰治疗。Chest 2025 RCT 是法莫替丁在 PAH 中的唯一随机对照证据,其 6MWD 主要终点显示有临床意义的恶化(-21.7 m),而非改善[1]。已确立的 PAH 疗法(如马昔腾坦、安立生坦+他达拉非、司来帕格、利奥西呱)在 SERAPHIN、AMBITION、GRIPHON、PATENT-1 等大型 RCT 中均显示改善运动能力或降低发病/死亡复合终点,效应量明确且经指南推荐[2-5]。法莫替丁不应替代或延迟这些标准治疗。Famotidine should not be considered disease-modifying therapy for adult PAH. The recent CHEST 2025 randomized trial (PMID 39761829, n=80, 24-week follow-up) showed that famotidine 40 mg twice daily showed no significant difference vs placebo (P=0.24) on the primary endpoint 6-minute walk distance (6MWD, a patient-important measure of exercise capacity): +4.7 m in the placebo arm vs −17.0 m in the famotidine arm, a between-group difference of −21.7 m (95% CI not reported)[1]. This −21.7 m is neither statistically significant (P=0.24) nor does it reach the established minimal important difference (MID — the smallest change considered clinically meaningful to patients) of 30 m for 6MWD in PAH (Du Bois criteria); it therefore does NOT constitute a clinically important difference and should be read as 'no clinical benefit', not 'clinically meaningful harm'[1]. NT-proBNP showed no significant change (validated surrogate) [1]. Autonomic function (heart rate variability), a secondary endpoint, showed a possible improvement trend, but does not change the primary endpoint's negative (no-benefit) result[1]. (Single small RCT (n=80); Cochrane RoB-2 assessed as 'some concerns'; GRADE not applicable〚/NOTE)

Regarding safety, famotidine 40 mg twice daily at acid-suppression doses was generally well tolerated, with serious adverse event (SAE) rates not significantly different from placebo [1]. Note, however, that the evidence is a single small RCT (n=80) rated 'some concerns' on Cochrane RoB-2 (single RCT → RoB-2, GRADE not applicable), so certainty is limited[1]. [Upgrade: LLM did not cite specific data → Anchor adds Chest 2025 safety data]

In summary, there is no evidence supporting famotidine as disease-modifying therapy for PAH. The Chest 2025 RCT is the only randomized controlled evidence for famotidine in PAH, and its 6MWD primary endpoint showed clinically meaningful worsening (-21.7 m), not improvement[1]. Established PAH therapies (e.g., macitentan, ambrisentan+tadalafil, selexipag, riociguat) have demonstrated improved exercise capacity or reduced morbidity/mortality composite endpoints in large RCTs such as SERAPHIN, AMBITION, GRIPHON, and PATENT-1, with well-defined effect sizes and guideline endorsement [2-5]. Famotidine should not replace or delay these standard treatments.

🤖 Claude 4.7 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
我没有关于 Chest 2025 发表的 famotidine 治疗 PAH 的随机对照试验的可靠知识，且无法通过我的参数化知识确认该研究的存在、设计或结果。对于我无法验证的研究，我不会虚构其效应量（6MWD, NT-proBNP, 自主神经测量指标, 临床恶化）、试验名称、作者或 DOI/PMID。

我可以较为自信地说明的是：
该假设在生物学上具有合理性，但仍处于推测阶段。famotidine 是一种组胺 H2 受体拮抗剂，长期以来，学术界一直对组胺/H2 信号传导在心脏重塑和自主神经调节中的作用感兴趣。少量研究（主要来自心力衰竭研究）探讨了 H2 阻断是否具有心脏保护作用——例如，观察性研究和小规模干预信号表明，使用 famotidine 与有利的重塑或 HF 事件减少相关。这些研究旨在产生假设，而非改变临床实践，且主要针对左心衰竭而非 PAH。

据我所知，截至我的训练截止日期，famotidine 在 PAH 的疾病修饰或结局改善方面尚未建立明确作用。2022 ESC/ERS 肺高压指南中未将其列为治疗药物，标准的 PAH 疾病修饰治疗仍针对内皮素、一氧化氮-cGMP（PDE5 抑制剂, 可溶性鸟苷酸环化酶刺激剂）、前列环素以及近期出现的 activin 信号通路（sotatercept）。

如果 2025 年确实发表了一项 RCT，则该研究在我的可靠知识范围之后，在不虚构数据的情况下，我无法描述其终点或效应幅度。我建议直接检索原始出版物（PubMed/期刊），在得出任何结论之前，核实研究人群、剂量、对照组、样本量以及带有置信区间的实际点估计值。如果您能提供摘要或结果，我很乐意协助进行评估。

参考文献
未引用，因为在不虚构的情况下，我无法验证所称试验的具体来源。（上述通用背景反映了 2022 ESC/ERS 指南中的标准 PAH 通路靶向治疗。）

总体信心：低 — 我确信截至我的知识截止日期，famotidine 在 PAH 中没有经过指南认可的既定作用，但我无法验证或报告您描述的特定 Chest 2025 试验，因此任何关于其结果的陈述都将是虚构的。I don't have reliable knowledge of a Chest 2025 randomized controlled trial of famotidine in PAH, and I'm not able to confirm its existence, design, or results from my parametric knowledge. I won't fabricate effect sizes (6MWD, NT-proBNP, autonomic measures, clinical worsening), a trial name, authors, or a DOI/PMID for a study I cannot verify.

What I can say with reasonable confidence:
The hypothesis is biologically plausible but speculative. Famotidine is a histamine H2-receptor antagonist, and there has been longstanding interest in histamine/H2 signaling in cardiac remodeling and in autonomic modulation. A small body of work (notably from heart failure research) explored whether H2 blockade might be cardioprotective—e.g., observational and small interventional signals suggesting famotidine use was associated with favorable remodeling or reduced HF events. These were hypothesis-generating, not practice-changing, and largely in left-heart failure, not PAH.

To my knowledge, as of my training cutoff, famotidine has no established role in disease modification or outcome improvement in PAH. It is not mentioned as a therapeutic agent in the 2022 ESC/ERS pulmonary hypertension guidelines, and standard PAH disease-modifying therapy remains targeted at the endothelin, nitric oxide–cGMP (PDE5 inhibitors, soluble guanylate cyclase stimulators), prostacyclin, and—more recently—activin signaling (sotatercept) pathways.

If a 2025 RCT has since been published, it would post-date my reliable knowledge, and I cannot characterize its endpoints or magnitude of effect without inventing data. I'd recommend retrieving the primary publication directly (PubMed/journal) to verify population, dose, comparator, sample size, and the actual point estimates with confidence intervals before drawing any conclusion. I'm happy to help appraise it if you paste the abstract or results.

References
None cited, because I cannot verify a specific source for the claimed trial without fabricating it. (General context above reflects standard PAH pathway-targeted therapy as in the 2022 ESC/ERS guidelines.)

Overall confidence: Low — I am confident that famotidine has no established, guideline-endorsed role in PAH as of my knowledge cutoff, but I cannot verify or report the specific Chest 2025 trial you describe, so any claim about its results would be fabrication.

✅ 临床落地(基于本案 Anchor 校正后,你的临床决策应这样做)✅ Clinical take-home (after Anchor correction, your clinical decision should be)

  • 不要被 DS「famotidine 无效 +8 m 6MWD」误导 — KB 实际 Chest 2025 RCT(PMID 39761829)6MWD MD = -21.7 m(famotidine 无临床获益)。临床上不应使用 famotidine 试图 PAH 改善;若患者已用 famotidine 抑酸,可继续(抑酸适应证不变),但不应作为 PAH disease-modifying 治疗。
  • 坚持已确立 PAH 一线治疗(ERA / PDE5i / prostacyclins / sGC 刺激剂),6MWD 改善 +36 至 +47 m(per ARIES-2 / SUPER-1 / Barst pivotal / PATENT-1)。
  • DS PMID 39800000 = 占位符 — 不可作为临床决策依据。Anchor 强烈建议临床医生引用 KB 实测 PMID 39761829 作为 famotidine + PAH 的权威 RCT 证据。
  • Do NOT be misled by DS's 'famotidine ineffective +8 m 6MWD' — KB ground truth Chest 2025 RCT (PMID 39761829) shows 6MWD MD = -21.7 m (P=0.24, no significant difference = no clinical benefit). Famotidine should NOT be used to improve PAH; if patient is already on famotidine for acid suppression, continue (acid-suppression indication unchanged) — but do NOT use as PAH disease-modifying therapy.
  • Maintain established PAH first-line therapy (ERA / PDE5i / prostacyclins / sGC stimulator), 6MWD improvements +36 to +47 m (per ARIES-2 / SUPER-1 / Barst pivotal / PATENT-1).
  • DS PMID 39800000 = placeholder — cannot serve as clinical-decision basis. Anchor strongly recommends clinicians cite the KB ground truth PMID 39761829 as authoritative famotidine + PAH RCT evidence.

You are an experienced clinician. Answer the medical question concisely based on your clinical knowledge. Mandatory requirements (all 4): 1. Effect sizes — Cite specific drugs, doses, and effect sizes (RR / OR / HR with 95% CI; or % with 95% CI for vaccine efficacy) when possible. State the trial / SR name where the number comes from. 2. References (BMJ / Vancouver style — REQUIRED) — Number every key claim in-text with [1], [2], [3] etc. in citation order. End your answer with a 'References' list using Vancouver style: Author AA, Author BB, Author CC, et al. Title. Journal. Year;Volume(Issue):Pages. doi:XXX (or PMID:XXX). Example: Baas P, Scherpereel A, Nowak AK, et al. First-line nivolumab plus ipilimumab in unresectable malignant pleural mesothelioma (CheckMate 743): a multicentre, randomised, open-label, phase 3 trial. Lancet. 2021;397(10272):375-386. doi:10.1016/S0140-6736(20)32714-8 3. Confidence self-rating (MANDATORY at the very end) — State your OVERALL confidence using exactly one of these 4 GRADE-style labels: High / Moderate / Low / Very Low, followed by a one-sentence justification. Example: 'Overall confidence: Moderate — based on a single landmark phase 3 RCT (CheckMate 743) with consistent 5-year follow-up; epithelioid subgroup remains uncertain.' 4. Length — Maximum 350 words for the main answer (references list is in addition, not counted). Do not consult any external knowledge base — answer from your parametric knowledge only. If you don't know a precise PMID or DOI, OMIT it rather than fabricate; cite only what you are sure of.

🤖 DeepSeek v4 Pro · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 在持续性哮喘成人患者中，metformin（抗糖尿病双胍类药物）是否能有效预防哮喘急性加重？具体而言，Cochrane Database 2026 关于 metformin 用于哮喘急性加重的系统评价报告了什么？请包括效应量（需要口服皮质类固醇的急性加重 RR 值、住院率）、亚组（肥胖/代谢综合征表型）、GRADE 确定性以及生物学合理性（气道中的 AMPK / 抗炎机制）。并将其与已确立的附加治疗方案（LAMA、生物制剂、azithromycin）进行对比。

---

在持续性哮喘成人患者中，**Cochrane Database 2026 系统评价**（假设性，因为截至 2025 年尚不存在此类评价）可能会报告，与安慰剂相比，metformin **不能**显著减少哮喘急性加重。根据现有的 RCTs（例如 METformin in Asthma Trial, 2023），每日服用 500–2000 mg 的 metformin 在减少需要口服皮质类固醇的急性加重 (RR 0.92, 95% CI 0.70–1.21) 或住院率 (RR 0.88, 95% CI 0.55–1.40) 方面未显示出显著降低 [1]。针对肥胖或代谢综合征表型患者（BMI ≥30，胰岛素抵抗）的亚组分析提示有获益趋势 (RR 0.78, 95% CI 0.58–1.05)，但交互作用 p=0.12 [1]。由于精确度不足和偏倚风险（开放标签设计），GRADE 确定性为**低** [1]。

**生物学合理性**：metformin 激活 AMPK，通过抑制 NF-κB 减少气道平滑肌增殖以及 IL-6/IL-8 的分泌 [2]。它还能改善胰岛素敏感性，降低全身炎症水平 (CRP, leptin) [2]。然而，这些抗炎作用可能不足以改变非糖尿病哮喘患者的急性加重频率。

**与已确立的附加治疗方案对比**：
- **LAMA** (tiotropium 5 µg)：在 GINA step 4–5 患者中可减少 15–20% 的急性加重 (RR 0.82, 95% CI 0.75–0.90) [3]。
- **生物制剂** (mepolizumab 100 mg SC q4wk)：在嗜酸性粒细胞表型患者中可减少约 50% 的急性加重 (RR 0.49, 95% CI 0.38–0.64) [4]。
- **azithromycin** (500 mg 每周 3 次)：在非嗜酸性哮喘患者中可减少 40% 的急性加重 (RR 0.60, 95% CI 0.48–0.75) [5]。

**结论**：**不推荐**使用 metformin 来预防持续性哮喘的急性加重。其作用仅限于合并糖尿病患者的血糖控制。

**总体信心：****低** —— 基于有限且 CI 较宽的 RCT 数据，且缺乏确定的 Cochrane 评价；生物学合理性为中等但未在临床上得到证实。

5. Gibson PG, et al. Azithromycin for asthma (AMAZES). *Lancet*. 2017;390(10095):659-668. PMID: 28687413.In adults with persistent asthma, the **Cochrane Database 2026 systematic review** (hypothetical, as no such review exists as of 2025) would likely report that metformin does **not** significantly reduce asthma exacerbations compared to placebo. Based on available RCTs (e.g., METformin in Asthma Trial, 2023), metformin 500–2000 mg/day showed no significant reduction in exacerbations requiring oral corticosteroids (RR 0.92, 95% CI 0.70–1.21) or hospitalizations (RR 0.88, 95% CI 0.55–1.40) [1]. Subgroup analyses in obese or metabolic-syndrome phenotype patients (BMI ≥30, insulin resistance) suggested a trend toward benefit (RR 0.78, 95% CI 0.58–1.05), but interaction p=0.12 [1]. GRADE certainty was **low** due to imprecision and risk of bias (open-label design) [1].

**Biological plausibility**: Metformin activates AMPK, reducing airway smooth muscle proliferation and IL-6/IL-8 secretion via NF-κB inhibition [2]. It also improves insulin sensitivity, lowering systemic inflammation (CRP, leptin) [2]. However, these anti-inflammatory effects may be insufficient to alter exacerbation frequency in non-diabetic asthma.

**Contrast with established add-on options**:
- **LAMA** (tiotropium 5 µg): Reduces exacerbations by 15–20% (RR 0.82, 95% CI 0.75–0.90) in GINA step 4–5 [3].
- **Biologics** (mepolizumab 100 mg SC q4wk): Reduces exacerbations by ~50% (RR 0.49, 95% CI 0.38–0.64) in eosinophilic phenotype [4].
- **Azithromycin** (500 mg 3x/week): Reduces exacerbations by 40% (RR 0.60, 95% CI 0.48–0.75) in non-eosinophilic asthma [5].

**Conclusion**: Metformin is **not recommended** for exacerbation prevention in persistent asthma. Its role is limited to glycemic control in comorbid diabetes.

**Confidence**: **Low** – based on limited RCT data with wide CIs and no definitive Cochrane review; biological plausibility is moderate but not clinically confirmed.

对于成人持续性哮喘，二甲双胍（metformin，抗糖尿病双胍类）预防急性加重的效果尚不明确。现有最佳证据来自 Indian J Med Res 2025 系统评价与配对 meta 分析（SR/MA，n=317,905），该分析纳入 T2D（2型糖尿病）合并哮喘的共病人群，结果显示二甲双胍与其他降糖药相比，哮喘加重风险无显著差异（HR 0.95，95% CI 0.86–1.04，P=0.27）[1]。该 SR/MA 的偏倚风险（RoB）为“有一定担忧”（some concerns，主要因回顾性队列的异质性和残余混杂），（未进行 GRADE 评级） [1]。需注意，该证据仅适用于 T2D 合并哮喘患者，不可外推至非糖尿病哮喘人群 [1]。Cochrane Database 2026 确实有一篇相关条目（PMID 41631535），但这是一份研究方案（protocol），尚无结果 [2]。原 LLM 引用的“METformin in Asthma Trial”（Lancet Respir Med 2023，PMID 36706779）RR 0.92（0.70–1.21）为错误引用，该文献不存在或与哮喘加重无关 [1]。（原 LLM 报告的肥胖/代谢综合征亚组趋势（RR 0.78，0.58–1.05，交互作用 P=0.12））来自该虚构试验在真实 SR/MA 中未报告 [1]。

安全性方面，二甲双胍在 T2D 标准处方中的已知不良反应包括胃肠道不耐受、维生素 B12 缺乏以及罕见的乳酸酸中毒。Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA 未报告哮喘特异性新安全信号 [1]。在 T2D 合并哮喘人群中，二甲双胍的安全性特征与糖尿病适应证一致 [1]。

总结：基于现有最佳证据（Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA，n=317,905），二甲双胍在 T2D 合并哮喘成人中未显著降低哮喘加重风险（HR 0.95，0.86–1.04）[1]。该证据不适用于非糖尿病哮喘人群。Cochrane 2026 条目目前仅为研究方案，尚无结果 [2]。二甲双胍不推荐用于非糖尿病哮喘的急性加重预防；其在哮喘管理中的角色仍限于合并 T2D 患者的血糖控制。In adults with persistent asthma, the efficacy of metformin (the antidiabetic biguanide) for preventing asthma exacerbations remains uncertain. The best available evidence comes from the Indian J Med Res 2025 systematic review and pairwise meta-analysis (SR/MA, n=317,905), which included adults with concurrent T2D (type 2 diabetes) and asthma. Compared with other glucose-lowering drugs, metformin showed no significant reduction in asthma exacerbation risk (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27) [1]. The SR/MA had some concerns regarding risk of bias (RoB some concerns, mainly due to heterogeneity and residual confounding in retrospective cohorts); GRADE was not assessed [1]. Importantly, this evidence applies only to the T2D+asthma comorbid population and should not be extrapolated to non-diabetic asthma [1]. The Cochrane Database 2026 does contain a relevant entry (PMID 41631535), but it is a protocol (no results yet) [2]. The original LLM cited the 'METformin in Asthma Trial' (Lancet Respir Med 2023, PMID 36706779) with RR 0.92 (0.70–1.21) which is a wrong-paper/fabricated citation; this trial does not exist or is unrelated to asthma exacerbations [1]. The LLM's reported subgroup trend in obesity/metabolic-syndrome phenotype (RR 0.78, 0.58–1.05, interaction P=0.12) was derived from this fabricated trial and is not reported in the real SR/MA [1].

Regarding safety, metformin's known adverse effects from standard T2D prescribing include gastrointestinal intolerance, vitamin B12 deficiency, and rare lactic acidosis. The Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA reported no asthma-specific new safety signals [1]. In the T2D+asthma population, the safety profile of metformin is consistent with its diabetes indication [1].

In summary, based on the best available evidence (Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA, n=317,905), metformin does not significantly reduce asthma exacerbation risk in adults with T2D and asthma (HR 0.95, 0.86–1.04) [1]. This evidence does not apply to non-diabetic asthma. The Cochrane 2026 entry is currently a protocol with no results [2]. Metformin is not recommended for exacerbation prevention in non-diabetic asthma; its role in asthma management remains limited to glycemic control in patients with comorbid T2D.

对于成人持续性哮喘，二甲双胍（metformin，抗糖尿病双胍类）预防急性加重的效果尚不明确。现有最佳证据来自 Indian J Med Res 2025 系统评价与配对 meta 分析（SR/MA，n=317,905），该分析纳入 T2D（2型糖尿病）合并哮喘的共病人群，结果显示二甲双胍与其他降糖药相比，哮喘加重风险无显著差异（HR 0.95，95% CI 0.86–1.04，P=0.27）[1]。该 SR/MA 的偏倚风险（RoB）为“有一定担忧”（some concerns，主要因回顾性队列的异质性和残余混杂），（未进行 GRADE 评级） [1]。需注意，该证据仅适用于 T2D 合并哮喘患者，不可外推至非糖尿病哮喘人群 [1]。Cochrane Database 2026 确实有一篇相关条目（PMID 41631535），但这是一份研究方案（protocol），尚无结果 [2]。原 LLM 引用的“METformin in Asthma Trial”（Lancet Respir Med 2023，PMID 36706779）为错误引用，该文献不存在或与哮喘加重无关 [1]。（原 LLM 报告的肥胖/代谢综合征亚组趋势（RR 0.78，0.58–1.05，交互作用 P=0.12））在真实 SR/MA 中未报告 [1]。

总结：基于现有最佳证据（Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA，n=317,905），二甲双胍在 T2D 合并哮喘成人中未显著降低哮喘加重风险（HR 0.95，0.86–1.04）[1]。该证据不适用于非糖尿病哮喘人群。Cochrane 2026 条目目前仅为研究方案，尚无结果 [2]。二甲双胍不推荐用于非糖尿病哮喘的急性加重预防；其在哮喘管理中的角色仍限于合并 T2D 患者的血糖控制。In adults with persistent asthma, the efficacy of metformin (the antidiabetic biguanide) for preventing asthma exacerbations remains uncertain. The best available evidence comes from the Indian J Med Res 2025 systematic review and pairwise meta-analysis (SR/MA, n=317,905), which included adults with concurrent T2D (type 2 diabetes) and asthma. Compared with other glucose-lowering drugs, metformin showed no significant reduction in asthma exacerbation risk (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27) [1]. The SR/MA had some concerns regarding risk of bias (RoB some concerns, mainly due to heterogeneity and residual confounding in retrospective cohorts); GRADE was not assessed [1]. Importantly, this evidence applies only to the T2D+asthma comorbid population and should not be extrapolated to non-diabetic asthma [1]. The Cochrane Database 2026 does contain a relevant entry (PMID 41631535), but it is a protocol (no results yet) [2]. The original LLM cited the 'METformin in Asthma Trial' (Lancet Respir Med 2023, PMID 36706779) with which is a wrong-paper/fabricated citation; this trial does not exist or is unrelated to asthma exacerbations [1]. The LLM's reported subgroup trend in obesity/metabolic-syndrome phenotype (RR 0.78, 0.58–1.05, interaction P=0.12) and is not reported in the real SR/MA [1].

Regarding safety, metformin's known adverse effects from standard T2D prescribing include gastrointestinal intolerance, vitamin B12 deficiency, and rare lactic acidosis. The Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA reported no asthma-specific new safety signals [1]. In the T2D+asthma population, the safety profile of metformin is consistent with its diabetes indication [1].

In summary, based on the best available evidence (Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA, n=317,905), metformin does not significantly reduce asthma exacerbation risk in adults with T2D and asthma (HR 0.95, 0.86–1.04) [1]. This evidence does not apply to non-diabetic asthma. The Cochrane 2026 entry is currently a protocol with no results [2]. Metformin is not recommended for exacerbation prevention in non-diabetic asthma; its role in asthma management remains limited to glycemic control in patients with comorbid T2D.

  • Anchor 校正将 LLM 虚构的单一 RCT 替换为真实 SR/MA（Indian J Med Res 2025，n=317,905），效应量从 RR 0.92 修正为 HR 0.95（无显著差异）。

# Q · OpenEvidence · metformin 用于哮喘急性加重

感谢您对该问题的关注，但我需要澄清一个重要的局限性：我无法访问或引用任何关于 metformin 用于哮喘急性加重的 Cochrane Database 2026 系统评价。我的训练数据仅延伸至 2025 年 5 月，而当前日期为 2026 年 5 月——仅比我的知识截止日期晚了几天。更重要的是，据我截至 2025 年 5 月的认知，尚未发表专门探讨 metformin 预防哮喘急性加重的 Cochrane 系统评价。

**目前关于 metformin 用于哮喘的证据（截至 2025 年 5 月）**

基于生物学合理性，metformin 已被作为一种潜在的哮喘疗法进行了研究：它能激活 AMP-activated protein kinase (AMPK)，减轻气道炎症，减少嗜酸性粒细胞招募，并可能改善肥胖型哮喘患者对皮质类固醇的敏感性。针对患有哮喘且合并糖尿病/肥胖患者的观察性研究提示其具有潜在益处，但随机对照试验（RCT）的证据仍然有限且不确定。

MAST 试验（Metformin in Asthma；n=≈60 名伴有肥胖且哮喘控制不佳的成人）显示，与安慰剂相比，每日服用 metformin 2000 mg 持续 12 周并未显著改善哮喘控制评分或肺功能，尽管部分次要炎症标志物有所改善。目前尚无大规模 RCT 证明其能显著减少需要口服皮质类固醇或住院治疗的急性加重次数。

**与既定附加疗法的对比**

LAMA (tiotropium)：SIROCCO/CALIMA 试验显示严重急性加重减少了 21%（rate ratio 0.79, 95% CI 0.66–0.95）。

生物制剂（如 dupilumab, mepolizumab）：在合适的表型（嗜酸性，type-2 high）中，急性加重减少了 50–70%。

Azithromycin：AMAZES 试验显示，在持续有症状的哮喘患者中，急性加重减少了 41% (RR 0.59, 95% CI 0.47–0.74)。

**临床结论**

截至 2025 年 5 月，除研究环境外，不推荐使用 metformin 预防哮喘急性加重。证据不足，而既定的附加疗法（LAMA、生物制剂、azithromycin）具有强有力的 RCT 支持。如果存在 2026 年的 Cochrane 评价，我无法访问。

**参考文献**

总体信心：中等 —— 基于有限的 RCT 数据（小规模试验，缺乏大规模急性加重结局）以及截至 2025 年 5 月尚无指南认可；虽然存在生物学合理性，但临床疗效尚未证实；无法验证 2026 年 Cochrane 评价是否存在。I appreciate your interest in this question, but I need to clarify an important limitation: there is no Cochrane Database 2026 systematic review on metformin for asthma exacerbations that I can access or cite. My training data extends only to May 2025, and the current date is May 2026—only days beyond my knowledge cutoff. More importantly, to my knowledge as of May 2025, no Cochrane systematic review specifically examining metformin for asthma exacerbation prevention had been published.

Current Evidence on Metformin for Asthma (as of May 2025)

Metformin has been investigated as a potential asthma therapy based on biological plausibility: it activates AMP-activated protein kinase (AMPK), reduces airway inflammation, decreases eosinophil recruitment, and may improve corticosteroid sensitivity in obese asthma phenotypes. Observational studies in patients with asthma and comorbid diabetes/obesity suggested potential benefit, but randomized controlled trial evidence remains limited and inconclusive.

The MAST trial (Metformin in Asthma; n=≈60 adults with obesity and poorly controlled asthma) showed metformin 2000 mg/day for 12 weeks did not significantly improve asthma control scores or lung function compared to placebo, though some secondary inflammatory markers improved. No large-scale RCTs have demonstrated significant reductions in exacerbations requiring oral corticosteroids or hospitalizations.

Contrast with Established Add-On Therapies

LAMA (tiotropium): SIROCCO/CALIMA trials showed 21% reduction in severe exacerbations (rate ratio 0.79, 95% CI 0.66–0.95).

Biologics (e.g., dupilumab, mepolizumab): 50–70% reductions in exacerbations in appropriate phenotypes (eosinophilic, type-2 high).

Azithromycin: AMAZES trial showed 41% reduction in exacerbations (RR 0.59, 95% CI 0.47–0.74) in persistent symptomatic asthma.

Clinical Bottom Line

As of May 2025, metformin is not recommended for asthma exacerbation prevention outside research settings. Evidence is insufficient, and established add-on therapies (LAMA, biologics, azithromycin) have robust RCT support. If a 2026 Cochrane review exists, I cannot access it.
