对于成人持续性哮喘，二甲双胍预防急性加重的有效性证据有限。印度《印度医学研究杂志》(Indian J Med Res)2025 年发表的一项大型系统评价与 meta 分析(纳入 317,905 例 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘患者)显示，二甲双胍与其他降糖药相比，哮喘急性加重风险无显著差异(HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27)[1]。该分析汇总了中位随访 3 年的回顾性队列数据，偏倚风险为“有一定担忧”(some concerns，因异质性和残余混杂)[1]。The MAST trial (Metformin in Asthma; n=≈60 adults with obesity and poorly controlled asthma) showed metformin 2000 mg/day for 12 weeks did not significantly improve asthma control scores or lung function compared to placebo, though some secondary inflammatory markers improved. No large-scale RCTs have demonstrated significant reductions in exacerbations requiring oral corticosteroids or hospitalizations.需注意，上述证据仅限于 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘人群，不应外推至非糖尿病哮喘患者[1]。Cochrane Database 2026 年已注册一项关于二甲双胍治疗哮喘急性加重的系统评价方案(protocol，PMID 41631535)，但该方案尚未完成，目前（无效应量或 GRADE 评级可报告）[2]。临床警示:现有证据不支持在 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘人群之外使用二甲双胍预防哮喘急性加重；对于已使用二甲双胍的 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘患者，无需因哮喘原因调整用药。

安全性方面，二甲双胍在糖尿病适应证中的已知不良反应包括胃肠道不耐受、维生素 B12 缺乏以及罕见的乳酸酸中毒。上述 Indian J Med Res 2025 系统评价未报告哮喘特异性新安全信号[1]。Contrast with Established Add-On Therapies: LAMA (tiotropium): SIROCCO/CALIMA trials showed 21% reduction in severe exacerbations (rate ratio 0.79, 95% CI 0.66–0.95). Biologics (e.g., dupilumab, 美泊利单抗(mepolizumab)): 50–70% reductions in exacerbations in appropriate phenotypes (eosinophilic, type-2 high). 阿奇霉素(Azithromycin): AMAZES trial showed 41% reduction in exacerbations (RR 0.59, 95% CI 0.47–0.74) in persistent symptomatic asthma.

总结而言，As of May 2025, metformin is not recommended for asthma exacerbation prevention outside research settings. Evidence is insufficient, and established add-on therapies (LAMA, biologics, 阿奇霉素) have robust RCT support. If a 2026 Cochrane review exists, I cannot access it.截至 2025 年，二甲双胍不推荐用于哮喘急性加重预防(除研究环境外)。现有最佳证据(Indian J Med Res 2025 系统评价，n=317,905)显示，在 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘人群中，二甲双胍未显著降低急性加重风险(HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04)[1]。Cochrane 2026 年方案已注册但尚未完成[2]。已确立的附加疗法(如长效抗胆碱能药物、生物制剂、阿奇霉素)有更充分的随机对照试验支持。For adults with persistent asthma, evidence for metformin in preventing exacerbations is limited. A large systematic review and meta-analysis published in the Indian Journal of Medical Research in 2025 (n=317,905 patients with type 2 diabetes and asthma) showed that metformin, compared with other glucose-lowering drugs, did not significantly affect the risk of asthma exacerbation (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27) [1]. This analysis pooled retrospective cohort data with a median follow-up of 3 years; risk of bias was rated as 'some concerns' (due to heterogeneity and residual confounding) [1]. The MAST trial (Metformin in Asthma; n=≈60 adults with obesity and poorly controlled asthma) showed metformin 2000 mg/day for 12 weeks did not significantly improve asthma control scores or lung function compared to placebo, though some secondary inflammatory markers improved. No large-scale RCTs have demonstrated significant reductions in exacerbations requiring oral corticosteroids or hospitalizations.Of note, this evidence is restricted to patients with comorbid type 2 diabetes and asthma and should not be extrapolated to non-diabetic asthma populations [1]. The Cochrane Database 2026 has registered a protocol for a systematic review on metformin for asthma exacerbations (PMID 41631535), but this protocol is not yet completed; no effect sizes or GRADE ratings are available [2]. Clinical caution: current evidence does not support using metformin for asthma exacerbation prevention outside the T2D+asthma population; for patients already on metformin for T2D with asthma, no change in medication is warranted for asthma reasons.

Regarding safety, known adverse effects of metformin from its diabetes indication include gastrointestinal intolerance, vitamin B12 deficiency, and rare lactic acidosis. The Indian J Med Res 2025 systematic review reported no asthma-specific new safety signals [1]. Contrast with Established Add-On Therapies: LAMA (tiotropium): SIROCCO/CALIMA trials showed 21% reduction in severe exacerbations (rate ratio 0.79, 95% CI 0.66–0.95). Biologics (e.g., dupilumab, mepolizumab): 50–70% reductions in exacerbations in appropriate phenotypes (eosinophilic, type-2 high). Azithromycin: AMAZES trial showed 41% reduction in exacerbations (RR 0.59, 95% CI 0.47–0.74) in persistent symptomatic asthma.

In summary, As of May 2025, metformin is not recommended for asthma exacerbation prevention outside research settings. Evidence is insufficient, and established add-on therapies (LAMA, biologics, azithromycin) have robust RCT support. If a 2026 Cochrane review exists, I cannot access it.as of 2025, metformin is not recommended for asthma exacerbation prevention outside research settings. The best available evidence (Indian J Med Res 2025 systematic review, n=317,905) shows that metformin does not significantly reduce exacerbation risk in patients with type 2 diabetes and asthma (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04) [1]. The Cochrane 2026 protocol is registered but not yet completed [2]. Established add-on therapies (e.g., long-acting muscarinic antagonists, biologics, azithromycin) have more robust RCT support.

对于成人持续性哮喘，二甲双胍预防急性加重的有效性证据有限。印度《印度医学研究杂志》(Indian J Med Res)2025 年发表的一项大型系统评价与 meta 分析(纳入 317,905 例 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘患者)显示，二甲双胍与其他降糖药相比，哮喘急性加重风险无显著差异(HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27)[1]。该分析汇总了中位随访 3 年的回顾性队列数据，偏倚风险为“有一定担忧”(some concerns，因异质性和残余混杂)[1]。需注意，上述证据仅限于 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘人群，不应外推至非糖尿病哮喘患者[1]。Cochrane Database 2026 年已注册一项关于二甲双胍治疗哮喘急性加重的系统评价方案(protocol，PMID 41631535)，但该方案尚未完成，目前（无效应量或 GRADE 评级可报告）[2]。(临床警示:现有证据不支持在 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘人群之外使用二甲双胍预防哮喘急性加重；对于已使用二甲双胍的 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘患者，无需因哮喘原因调整用药。)

安全性方面，二甲双胍在糖尿病适应证中的已知不良反应包括胃肠道不耐受、维生素 B12 缺乏以及罕见的乳酸酸中毒。上述 Indian J Med Res 2025 系统评价未报告哮喘特异性新安全信号[1]。

总结而言，截至 2025 年，二甲双胍不推荐用于哮喘急性加重预防(除研究环境外)。现有最佳证据(Indian J Med Res 2025 系统评价，n=317,905)显示，在 2 型糖尿病合并哮喘人群中，二甲双胍未显著降低急性加重风险(HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04)[1]。Cochrane 2026 年方案已注册但尚未完成[2]。已确立的附加疗法(如长效抗胆碱能药物、生物制剂、阿奇霉素)有更充分的随机对照试验支持。For adults with persistent asthma, evidence for metformin in preventing exacerbations is limited. In summary, as of 2025, metformin is not recommended for asthma exacerbation prevention outside research settings. The best available evidence (Indian J Med Res 2025 systematic review, n=317,905) shows that metformin does not significantly reduce exacerbation risk in patients with type 2 diabetes and asthma (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04) [1]. The Cochrane 2026 protocol is registered but not yet completed [2]. Established add-on therapies (e.g., long-acting muscarinic antagonists, biologics, azithromycin) have more robust RCT support.

# Q · ChatGPT 5.5 · Metformin 用于哮喘急性加重

目前不应将 metformin 用于预防持续性哮喘成年患者的哮喘急性加重。由于我不清楚 Cochrane Database 2026 综述中的精确效应估计值、PMID 或 GRADE 评级，因此无法在不冒伪造风险的情况下安全地报告其针对口服皮质类固醇治疗的急性加重或住院率的 RR 值。

基于既有的临床知识，metformin 在哮喘中的任何信号在生物学上是合理的，但不足以改变临床实践。metformin 激活 AMP-activated protein kinase，可能减轻全身胰岛素抵抗、肥胖相关炎症、氧化应激以及气道炎症信号传导，但这些机制并未确立其临床疗效 [1]。在肥胖或代谢综合征相关的哮喘表型中，这种作用可能最为合理，但亚组的可信度需要预设的交互作用测试和一致的临床结果支持。

这与已证实的哮喘附加治疗方案形成对比。在未控制的哮喘中，在 ICS/LABA 基础上每日一次增加 tiotropium Respimat 5 μg 可延长首次严重急性加重的时间（PrimoTinA-asthma 研究），HR 0.79, 95% CI 0.62–1.00 [2]。在严重嗜酸性粒细胞性哮喘中，每 4 周皮下注射 mepolizumab 100 mg 可减少具有临床意义的急性加重（MENSA 研究），rate ratio 0.47, 95% CI 0.35–0.64 [3]。benralizumab 30 mg 皮下注射在 SIROCCO/CALIMA 研究中降低了嗜酸性粒细胞性哮喘的年急性加重率，根据试验/剂量组的不同，rate ratios 约为 0.45–0.64 [4]。每 2 周皮下注射 dupilumab 200/300 mg 可减少严重急性加重（QUEST 研究），200 mg 组 rate ratio 0.52, 95% CI 0.41–0.66，300 mg 组 rate ratio 0.54, 95% CI 0.43–0.68 [5]。每周三次 azithromycin 500 mg 可减少急性加重（AMAZES 研究），incidence rate ratio 0.59, 95% CI 0.47–0.74 [6]。

**底线：metformin 是一个有趣的药物重新定位假设，特别是针对肥胖/代谢性哮喘，但不应取代指南指导的附加治疗。**

参考文献

信心自我评级：低 — 我了解既有的哮喘治疗证据，但在没有外部验证的情况下，不清楚 Cochrane 2026 关于 metformin 综述的精确结果。Metformin should not currently be used to prevent asthma exacerbations in adults with persistent asthma. I do not know the precise effect estimates, PMID, or GRADE judgments from the Cochrane Database 2026 review, so I cannot safely report its RR values for oral-corticosteroid-treated exacerbations or hospitalisation without risking fabrication.

Based on established clinical knowledge, any metformin signal in asthma is biologically plausible but not practice-changing. Metformin activates AMP-activated protein kinase, may reduce systemic insulin resistance, obesity-related inflammation, oxidative stress, and airway inflammatory signalling, but these mechanisms do not establish clinical efficacy [1]. It may be most plausible in obesity or metabolic-syndrome asthma phenotypes, but subgroup credibility would require prespecified interaction testing and consistent clinical outcomes.

This contrasts with proven add-on asthma options. In uncontrolled asthma, tiotropium Respimat 5 μg once daily added to ICS/LABA increased time to first severe exacerbation in PrimoTinA-asthma, HR 0.79, 95% CI 0.62–1.00 [2]. In severe eosinophilic asthma, mepolizumab 100 mg SC every 4 weeks reduced clinically significant exacerbations in MENSA, rate ratio 0.47, 95% CI 0.35–0.64 [3]. Benralizumab 30 mg SC reduced annual exacerbation rates in SIROCCO/CALIMA in eosinophilic asthma, rate ratios approximately 0.45–0.64 depending on trial/dosing group [4]. Dupilumab 200/300 mg SC every 2 weeks reduced severe exacerbations in QUEST, rate ratio 0.52, 95% CI 0.41–0.66 for 200 mg and 0.54, 95% CI 0.43–0.68 for 300 mg [5]. Azithromycin 500 mg three times weekly reduced exacerbations in AMAZES, incidence rate ratio 0.59, 95% CI 0.47–0.74 [6].

Bottom line: metformin is an interesting repurposing hypothesis, especially for obese/metabolic asthma, but should not replace guideline-directed add-ons.

对于成人持续性哮喘，二甲双胍（metformin）预防急性加重的有效性目前尚无充分证据支持。印度《Indian Journal of Medical Research》2025 年系统评价（meta 分析，n=317,905，T2D 合并哮喘共病人群）汇总显示，二甲双胍与其他降糖药相比，哮喘加重调整后风险比（HR）为 0.95（95% CI 0.86–1.04，P=0.27），未达到统计学显著性，即二甲双胍在 T2D 合并哮喘患者中未显著降低加重风险 [1]。该 meta 分析纳入大型回顾性队列，偏倚风险（RoB）为“有一定担忧”（some concerns，因异质性和残余混杂），（未进行 GRADE 评级） [1]。Cochrane Database 2026 年相关条目（PMID 41631535）实际为一项已注册的 Cochrane 系统评价方案（protocol），尚未完成，因此目前（无效应量或 GRADE 评级可供报告） [2]。原回答中提及的二甲双胍通过 AMPK 激活、减轻胰岛素抵抗和气道炎症等机制具有生物学合理性，但这些机制并不能确立临床疗效这些机制尚未转化为临床获益，尤其在非糖尿病哮喘人群中缺乏证据 [1]。（亚组方面，肥胖或代谢综合征表型可能更具潜力，但现有数据未提供预设交互检验结果，亚组可信度有限 [1]。）临床警示：现有最佳证据不支持在非糖尿病哮喘患者中使用二甲双胍预防加重；在 T2D 合并哮喘患者中，二甲双胍也未显示优于其他降糖药的额外获益。

安全性方面，二甲双胍在糖尿病适应证中的已知不良反应包括胃肠道不耐受、维生素 B12 缺乏及罕见乳酸酸中毒。Indian MA 2025 未报告哮喘特异性新安全信号，其安全性特征与 T2D 标准处方一致 [1]。原回答中引用的其他哮喘治疗药物（噻托溴铵、美泊利单抗、贝那利珠单抗、度普利尤单抗、阿奇霉素）的安全性数据与各自注册试验一致，此处不赘述。

总结：二甲双胍预防哮喘急性加重目前（缺乏高质量临床证据）。现有最佳证据（Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA，n=317,905）显示在 T2D 合并哮喘共病人群中效应为阴性（HR 0.95，95% CI 0.86–1.04）[1]；Cochrane 2026 方案尚未完成 [2]。二甲双胍作为哮喘附加治疗仍属研究性假设，不应替代指南推荐的哮喘控制药物（如 ICS/LABA、生物制剂、阿奇霉素等）。In adults with persistent asthma, metformin is not currently supported by sufficient evidence for preventing exacerbations. The Indian Journal of Medical Research 2025 systematic review and meta-analysis (n=317,905, population with concurrent T2D and asthma) reported a null effect: adjusted hazard ratio (HR) 0.95 (95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27) for metformin versus other glucose-lowering drugs, indicating no significant reduction in asthma exacerbation risk [1]. This meta-analysis included large retrospective cohorts; risk of bias (RoB) was rated 'some concerns' (due to heterogeneity and residual confounding), and GRADE was not assessed [1]. The Cochrane Database 2026 entry (PMID 41631535) is a registered protocol for a future Cochrane Review, not a completed systematic review; therefore, no effect estimates or GRADE ratings are yet available [2]. The original answer noted biological plausibility via AMPK activation, reduced insulin resistance, and airway inflammation, but these mechanisms do not establish clinical efficacy these mechanisms have not translated into clinical benefit, especially in non-diabetic asthma populations [1]. Regarding subgroups, the obesity or metabolic-syndrome phenotype may hold potential, but current data lack prespecified interaction testing, limiting subgroup credibility [1]. Clinical caution: The best available evidence does not support metformin for exacerbation prevention in non-diabetic asthma; in T2D+asthma patients, metformin shows no additional benefit over other glucose-lowering drugs.

Regarding safety, metformin's known adverse effects from its diabetes indication include gastrointestinal intolerance, vitamin B12 deficiency, and rare lactic acidosis. The Indian MA 2025 reported no asthma-specific new safety signals; the safety profile is consistent with standard T2D prescribing [1]. The original answer's references to other asthma therapies (tiotropium, mepolizumab, benralizumab, dupilumab, azithromycin) have safety data consistent with their respective registration trials and are not repeated here.

In summary, metformin for preventing asthma exacerbations currently lacks high-quality clinical evidence. The best available evidence (Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA, n=317,905) shows a null effect in T2D+asthma comorbid patients (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04) [1]; the Cochrane 2026 protocol is not yet completed [2]. Metformin as an add-on asthma therapy remains a research hypothesis and should not replace guideline-directed asthma controllers (e.g., ICS/LABA, biologics, azithromycin).

对于成人持续性哮喘，二甲双胍（metformin）预防急性加重的有效性目前尚无充分证据支持。印度《Indian Journal of Medical Research》2025 年系统评价（meta 分析，n=317,905，T2D 合并哮喘共病人群）汇总显示，二甲双胍与其他降糖药相比，哮喘加重调整后风险比（HR）为 0.95（95% CI 0.86–1.04，P=0.27），未达到统计学显著性，即二甲双胍在 T2D 合并哮喘患者中未显著降低加重风险 [1]。该 meta 分析纳入大型回顾性队列，偏倚风险（RoB）为“有一定担忧”（some concerns，因异质性和残余混杂），（未进行 GRADE 评级） [1]。Cochrane Database 2026 年相关条目（PMID 41631535）实际为一项已注册的 Cochrane 系统评价方案（protocol），尚未完成，因此目前（无效应量或 GRADE 评级可供报告） [2]。原回答中提及的二甲双胍通过 AMPK 激活、减轻胰岛素抵抗和气道炎症等机制具有生物学合理性，但这些机制尚未转化为临床获益，尤其在非糖尿病哮喘人群中缺乏证据 [1]。（亚组方面，肥胖或代谢综合征表型可能更具潜力，但现有数据未提供预设交互检验结果，亚组可信度有限 [1]。）(临床警示：现有最佳证据不支持在非糖尿病哮喘患者中使用二甲双胍预防加重；在 T2D 合并哮喘患者中，二甲双胍也未显示优于其他降糖药的额外获益。)

安全性方面，二甲双胍在糖尿病适应证中的已知不良反应包括胃肠道不耐受、维生素 B12 缺乏及罕见乳酸酸中毒。Indian MA 2025 未报告哮喘特异性新安全信号，其安全性特征与 T2D 标准处方一致 [1]。原回答中引用的其他哮喘治疗药物（噻托溴铵、美泊利单抗、贝那利珠单抗、度普利尤单抗、阿奇霉素）的安全性数据与各自注册试验一致，此处不赘述。

总结：二甲双胍预防哮喘急性加重目前（缺乏高质量临床证据）。现有最佳证据（Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA，n=317,905）显示在 T2D 合并哮喘共病人群中效应为阴性（HR 0.95，95% CI 0.86–1.04）[1]；Cochrane 2026 方案尚未完成 [2]。二甲双胍作为哮喘附加治疗仍属研究性假设，不应替代指南推荐的哮喘控制药物（如 ICS/LABA、生物制剂、阿奇霉素等）。In adults with persistent asthma, metformin is not currently supported by sufficient evidence for preventing exacerbations. The Indian Journal of Medical Research 2025 systematic review and meta-analysis (n=317,905, population with concurrent T2D and asthma) reported a null effect: adjusted hazard ratio (HR) 0.95 (95% CI 0.86–1.04, P=0.27) for metformin versus other glucose-lowering drugs, indicating no significant reduction in asthma exacerbation risk [1]. This meta-analysis included large retrospective cohorts; risk of bias (RoB) was rated 'some concerns' (due to heterogeneity and residual confounding), and GRADE was not assessed [1]. The Cochrane Database 2026 entry (PMID 41631535) is a registered protocol for a future Cochrane Review, not a completed systematic review; therefore, no effect estimates or GRADE ratings are yet available [2]. The original answer noted biological plausibility via AMPK activation, reduced insulin resistance, and airway inflammation, but these mechanisms have not translated into clinical benefit, especially in non-diabetic asthma populations [1]. Regarding subgroups, the obesity or metabolic-syndrome phenotype may hold potential, but current data lack prespecified interaction testing, limiting subgroup credibility [1]. (Clinical caution: The best available evidence does not support metformin for exacerbation prevention in non-diabetic asthma; in T2D+asthma patients, metformin shows no additional benefit over other glucose-lowering drugs.)

Regarding safety, metformin's known adverse effects from its diabetes indication include gastrointestinal intolerance, vitamin B12 deficiency, and rare lactic acidosis. The Indian MA 2025 reported no asthma-specific new safety signals; the safety profile is consistent with standard T2D prescribing [1]. The original answer's references to other asthma therapies (tiotropium, mepolizumab, benralizumab, dupilumab, azithromycin) have safety data consistent with their respective registration trials and are not repeated here.

In summary, metformin for preventing asthma exacerbations currently lacks high-quality clinical evidence. The best available evidence (Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA, n=317,905) shows a null effect in T2D+asthma comorbid patients (HR 0.95, 95% CI 0.86–1.04) [1]; the Cochrane 2026 protocol is not yet completed [2]. Metformin as an add-on asthma therapy remains a research hypothesis and should not replace guideline-directed asthma controllers (e.g., ICS/LABA, biologics, azithromycin).

🤖 Claude 4.7 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
我无法核实关于 metformin 用于哮喘急性加重的一篇 2026 年 Cochrane Database 系统评价的存在、范围或研究结果。2026 年的出版物超出了我的可靠知识截止日期，对于我无法确认的综述，我不会伪造汇总风险比（RR）、亚组估算值、GRADE 分级、作者或 DOI/PMID。

基于可验证的参数化知识，我可以说明：
metformin 在预防哮喘急性加重方面没有确立且获得指南认可的作用。在我知识截止日期前的 GINA 策略文档中，它不被推荐作为哮喘治疗药物。这种研究兴趣在生物学上是合理的——metformin 的 AMPK 激活、抗炎作用以及对代谢功能障碍的改善，被认为可能使肥胖/代谢型哮喘表型获益，而该表型通常与控制效果较差和类固醇耐药相关。

目前最强有力的证据来自观察性研究而非随机对照试验。一项经常被引用的英国队列/药物流行病学分析（Wu 及其同事使用初级保健数据库）报告称，在患有糖尿病的患者中，使用 metformin 与哮喘急性加重和哮喘相关住院率降低相关，且在肥胖亚组中信号更强 [1]。此类研究仅用于产生假设，且容易受到适应症混杂（confounding by indication）和永生时间偏倚（immortal-time bias）的影响；它们不能确立疗效。据我所知，目前还没有足够样本量的随机对照试验证实 metformin 能减少持续性哮喘的急性加重，我也没注意到有任何 Cochrane 综述对这类试验进行过综合分析。

如果现在确实存在一篇 2026 年的 Cochrane 综述，在不编造数字的情况下，我无法描述其关于需要 OCS 的急性加重、住院率、肥胖/代谢综合征亚组的 RR 值或 GRADE 确定性。我建议从 Cochrane Library 获取实际的综述，以核实纳入的试验、对照组、带有置信区间的汇总效应估算值以及确定性分级。如果您能粘贴摘要或结果总结表（summary-of-findings table），我很乐意对其进行批判性评估。

References
1. Wu TD, Fawzy A, Kinney GL, et al. Metformin use and risk of asthma exacerbation among asthma patients with glycemic dysfunction. J Allergy Clin Immunol Pract. 2021;9(11):4014–4020. (根据记忆引用作为说明性观察证据；请在使用前核实卷号/页码。)

总体信心：低 — 我确信 metformin 在预防哮喘急性加重方面没有指南确立的作用，且支持数据均为观察性的，但我无法核实您描述的特定的 2026 年 Cochrane 综述，因此任何对其结果的报告都将是伪造的。I cannot verify the existence, scope, or findings of a Cochrane Database 2026 systematic review on metformin for asthma exacerbations. A 2026 publication would post-date my reliable knowledge cutoff, and I will not fabricate pooled risk ratios, subgroup estimates, GRADE ratings, authors, or a DOI/PMID for a review I cannot confirm.

What I can say from verifiable parametric knowledge:
Metformin has no established, guideline-endorsed role for preventing asthma exacerbations. It is not recommended as asthma therapy in GINA strategy documents as of my knowledge cutoff. The interest is biologically plausible—metformin's AMPK activation, anti-inflammatory effects, and improvement of metabolic dysfunction have been proposed to benefit the obese/metabolic asthma phenotype, which is associated with worse control and steroid resistance.

The strongest existing evidence is observational, not randomized. A frequently cited UK cohort/pharmaco-epidemiologic analysis (Wu and colleagues, using a primary-care database) reported that metformin use was associated with reduced asthma exacerbations and asthma-related hospitalizations among patients with diabetes, with stronger signals in obese subgroups [1]. Such studies are hypothesis-generating and subject to confounding by indication and immortal-time bias; they do not establish efficacy. To my knowledge, no adequately powered randomized controlled trial has confirmed that metformin reduces exacerbations in persistent asthma, and I am not aware of a Cochrane review synthesizing such trials.

If a 2026 Cochrane review now exists, I cannot characterize its RR for OCS-requiring exacerbations, hospitalization, obesity/metabolic-syndrome subgroups, or GRADE certainty without inventing numbers. I would recommend retrieving the actual review from the Cochrane Library to verify the included trials, comparator, pooled effect estimates with confidence intervals, and certainty ratings. I'm glad to appraise it critically if you paste the abstract or summary-of-findings table.

References
1. Wu TD, Fawzy A, Kinney GL, et al. Metformin use and risk of asthma exacerbation among asthma patients with glycemic dysfunction. J Allergy Clin Immunol Pract. 2021;9(11):4014–4020. (Cited from memory as illustrative observational evidence; please verify volume/pages before use.)

Overall confidence: Low — I am confident metformin has no guideline-established role in asthma exacerbation prevention and that the supporting data are observational, but I cannot verify the specific 2026 Cochrane review you describe, so any report of its findings would be fabrication.

✅ 临床落地(基于本案 Anchor 校正后,你的临床决策应这样做)✅ Clinical take-home (after Anchor correction, your clinical decision should be)

  • 不要被 DS 编的「RR 0.92 + PMID 36706779」误导 — KB 真正顶部证据是 Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA(PMID 41100655,n=317,905):调整后哮喘加重 HR = 0.95 [0.86, 1.04] — null effect。临床含义:metformin 不显著影响哮喘加重风险。
  • 关键人群限定:Indian MA 限定 P = T2D + asthma 共病。不要外推到 non-diabetic asthma(没有适应证)。若患者本已用 metformin 治糖尿病,可继续(糖尿病适应证不变),但不应期待哮喘获益。
  • Cochrane 2026 PMID 41631535 是 PROTOCOL — 规划中的 SR,目前无结果。DS 把 protocol 当 completed SR 引,是 evidence-quality 错误。等 Cochrane 完成后(可能 2027-2028 出结果),应再评估。
  • 当前金标准 add-on 仍为:LAMA(tiotropium TALC PMID 22938706)/ 生物制剂(mepolizumab MENSA PMID 25199059,严重嗜酸性表型)/ azithromycin(AMAZES PMID 28687413,非嗜酸性表型);metformin 不在 add-on 路径。
  • Do NOT be misled by DS's fabricated 'RR 0.92 + PMID 36706779' — the KB true top evidence is Indian J Med Res 2025 SR/MA (PMID 41100655, n=317,905): adjusted asthma-exacerbation HR = 0.95 [0.86, 1.04] — null effect. Clinical implication: metformin does NOT significantly affect asthma exacerbation risk.
  • Key population restriction: the Indian MA restricts P = T2D + asthma comorbidity. Do NOT extrapolate to non-diabetic asthma (no indication). If a patient is already on metformin for diabetes, continue (diabetes indication unchanged); but do NOT expect asthma benefit.
  • Cochrane 2026 PMID 41631535 is a PROTOCOL — a planned SR with no results yet. DS citing the protocol as a completed SR is an evidence-quality error. Re-assess once Cochrane completes (likely 2027-2028 results).
  • Current gold-standard add-ons remain: LAMA (tiotropium TALC PMID 22938706) / biologics (mepolizumab MENSA PMID 25199059, severe eosinophilic phenotype) / azithromycin (AMAZES PMID 28687413, non-eosinophilic phenotype); metformin is NOT in the add-on pathway.

🤖 DeepSeek v4 Pro · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 对于患有中至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停 (OSA) 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，持续正压通气 (CPAP) 是否能减少主要不良心血管事件 (MACE：心血管死亡、MI、卒中或因心力衰竭住院)？请提供效应量 (HR 及 95% CI)、GRADE 确定性以及任何获益更明显的亚组。

---

对于患有中至重度 OSA 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，CPAP 总体上**不能**显著降低 MACE，但在 CPAP 依从性较高的人群中可能存在获益。

里程碑式的 **SAVE trial** (2016) 将 2,717 名患有中至重度 OSA 且已有 CVD 的患者随机分为 CPAP 联合常规护理组与单纯常规护理组。在平均 3.7 年的随访期间，主要复合终点（心血管死亡、MI、卒中或因心力衰竭、急性冠脉综合征或 TIA 住院）在 CPAP 组中的发生率为 17.0%，而对照组为 15.4% (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]。对于更窄的 MACE 定义（心血管死亡、MI、卒中），HR 为 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]。**GRADE 确定性：中等** —— 因精确度不足（CI 较宽且跨越 1）和间接性（依从性低）而下调。

**获益更明显的亚组**：在针对每晚使用 CPAP ≥4 小时（平均 5.3 小时/晚）患者的预设符合方案分析中，主要结局有所降低 (HR 0.80; 95% CI 0.60–1.01; p=0.06)，且卒中风险显著降低 (HR 0.56; 95% CI 0.32–1.00; p=0.05) [1]。随后一项包含 4 项 RCT (n=3,786) 的荟萃分析证实，总体上 MACE 没有显著降低 (RR 0.95; 95% CI 0.80–1.13)，但在每晚依从性 ≥4 小时的患者中，MACE 风险有所降低 (RR 0.70; 95% CI 0.52–0.94) [2]。

**结论**：在意向治疗分析中，CPAP 不能降低 MACE，但依从性良好的使用（≥4 小时/晚）可能会降低卒中和复合心血管事件的风险。

**参考文献**
1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
2. Khan SU, Duran CA, Rahman H, et al. A meta-analysis of continuous positive airway pressure therapy in prevention of cardiovascular events in patients with obstructive sleep apnoea. Eur Heart J. 2018;39(24):2291-2297. doi:10.1093/eurheartj/ehx597

总体信心：**中等** —— 基于一项大型 RCT (SAVE) 及一致的荟萃分析支持，但受限于主要分析中的低依从性和精确度不足。In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, CPAP does **not** significantly reduce MACE overall, though a benefit may exist in those with higher CPAP adherence.

The landmark **SAVE trial** (2016) randomized 2,717 patients with moderate-to-severe OSA and established CVD to CPAP plus usual care vs. usual care alone. Over a mean follow-up of 3.7 years, the primary composite endpoint (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for heart failure, acute coronary syndrome, or TIA) occurred in 17.0% of the CPAP group vs. 15.4% of controls (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]. For the narrower MACE definition (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke), the HR was 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]. **GRADE certainty: Moderate** — downgraded for imprecision (wide CI crossing 1) and indirectness (low adherence).

**Subgroup with clearer benefit**: In a pre-specified per-protocol analysis of patients using CPAP ≥4 hours/night (mean 5.3 h/night), the primary outcome was reduced (HR 0.80; 95% CI 0.60–1.01; p=0.06), and stroke risk was significantly lower (HR 0.56; 95% CI 0.32–1.00; p=0.05) [1]. A subsequent meta-analysis of 4 RCTs (n=3,786) confirmed no significant MACE reduction overall (RR 0.95; 95% CI 0.80–1.13), but in patients with ≥4 h/night adherence, MACE risk was reduced (RR 0.70; 95% CI 0.52–0.94) [2].

**Conclusion**: CPAP does not reduce MACE in intention-to-treat analysis, but adherent use (≥4 h/night) may lower stroke and composite cardiovascular events.

**References**
1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
2. Khan SU, Duran CA, Rahman H, et al. A meta-analysis of continuous positive airway pressure therapy in prevention of cardiovascular events in patients with obstructive sleep apnoea. Eur Heart J. 2018;39(24):2291-2297. doi:10.1093/eurheartj/ehx597

Overall confidence: **Moderate** — based on one large RCT (SAVE) with consistent meta-analytic support, but limited by low adherence and imprecision in the primary analysis.

对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,持续气道正压(CPAP)在总体意向治疗(ITT)分析中不显著降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中、心衰住院)。

SAVE 试验(2016)纳入 2717 例中重度 OSA 伴已确诊 CVD 患者,随机分配至 CPAP 联合常规治疗 vs 常规治疗,中位随访 3.7 年,主要复合终点(心血管死亡、心梗、卒中、心衰住院、急性冠脉综合征或 TIA)发生率 CPAP 组 17.0% vs 对照组 15.4%(HR 1.10;95% CI 0.91–1.32;p=0.34)[1]。对于更窄的 MACE 定义(心血管死亡、心梗、卒中),HR 为 0.90(95% CI 0.69–1.17)[1]。更新的 Sánchez-de-la-Torre JAMA 2023 个体患者数据(IPD)meta 分析(3 项 RCT,n=4186)确认 ITT 无获益:HR 1.01(95% CI 0.87–1.17)[2]。Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 多试验分析(n=3549)进一步发现,在 OSA 高风险亚组(高缺氧负担)中 CPAP 可能降低 MACE(交互 HR 0.69;95% CI 0.50–0.95);但低风险 OSA 患者中 CPAP 可能有害[3]。临床警示:在低风险 OSA 患者中 CPAP 可能有害,不应给所有 OSA 患者常规开具以预防 MACE。

安全性方面,CPAP 总体耐受良好,常见不良反应包括鼻部刺激、口干、面罩不适等,但无严重治疗相关不良事件报告[1]。Cochrane 2019 系统评价(n=2125)显示全因死亡率无显著差异(HR 0.81;95% CI 0.54–1.21)[4]。

总结:CPAP 在 ITT 分析中不降低 MACE,但依从性≥4 小时/晚的患者可能获益(HR 0.69;95% CI 0.52–0.92)[2];OSA 高风险亚组(高缺氧负担)获益更明确[3]。临床决策应基于患者 OSA 风险分层和 CPAP 依从性,而非常规开具。In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not significantly reduce major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, hospitalization for heart failure) in the overall intention-to-treat (ITT) analysis.

The landmark SAVE trial (2016) randomized 2,717 patients with moderate-to-severe OSA and established CVD to CPAP plus usual care vs. usual care alone. Over a mean follow-up of 3.7 years, the primary composite endpoint (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke, hospitalization for heart failure, acute coronary syndrome, or TIA) occurred in 17.0% of the CPAP group vs. 15.4% of controls (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]. For the narrower MACE definition (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke), the HR was 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]. An updated individual patient data (IPD) meta-analysis by Sánchez-de-la-Torre JAMA 2023 (3 RCTs, n=4,186) confirmed no ITT benefit: HR 1.01 (95% CI 0.87–1.17) [2]. A multi-trial analysis by Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 (n=3,549) further identified a high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) where CPAP may reduce MACE (interaction HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.50–0.95); however, CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA [3]. Clinical caveat: CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA; do not prescribe routinely to all OSA patients for MACE prevention.

Regarding safety, CPAP is generally well tolerated; common adverse effects include nasal irritation, dry mouth, and mask discomfort, with no serious treatment-related adverse events reported [1]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2,125) showed no significant difference in all-cause mortality (HR 0.81; 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [4].

In summary, CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analysis, but adherent use (≥4 hours/night) may confer benefit (HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92) [2]; the benefit is clearer in the high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) [3]. Clinical decisions should be guided by patient OSA risk stratification and CPAP adherence, rather than routine prescription.

对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,持续气道正压(CPAP)在总体意向治疗(ITT)分析中不显著降低重大不良心血管事件(MACE:心血管死亡、心肌梗死、卒中、心衰住院)。

SAVE 试验(2016)纳入 2717 例中重度 OSA 伴已确诊 CVD 患者,随机分配至 CPAP 联合常规治疗 vs 常规治疗,中位随访 3.7 年,主要复合终点(心血管死亡、心梗、卒中、心衰住院、急性冠脉综合征或 TIA)发生率 CPAP 组 17.0% vs 对照组 15.4%(HR 1.10;95% CI 0.91–1.32;p=0.34)[1]。对于更窄的 MACE 定义(心血管死亡、心梗、卒中),HR 为 0.90(95% CI 0.69–1.17)[1]。更新的 Sánchez-de-la-Torre JAMA 2023 个体患者数据(IPD)meta 分析(3 项 RCT,n=4186)确认 ITT 无获益:HR 1.01(95% CI 0.87–1.17)[2]。Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 多试验分析(n=3549)进一步发现,在 OSA 高风险亚组(高缺氧负担)中 CPAP 可能降低 MACE(交互 HR 0.69;95% CI 0.50–0.95);但低风险 OSA 患者中 CPAP 可能有害[3]。(临床警示:在低风险 OSA 患者中 CPAP 可能有害,不应给所有 OSA 患者常规开具以预防 MACE。)

安全性方面,CPAP 总体耐受良好,常见不良反应包括鼻部刺激、口干、面罩不适等,但无严重治疗相关不良事件报告[1]。Cochrane 2019 系统评价(n=2125)显示全因死亡率无显著差异(HR 0.81;95% CI 0.54–1.21)[4]。

总结:CPAP 在 ITT 分析中不降低 MACE,但依从性≥4 小时/晚的患者可能获益(HR 0.69;95% CI 0.52–0.92)[2];OSA 高风险亚组(高缺氧负担)获益更明确[3]。临床决策应基于患者 OSA 风险分层和 CPAP 依从性,而非常规开具。In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), continuous positive airway pressure (CPAP) does not significantly reduce major adverse cardiovascular events (MACE: cardiovascular death, myocardial infarction, stroke, hospitalization for heart failure) in the overall intention-to-treat (ITT) analysis.

The landmark SAVE trial (2016) randomized 2,717 patients with moderate-to-severe OSA and established CVD to CPAP plus usual care vs. usual care alone. Over a mean follow-up of 3.7 years, the primary composite endpoint (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke, hospitalization for heart failure, acute coronary syndrome, or TIA) occurred in 17.0% of the CPAP group vs. 15.4% of controls (HR 1.10; 95% CI 0.91–1.32; p=0.34) [1]. For the narrower MACE definition (cardiovascular death, MI, stroke), the HR was 0.90 (95% CI 0.69–1.17) [1]. An updated individual patient data (IPD) meta-analysis by Sánchez-de-la-Torre JAMA 2023 (3 RCTs, n=4,186) confirmed no ITT benefit: HR 1.01 (95% CI 0.87–1.17) [2]. A multi-trial analysis by Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 (n=3,549) further identified a high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) where CPAP may reduce MACE (interaction HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.50–0.95); however, CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA [3]. (Clinical caveat: CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA; do not prescribe routinely to all OSA patients for MACE prevention.)

Regarding safety, CPAP is generally well tolerated; common adverse effects include nasal irritation, dry mouth, and mask discomfort, with no serious treatment-related adverse events reported [1]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2,125) showed no significant difference in all-cause mortality (HR 0.81; 95% CI 0.54–1.21) [4].

In summary, CPAP does not reduce MACE in ITT analysis, but adherent use (≥4 hours/night) may confer benefit (HR 0.69; 95% CI 0.52–0.92) [2]; the benefit is clearer in the high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) [3]. Clinical decisions should be guided by patient OSA risk stratification and CPAP adherence, rather than routine prescription.

💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • Anchor 校正将 LLM 引用的 2018 年 meta 分析更新为 2023 年 IPD meta 分析(样本量更大、终点更精确)。
  • 新增高风险 OSA 亚组(高缺氧负担)可能获益,低风险 OSA 可能有害的临床警示。
  • ITT 分析无获益的结论不变,但依从性亚组获益证据更精确(HR 0.69 vs RR 0.70)。
  • Anchor correction updates the LLM's 2018 meta-analysis to the 2023 IPD meta-analysis (larger sample, more precise endpoints).
  • Adds high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) may benefit, with a clinical caveat that CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA.
  • ITT analysis of no benefit remains unchanged, but adherent subgroup benefit is more precisely estimated (HR 0.69 vs RR 0.70).
🤖 OpenEvidence · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
# O · OpenEvidence · OSA · CPAP 用于心血管事件

**问题：** 对于患有中至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停 (OSA) 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，持续正压通气 (CPAP) 是否能减少主要不良心血管事件 (MACE：心血管死亡、MI、卒中或心力衰竭住院)？请提供效应量 (HR 及 95% CI)、GRADE 一致性以及任何获益更明显的亚组。

---

在意向治疗分析中，CPAP 未能降低患有中至重度 OSA 且已有心血管疾病成年人的 MACE 风险。然而，依从的 CPAP 使用（≥4 小时/天）与 MACE 的显著降低相关。[5]

**效应量：**

意向治疗分析 (3 项汇总 RCT, n=4,186)：CPAP 对 MACE 没有影响 (HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87-1.17)。[5] 最大规模的试验 (SAVE, n=2,687) 同样未发现获益：HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91-1.32)。[6]

基于依从性的分析 (治疗中分析，边际结构模型)：CPAP 使用 ≥4 小时/天与 MACE 降低 31% 相关 (HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52-0.92)。[5] 一项独立的荟萃分析 (11 项研究, n=5,410) 证实，依从的 CPAP (≥4 小时/夜) 使 MACE 降低了 42% (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42-0.79)，全因死亡率降低了 23% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60-0.99)。[7]

**获益更明显的亚组：**

高风险 OSA（定义为心率反应 >9.4 bpm 或缺氧负荷 >87.1% min/h）：CPAP 在高风险 OSA 患者中显示出优先获益 (交互 HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50-0.95, p=0.024)。[8] 该效应在没有过度嗜睡 (Epworth <11; 交互 HR 0.59, 95% CI 0.41-0.84) 或没有高血压 (BP <140/90 mmHg; 交互 HR 0.54, 95% CI 0.36-0.81) 的患者中更为强烈。[8] 值得注意的是，CPAP 在低风险 OSA 患者中似乎有害。[8]

**GRADE 一致性：**

意向治疗 (无获益)：中等 certainty。基于 3 项结果一致的大规模 RCT，但受限于依从性差（平均 3.1 小时/天）。[5]

基于依从性的获益：低 certainty。源自事后治疗中分析和观察性数据；依从性并非随机分配，尽管进行了统计学调整，但仍存在混杂风险。[5]

**参考文献：**

5. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP Treatment and the Risk of Recurrent Cardiovascular Events: A Meta-Analysis. The Journal of the American Medical Association. 2023. (SR)
6. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. The New England Journal of Medicine. 2016. (RCT)
7. Yang D, Li L, Dong J, Yang W, Liu Z. Effects of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure on Cardiac Events and Metabolic Components in Patients With Moderate to Severe Obstructive Sleep Apnea and Coronary Artery Disease: A Meta-Analysis. Journal of Clinical Sleep Medicine. 2023. (SR)
8. Azarbarzin A, Vena D, Esmaeili N, et al. Cardiovascular Benefit of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure According to High-Risk Obstructive Sleep Apnoea: A Multi-Trial Analysis. European Heart Journal. 2026. (NewRCT)In intention-to-treat analyses, CPAP does not reduce MACE in adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease. However, adherent CPAP use (≥4 hours/day) is associated with significant MACE reduction. [5]

**Effect Sizes:**

Intention-to-treat analysis (3 pooled RCTs, n=4,186): CPAP showed no effect on MACE (HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87-1.17). [5] The largest trial (SAVE, n=2,687) similarly found no benefit: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91-1.32). [6]

Adherence-based analysis (on-treatment, marginal structural model): CPAP use ≥4 hours/day was associated with 31% reduction in MACE (HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52-0.92). [5] A separate meta-analysis (11 studies, n=5,410) confirmed adherent CPAP (≥4 hours/night) reduced MACE by 42% (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42-0.79) and all-cause death by 23% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60-0.99). [7]

**Subgroup With Clearer Benefit:**

High-risk OSA (defined by heart rate response >9.4 bpm or hypoxic burden >87.1% min/h): CPAP showed preferential benefit in high-risk OSA (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50-0.95, p=0.024). [8] The effect was stronger in patients without excessive sleepiness (Epworth <11; interaction HR 0.59, 95% CI 0.41-0.84) or without hypertension (BP <140/90 mmHg; interaction HR 0.54, 95% CI 0.36-0.81). [8] Notably, CPAP appeared harmful in low-risk OSA. [8]

**GRADE Certainty:**

Moderate certainty for intention-to-treat (no benefit): Based on 3 large RCTs with consistent findings but limited by poor adherence (mean 3.1 hours/day). [5]

Low certainty for adherence-based benefit: Derived from post hoc on-treatment analyses and observational data; adherence is not randomized, introducing confounding risk despite statistical adjustment. [5]

**References:**

5. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP Treatment and the Risk of Recurrent Cardiovascular Events: A Meta-Analysis. The Journal of the American Medical Association. 2023. (SR)
6. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for Prevention of Cardiovascular Events in Obstructive Sleep Apnea. The New England Journal of Medicine. 2016. (RCT)
7. Yang D, Li L, Dong J, Yang W, Liu Z. Effects of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure on Cardiac Events and Metabolic Components in Patients With Moderate to Severe Obstructive Sleep Apnea and Coronary Artery Disease: A Meta-Analysis. Journal of Clinical Sleep Medicine. 2023. (SR)
8. Azarbarzin A, Vena D, Esmaeili N, et al. Cardiovascular Benefit of Continuous Positive Airway Pressure According to High-Risk Obstructive Sleep Apnoea: A Multi-Trial Analysis. European Heart Journal. 2026. (NewRCT)

在按意向性治疗(ITT)分析中,CPAP 未能降低中重度 OSA 伴已确诊 CVD 成人的 MACE。然而,依从性好的 CPAP 使用(≥4 小时/天)与 MACE 显著降低相关 [5]。

ITT 分析(3 项 RCT 的 IPD meta 分析,n=4,186;对照均为常规治疗/不戴 CPAP):CPAP 对 MACE 无影响(HR 1.01,95% CI 0.87–1.17)[5]。最大型试验 SAVE(n=2,687)同样未发现获益:HR 1.10(95% CI 0.91–1.32)[6]。依从性分析(边际结构模型):CPAP 使用≥4 小时/天与 MACE 降低 31% 相关(HR 0.69,95% CI 0.52–0.92)[5]。另一项 meta 分析(11 项研究,n=5,410)确认依从性好的 CPAP(≥4 小时/晚)可降低 MACE 42%(RR 0.58,95% CI 0.42–0.79)和全因死亡 23%(RR 0.77,95% CI 0.60–0.99)[7]。

获益更明确的亚组:高风险 OSA(定义为心率反应>9.4 bpm 或缺氧负担>87.1% min/h)患者中,CPAP 显示出优先获益(交互作用 HR 0.69,95% CI 0.50–0.95,p=0.024)[8]。在无过度嗜睡(Epworth<11;交互作用 HR 0.59,95% CI 0.41–0.84)或无高血压(BP<140/90 mmHg;交互作用 HR 0.54,95% CI 0.36–0.81)的患者中效应更强 [8]。值得注意的是,CPAP 在低风险 OSA 中似乎有害 [8]。

ITT 分析(无获益)为中等质量证据:基于 3 项大型 RCT,结果一致,但受限于依从性差(平均 3.1 小时/天)[5]。依从性获益为低质量证据:源于事后依从性分析和观察性数据;依从性非随机化,尽管经统计调整仍存在混杂风险 [5]。In intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses, CPAP does not reduce MACE in adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease. However, adherent CPAP use (≥4 hours/day) is associated with significant MACE reduction [5].

ITT analysis (IPD meta-analysis of 3 RCTs, n=4,186; comparator = usual care / no CPAP): CPAP showed no effect on MACE (HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17) [5]. The largest trial (SAVE, n=2,687) similarly found no benefit: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) [6]. Adherence-based analysis (marginal structural model): CPAP use ≥4 hours/day was associated with 31% reduction in MACE (HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92) [5]. A separate meta-analysis (11 studies, n=5,410) confirmed adherent CPAP (≥4 hours/night) reduced MACE by 42% (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42–0.79) and all-cause death by 23% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60–0.99) [7].

Subgroup with clearer benefit: High-risk OSA (defined by heart rate response >9.4 bpm or hypoxic burden >87.1% min/h) showed preferential benefit with CPAP (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95, p=0.024) [8]. The effect was stronger in patients without excessive sleepiness (Epworth <11; interaction HR 0.59, 95% CI 0.41–0.84) or without hypertension (BP <140/90 mmHg; interaction HR 0.54, 95% CI 0.36–0.81) [8]. Notably, CPAP appeared harmful in low-risk OSA [8].

GRADE certainty: Moderate certainty for ITT (no benefit) — based on 3 large RCTs with consistent findings but limited by poor adherence (mean 3.1 hours/day) [5]. Low certainty for adherence-based benefit — derived from post hoc on-treatment analyses and observational data; adherence is not randomized, introducing confounding risk despite statistical adjustment [5].

在按意向性治疗(ITT)分析中,CPAP 未能降低中重度 OSA 伴已确诊 CVD 成人的 MACE。然而,依从性好的 CPAP 使用(≥4 小时/天)与 MACE 显著降低相关 [5]。

ITT 分析(3 项 RCT 的 IPD meta 分析,n=4,186;对照均为常规治疗/不戴 CPAP):CPAP 对 MACE 无影响(HR 1.01,95% CI 0.87–1.17)[5]。最大型试验 SAVE(n=2,687)同样未发现获益:HR 1.10(95% CI 0.91–1.32)[6]。依从性分析(边际结构模型):CPAP 使用≥4 小时/天与 MACE 降低 31% 相关(HR 0.69,95% CI 0.52–0.92)[5]。另一项 meta 分析(11 项研究,n=5,410)确认依从性好的 CPAP(≥4 小时/晚)可降低 MACE 42%(RR 0.58,95% CI 0.42–0.79)和全因死亡 23%(RR 0.77,95% CI 0.60–0.99)[7]。

获益更明确的亚组:高风险 OSA(定义为心率反应>9.4 bpm 或缺氧负担>87.1% min/h)患者中,CPAP 显示出优先获益(交互作用 HR 0.69,95% CI 0.50–0.95,p=0.024)[8]。在无过度嗜睡(Epworth<11;交互作用 HR 0.59,95% CI 0.41–0.84)或无高血压(BP<140/90 mmHg;交互作用 HR 0.54,95% CI 0.36–0.81)的患者中效应更强 [8]。值得注意的是,CPAP 在低风险 OSA 中似乎有害 [8]。

ITT 分析(无获益)为中等质量证据:基于 3 项大型 RCT,结果一致,但受限于依从性差(平均 3.1 小时/天)[5]。依从性获益为低质量证据:源于事后依从性分析和观察性数据;依从性非随机化,尽管经统计调整仍存在混杂风险 [5]。In intention-to-treat (ITT) analyses, CPAP does not reduce MACE in adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease. However, adherent CPAP use (≥4 hours/day) is associated with significant MACE reduction [5].

ITT analysis (IPD meta-analysis of 3 RCTs, n=4,186; comparator = usual care / no CPAP): CPAP showed no effect on MACE (HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17) [5]. The largest trial (SAVE, n=2,687) similarly found no benefit: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.32) [6]. Adherence-based analysis (marginal structural model): CPAP use ≥4 hours/day was associated with 31% reduction in MACE (HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92) [5]. A separate meta-analysis (11 studies, n=5,410) confirmed adherent CPAP (≥4 hours/night) reduced MACE by 42% (RR 0.58, 95% CI 0.42–0.79) and all-cause death by 23% (RR 0.77, 95% CI 0.60–0.99) [7].

Subgroup with clearer benefit: High-risk OSA (defined by heart rate response >9.4 bpm or hypoxic burden >87.1% min/h) showed preferential benefit with CPAP (interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95, p=0.024) [8]. The effect was stronger in patients without excessive sleepiness (Epworth <11; interaction HR 0.59, 95% CI 0.41–0.84) or without hypertension (BP <140/90 mmHg; interaction HR 0.54, 95% CI 0.36–0.81) [8]. Notably, CPAP appeared harmful in low-risk OSA [8].

GRADE certainty: Moderate certainty for ITT (no benefit) — based on 3 large RCTs with consistent findings but limited by poor adherence (mean 3.1 hours/day) [5]. Low certainty for adherence-based benefit — derived from post hoc on-treatment analyses and observational data; adherence is not randomized, introducing confounding risk despite statistical adjustment [5].

# O · ChatGPT 5.5 · OSA · CPAP 用于心血管事件

**问题：** 对于患有中至重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停 (OSA) 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，持续正压通气 (CPAP) 是否能减少主要不良心血管事件 (MACE：心血管死亡、MI、卒中或心力衰竭住院)？请提供效应量 (HR 及 95% CI)、GRADE 确定性以及任何获益更明显的亚组。

---

对于患有中至重度 OSA 且已有心血管疾病的成年人，不应仅为了减少 MACE 而开具 CPAP 处方，因为随机意向治疗证据显示心血管事件并未明显减少，尽管 CPAP 对于改善嗜睡、提高生活质量、控制血压和 OSA 症状仍然是合适的 [1–4]。

规模最大的 RCT 研究 SAVE 测试了在 2717 名主要无嗜睡症状且患有中重度 OSA 及冠状动脉/脑血管疾病的患者中，CPAP 联合常规护理与单纯常规护理的效果。CPAP 未能降低复合心血管终点：HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32；平均依从性仅为 3.3 小时/夜；GRADE：对于无显著 MACE 获益的结论为中等，因依从性问题及排除了极度嗜睡患者而下调 [1]。

另外两项二级预防 RCT 研究结果一致。在 RICCADSA 中，针对经血运重建的冠心病且无嗜睡症状的 OSA 患者，CPAP 在意向治疗分析中未显著降低原发性心血管复合终点：HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41；GRADE：由于样本量较小且精确度不足而定为低 [2]。在 ISAACC 中，急性冠脉综合征后的 CPAP 未能减少复发性心血管事件：HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17；GRADE：中等 [3]。

最可能的获益出现在实际使用 CPAP ≥4 小时/夜的患者中，但由于依从性是在随机化后确定的，因此该结论的可靠性较低。一项 2023 年针对 SAVE、RICCADSA 和 ISAACC 的个体参与者数据荟萃分析发现，在意向治疗分析中没有获益：HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17，但在依从用户中复发性 MACCE 较少：HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92；GRADE：对于依从亚组定为低，因为尽管进行了调整分析，但仍存在健康依从者偏差/混杂因素的担忧 [4]。

RCT 研究尚未证明其在降低死亡率方面的获益；这些试验在心血管死亡方面的统计效能不足且依从性一般。因此，我会针对有症状或具有生理重要性的 OSA 提供 CPAP 治疗，并告知患者减少心血管事件的效果尚不确定，且可能取决于持续的每晚使用。

**参考文献**

1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599.
2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, Wegscheider K, Herlitz J, Thunström E. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea: the RICCADSA randomized controlled trial. Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613-620.
3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with continuous positive airway pressure on cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome: the ISAACC study. Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359-367.
4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP treatment and the risk of recurrent cardiovascular events: a meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255-1265.

**总体信心：** 中等 —— 多项 RCT 一致显示意向治疗下 MACE 未减少，但依从性一般，且 ≥4 小时/夜亚组的获益虽具有合理性但存在混杂因素。In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, CPAP should not be prescribed solely to reduce MACE, because randomized intention-to-treat evidence shows no clear cardiovascular event reduction, although it remains appropriate for sleepiness, quality of life, blood pressure, and OSA symptom control [1–4].

The largest RCT, SAVE, tested CPAP plus usual care vs usual care in 2717 mostly nonsleepy patients with moderate–severe OSA and coronary/cerebrovascular disease. CPAP did not reduce the composite cardiovascular endpoint: HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32; mean adherence was only 3.3 h/night; GRADE: moderate for no large MACE benefit, downgraded for adherence and exclusion of very sleepy patients [1].

Two other secondary-prevention RCTs are consistent. In RICCADSA, CPAP in revascularized coronary artery disease with nonsleepy OSA did not significantly reduce the primary cardiovascular composite by intention-to-treat: HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41; GRADE: low because of smaller sample size and imprecision [2]. In ISAACC, CPAP after acute coronary syndrome did not reduce recurrent cardiovascular events: HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17; GRADE: moderate [3].

The clearest possible benefit is in patients who actually use CPAP ≥4 h/night, but this is less secure because adherence is post-randomization. A 2023 individual-participant-data meta-analysis of SAVE, RICCADSA, and ISAACC found no benefit in intention-to-treat analysis: HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17, but fewer recurrent MACCE among adherent users: HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92; GRADE: low for the adherence subgroup because of healthy-adherer/confounding concerns despite adjusted analyses [4].

Mortality benefit is unproven in RCTs; trials were underpowered for cardiovascular death and had modest adherence. Therefore, I would offer CPAP for symptomatic or physiologically important OSA, and discuss that cardiovascular event reduction is uncertain and probably depends on sustained nightly use.

**References**

1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919-931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599.
2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, Wegscheider K, Herlitz J, Thunström E. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea: the RICCADSA randomized controlled trial. Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613-620.
3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with continuous positive airway pressure on cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome: the ISAACC study. Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359-367.
4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP treatment and the risk of recurrent cardiovascular events: a meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255-1265.

**Overall confidence:** Moderate — multiple RCTs consistently show no intention-to-treat MACE reduction, but adherence is modest and the ≥4 h/night subgroup benefit is plausible but confounded.

图例(Word 修订风格):Legend (Word track-changes style): Anchor 删除 / LLM 原文错处Anchor-deleted / LLM original wrong Anchor 新增 / 替换内容Anchor-added / replacement （旁白 · 切题外插入）(aside · tangential) （证据旁注 · 无 MID/GRADE 等）(evidence caveat · no MID/GRADE …) 未标 = LLM 原话保留(minimal-diff 自证)unmarked = kept verbatim from LLM (proves minimal-diff)
对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,CPAP(持续气道正压通气)不应仅为了降低MACE(重大不良心血管事件)而处方,因为随机意向治疗(ITT)分析证据显示无明确心血管事件减少,但CPAP仍适用于嗜睡、生活质量、血压和OSA症状控制 [1–4]。

最大规模RCT(随机对照试验)SAVE在2717例主要为非嗜睡的中重度OSA合并冠脉/脑血管疾病患者中,CPAP联合常规治疗 vs 常规治疗未降低复合心血管终点:HR 1.10,95% CI 0.91–1.32;平均依从性仅3.3小时/晚;单一RCT不评GRADE,应评Cochrane RoB-2偏倚风险(本试验RoB-2:低偏倚风险) [1]。另两项二级预防RCT结果一致:RICCADSA中CPAP在血运重建后冠心病伴非嗜睡OSA患者中ITT未显著降低主要心血管复合终点:HR 0.80,95% CI 0.46–1.41 [2];ISAACC中急性冠脉综合征后CPAP未减少复发心血管事件:HR 0.89,95% CI 0.68–1.17 [3]。2023年个体参与者数据meta分析(IPD MA,纳入SAVE、RICCADSA、ISAACC,n=4186)显示ITT无获益:HR 1.01,95% CI 0.87–1.17;但在依从≥4小时/晚的亚组中复发MACCE(重大不良心脑血管事件)减少:HR 0.69,95% CI 0.52–0.92;ITT分析为中等质量证据,依从亚组为低质量证据(因健康依从者偏倚/混杂) [4]。Azarbarzin EHJ 2026多试验NMA(网状meta分析)(n=3549)进一步显示,在高风险OSA(高缺氧负担)亚组中CPAP降低MACE:交互作用HR 0.69,95% CI 0.50–0.95;而低风险OSA患者可能有害 [5]。临床警示:在低风险OSA患者中CPAP可能有害,不应给所有OSA患者处方以预防心血管事件

CPAP安全性良好,主要不良事件为面罩不适、皮肤压伤、鼻部干燥或充血等局部问题,严重不良事件罕见。SAVE试验中CPAP组与对照组严重不良事件发生率无差异 [1]。Cochrane 2019系统评价(n=2125)显示CPAP对全因死亡率无显著影响:HR 0.81,95% CI 0.54–1.21 [6]。

总结:现有证据不支持在ITT分析中CPAP降低中重度OSA伴CVD患者的MACE。但依从性≥4小时/晚的亚组和高风险OSA(高缺氧负担)亚组可能获益。临床决策应基于OSA症状控制,而非单纯心血管事件预防。证据边界:所有RCT依从性均不理想(平均3.3小时/晚),且排除了重度嗜睡患者,限制了ITT分析的效力。In adults with moderate-to-severe obstructive sleep apnea (OSA) and established cardiovascular disease (CVD), CPAP should not be prescribed solely to reduce MACE, because randomized intention-to-treat (ITT) evidence shows no clear cardiovascular event reduction, although it remains appropriate for sleepiness, quality of life, blood pressure, and OSA symptom control [1–4].

The largest RCT, SAVE, tested CPAP plus usual care vs usual care in 2717 mostly nonsleepy patients with moderate–severe OSA and coronary/cerebrovascular disease. CPAP did not reduce the composite cardiovascular endpoint: HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32; mean adherence was only 3.3 h/night; as a single RCT, GRADE is not applicable — Cochrane RoB-2 risk of bias is Low [1]. Two other secondary-prevention RCTs are consistent. In RICCADSA, CPAP in revascularized coronary artery disease with nonsleepy OSA did not significantly reduce the primary cardiovascular composite by ITT: HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41 [2]. In ISAACC, CPAP after acute coronary syndrome did not reduce recurrent cardiovascular events: HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17 [3]. A 2023 individual-participant-data meta-analysis (IPD MA) of SAVE, RICCADSA, and ISAACC (n=4186) found no benefit in ITT analysis: HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17, but fewer recurrent MACCE among adherent users (≥4 h/night): HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92; GRADE: moderate for ITT, low for adherence subgroup due to healthy-adherer/confounding concerns [4]. The Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 multi-trial network meta-analysis (NMA, n=3549) further characterized a high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) where CPAP reduced MACE: interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95; while low-risk OSA patients may experience harm [5]. Clinical caution: CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA; do not prescribe to all OSA patients for cardiovascular prevention

CPAP has a favorable safety profile; main adverse events are mask discomfort, skin pressure injury, nasal dryness or congestion, with rare serious adverse events. In SAVE, serious adverse event rates did not differ between CPAP and control groups [1]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2125) found no significant effect on all-cause mortality: HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.54–1.21 [6].

In summary, current evidence does not support CPAP reducing MACE in moderate-to-severe OSA with established CVD by ITT analysis. However, the ≥4 h/night adherence subgroup and the high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) subgroup may derive benefit. Clinical decisions should prioritize OSA symptom control rather than cardiovascular event prevention alone. Evidence boundary: all RCTs had suboptimal adherence (mean 3.3 h/night) and excluded very sleepy patients, limiting ITT analysis power.

对于中重度阻塞性睡眠呼吸暂停(OSA)伴已确诊心血管疾病(CVD)的成人,CPAP(持续气道正压通气)不应仅为了降低MACE(重大不良心血管事件)而处方,因为随机意向治疗(ITT)分析证据显示无明确心血管事件减少,但CPAP仍适用于嗜睡、生活质量、血压和OSA症状控制 [1–4]。

CPAP安全性良好,主要不良事件为面罩不适、皮肤压伤、鼻部干燥或充血等局部问题,严重不良事件罕见。SAVE试验中CPAP组与对照组严重不良事件发生率无差异 [1]。Cochrane 2019系统评价(n=2125)显示CPAP对全因死亡率无显著影响:HR 0.81,95% CI 0.54–1.21 [6]。

The largest RCT, SAVE, tested CPAP plus usual care vs usual care in 2717 mostly nonsleepy patients with moderate–severe OSA and coronary/cerebrovascular disease. CPAP did not reduce the composite cardiovascular endpoint: HR 1.10, 95% CI 0.91–1.32; mean adherence was only 3.3 h/night; as a single RCT, GRADE is not applicable — Cochrane RoB-2 risk of bias is Low [1]. Two other secondary-prevention RCTs are consistent. In RICCADSA, CPAP in revascularized coronary artery disease with nonsleepy OSA did not significantly reduce the primary cardiovascular composite by ITT: HR 0.80, 95% CI 0.46–1.41 [2]. In ISAACC, CPAP after acute coronary syndrome did not reduce recurrent cardiovascular events: HR 0.89, 95% CI 0.68–1.17 [3]. A 2023 individual-participant-data meta-analysis (IPD MA) of SAVE, RICCADSA, and ISAACC (n=4186) found no benefit in ITT analysis: HR 1.01, 95% CI 0.87–1.17, but fewer recurrent MACCE among adherent users (≥4 h/night): HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.52–0.92; GRADE: moderate for ITT, low for adherence subgroup due to healthy-adherer/confounding concerns [4]. The Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 multi-trial network meta-analysis (NMA, n=3549) further characterized a high-risk OSA subgroup (high hypoxic burden) where CPAP reduced MACE: interaction HR 0.69, 95% CI 0.50–0.95; while low-risk OSA patients may experience harm [5]. (Clinical caution: CPAP may be harmful in low-risk OSA; do not prescribe to all OSA patients for cardiovascular prevention)

CPAP has a favorable safety profile; main adverse events are mask discomfort, skin pressure injury, nasal dryness or congestion, with rare serious adverse events. In SAVE, serious adverse event rates did not differ between CPAP and control groups [1]. A Cochrane 2019 systematic review (n=2125) found no significant effect on all-cause mortality: HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.54–1.21 [6].

However, the ≥4 h/night adherence subgroup and the high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) subgroup may derive benefit. Clinical decisions should prioritize OSA symptom control rather than cardiovascular event prevention alone. Evidence boundary: all RCTs had suboptimal adherence (mean 3.3 h/night) and excluded very sleepy patients, limiting ITT analysis power.

💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • ITT 分析显示 CPAP 不降低中重度 OSA 伴 CVD 患者的 MACE(HR 1.01–1.10,均无统计学意义)
  • 依从性≥4小时/晚的亚组(HR 0.69)和高风险 OSA(高缺氧负担)亚组(交互作用 HR 0.69)可能获益,但均为观察性分析,证据等级低
  • Anchor 校正新增 Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 NMA,揭示高风险 OSA 亚组获益而低风险可能有害,提示需根据 OSA 缺氧负担分层决策
  • Cochrane 2019 系统评价确认 CPAP 不降低全因死亡率(HR 0.81,95% CI 0.54–1.21)
  • ITT analysis shows CPAP does not reduce MACE in moderate-to-severe OSA with CVD (HR 1.01–1.10, all non-significant)
  • The ≥4h/night adherence subgroup (HR 0.69) and high-risk OSA (high hypoxic burden) subgroup (interaction HR 0.69) may benefit, but both are observational analyses with low certainty evidence
  • Anchor correction adds the Azarbarzin EHJ 2026 NMA, revealing benefit in high-risk OSA but potential harm in low-risk OSA, suggesting stratification by hypoxic burden is needed
  • Cochrane 2019 systematic review confirms CPAP does not reduce all-cause mortality (HR 0.81, 95% CI 0.54–1.21)
🤖 Claude 4.7 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
在患有中至重度 OSA 且伴有既往心血管疾病的成人中，随机对照试验显示，在按意向治疗（intention-to-treat）原则开具处方时，CPAP 并不能降低 MACE [1,2,3]。

**主要 RCT 证据**
SAVE 试验（n=2,717；中至重度 OSA 且伴有既往冠心病或脑血管疾病）发现，与仅接受常规护理相比，CPAP 联合常规护理在降低心血管死亡、MI、卒中或因不稳定型心绞痛/HF/TIA 入院的复合终点方面没有显著效果：HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.34) [1]。平均 CPAP 依从性较低（约 3.3 小时/夜）。CPAP 确实改善了打鼾、日间嗜睡、情绪和生活质量 [1]。
RICCADSA 试验（CAD + 非嗜睡型 OSA）在意向治疗分析中显示 MACE 未显著降低，HR 0.80 (95% CI 0.46–1.41) [2]。
ISAACC 试验（急性冠脉综合征 + OSA）同样未发现获益，HR ~0.89 (95% CI 0.68–1.17) [3]。

**获益较为明显的亚组**
在依从性良好的使用者（≥4 小时/夜）中一致出现了相关信号。一项个体患者数据荟萃分析（Sánchez-de-la-Torre 等，JAMA 2023）发现虽然整体无效果，但在治疗分析（on-treatment analysis）中，良好的依从性与 MACE 降低相关——约为 HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) [4]。一项 SAVE 次级（倾向评分匹配）分析同样提示使用时间 ≥4 小时可减少脑血管事件 [1]。这些分析容易受到“健康依从者”偏倚和反向因果关系的影响，因此它们是用于产生假设而非证实结论的。

**实践综合：** CPAP 仍然是症状性 OSA（嗜睡、生活质量）的一线治疗方案，但根据目前的试验证据，不应将其主要用于心血管二级预防。针对高依从性和/或更嗜睡表型是否能产生心血管获益仍是一个开放性问题，将在正在进行或未来的试验中予以探讨。

GRADE 确定性：对于无整体 MACE 获益的结论为 中等-高（多项一致的 RCT，尽管受限于低依从性和排除了极度嗜睡患者）。对于依从性亚组获益的结论为 低（次级/观察性分析，存在混杂因素）。

References
1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919–931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, et al. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea (RICCADSA). Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613–620.
3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with CPAP on the prevalence of cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome (ISAACC). Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359–367.
4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP and cardiovascular outcomes in OSA: an individual patient data meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255–1265.

总体信心：（未明确标注；GRADE：无整体获益为 中等-高，依从性亚组为 低）In adults with moderate-to-severe OSA and established cardiovascular disease, randomized trials do not show that CPAP reduces MACE when prescribed on an intention-to-treat basis [1,2,3].

Primary RCT evidence
The SAVE trial (n=2,717; moderate–severe OSA with established coronary or cerebrovascular disease) found no reduction in the composite of CV death, MI, stroke, or hospitalization for unstable angina/HF/TIA: HR 1.10 (95% CI 0.91–1.34) with CPAP plus usual care versus usual care alone [1]. Mean CPAP adherence was low (~3.3 h/night). CPAP did improve snoring, daytime sleepiness, mood, and quality of life [1].
The RICCADSA trial (CAD + nonsleepy OSA) showed no significant MACE reduction in intention-to-treat analysis, HR 0.80 (95% CI 0.46–1.41) [2].
The ISAACC trial (acute coronary syndrome + OSA) likewise found no benefit, HR ~0.89 (95% CI 0.68–1.17) [3].

Subgroup where benefit appears clearer
The signal consistently emerges in adherent users (≥4 h/night). An individual-patient-data meta-analysis (Sánchez-de-la-Torre et al., JAMA 2023) found no overall effect but, in on-treatment analysis, good adherence was associated with reduced MACE — approximately HR 0.69 (95% CI 0.52–0.92) [4]. A SAVE secondary (propensity-matched) analysis similarly suggested reduced cerebrovascular events with ≥4 h use [1]. These analyses are vulnerable to "healthy adherer" bias and reverse causation, so they are hypothesis-generating rather than confirmatory.

Practical synthesis: CPAP remains first-line for symptomatic OSA (sleepiness, quality of life), but it should not be prescribed primarily for secondary CV prevention based on current trial evidence. Whether targeting highly adherent and/or sleepier phenotypes yields cardiovascular benefit is an open question being addressed in ongoing/future trials.

GRADE certainty: Moderate-to-high for no overall MACE benefit (multiple consistent RCTs, though limited by low adherence and exclusion of very sleepy patients). Low for the adherence-subgroup benefit (secondary/observational, confounded).

References
1. McEvoy RD, Antic NA, Heeley E, et al. CPAP for prevention of cardiovascular events in obstructive sleep apnea. N Engl J Med. 2016;375(10):919–931. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa1606599
2. Peker Y, Glantz H, Eulenburg C, et al. Effect of positive airway pressure on cardiovascular outcomes in coronary artery disease patients with nonsleepy obstructive sleep apnea (RICCADSA). Am J Respir Crit Care Med. 2016;194(5):613–620.
3. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Sánchez-de-la-Torre A, Bertran S, et al. Effect of obstructive sleep apnoea and its treatment with CPAP on the prevalence of cardiovascular events in patients with acute coronary syndrome (ISAACC). Lancet Respir Med. 2020;8(4):359–367.
4. Sánchez-de-la-Torre M, Gracia-Lavedan E, Benitez ID, et al. Adherence to CPAP and cardiovascular outcomes in OSA: an individual patient data meta-analysis. JAMA. 2023;330(13):1255–1265.

Overall confidence: (not explicitly labeled; GRADE: Moderate-to-high for no overall benefit, Low for adherence subgroup)

✅ 临床落地(基于本案 Anchor 校正后,你的临床决策应这样做)✅ Clinical take-home (after Anchor correction, your clinical decision should be)

  • 不要为 OSA + 已知 CVD 患者 routine 推 CPAP 用于 CV 事件预防 —— SAVE 阴性(HR 1.10)+ 3-RCT IPD MA 阴性(HR 1.01,n=4186);ITT 没有 MACE 获益。这是 3 LLM 高一致识别的临床误区。
  • CPAP 仍对 OSA 症状 / sleepiness / QoL / 血压有获益 —— 应继续推荐 CPAP for 症状性 OSA,但不要承诺 CV 事件减少,患者教育时清楚分清「症状管理」vs「CV 事件预防」。
  • 临床细分:High-risk OSA(hypoxia burden + heart-rate response)CPAP 可能有 CV 获益(EHJ 2026 NMA HR 0.69)· 低风险 OSA 可能有害(EHJ 2026)· 应先 stratify 风险再决定 CPAP 适应症。
  • Do NOT routinely prescribe CPAP for OSA + CVD purely for CV event prevention — SAVE null (HR 1.10) + 3-RCT IPD MA null (HR 1.01, n=4186); no ITT MACE benefit. This is a clinical misconception identified by all 3 LLMs.
  • CPAP still benefits OSA symptoms / sleepiness / QoL / BP — continue recommending CPAP for symptomatic OSA, but do NOT promise CV event reduction; clearly separate 'symptom management' from 'CV prevention' in patient counselling.
  • Clinical stratification: High-risk OSA (hypoxia burden + heart-rate response) may have CV benefit on CPAP (EHJ 2026 NMA HR 0.69); low-risk OSA may be harmful (EHJ 2026); stratify risk before CPAP indication.

🤖 DeepSeek v4 Pro · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 在妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇中，接种母体呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗（如 RSVpreF / Abrysvo）对于预防婴儿出生后前 6 个月严重 RSV 下呼吸道疾病的有效性和安全性如何？请包括疫苗效力 (% 及 95% CI)、关键安全性信号以及目前的监管状态。

---

在妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇中，RSVpreF 疫苗 (Abrysvo, 二价融合前 F 蛋白) 可显著降低婴儿出生后前 6 个月内发生严重 RSV 下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) 的风险。

**有效性**
在关键的 3 期 MATISSE 试验 (NCT04424316) 中，针对出生至 6 个月婴儿严重 RSV LRTI（定义为需要医疗干预的低氧血症或呼吸急促）的疫苗效力为 **81.8% (95% CI 40.6–96.3)** [1]。针对任何就医的 RSV LRTI，效力为 **57.1% (95% CI 14.7–79.8)** [1]。该效力在出生后 90 天和 180 天时保持一致。

**安全性**
母体和新生儿的安全性概况良好。在 MATISSE 试验中，疫苗组 (13.8%) 和安慰剂组 (13.5%) 的严重不良事件 (SAEs) 发生率相似 [1]。疫苗组的早产率在数值上较高 (5.7% vs 安慰剂组 4.7%)，但该差异不具有统计学意义 (risk ratio 1.21, 95% CI 0.95–1.55) [1]。未观察到母体高血压疾病或胎儿生长受限的增加。一项事后分析提示极早产 (<34 周) 存在轻微的不平衡（疫苗组 0.8% vs 安慰剂组 0.4%），需进行持续监测 [2]。

**监管状态**
FDA 已于 2023 年 8 月批准 Abrysvo 用于妊娠 32–36 周的母体免疫，以预防婴儿出生后至 6 个月发生 RSV LRTI [3]。EMA 于 2023 年 8 月针对相同适应症予以批准 [4]。美国 CDC/ACIP 建议在 9 月至次年 1 月期间，为妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇接种单剂 RSVpreF (Abrysvo) [5]。

**参考文献**
1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent prefusion F vaccine in pregnancy to prevent RSV illness in infants. N Engl J Med. 2023;388(16):1451-1464. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa2216480
2. Simões EAF, Center KJ, Tita ATN, et al. Preterm birth risk and maternal RSVpreF vaccine. Lancet. 2024;403(10423):331-332. doi:10.1016/S0140-6736(23)02756-8
3. US Food and Drug Administration. FDA approves first vaccine for pregnant individuals to prevent RSV in infants. August 21, 2023.
4. European Medicines Agency. Abrysvo summary of product characteristics. August 2023.
5. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. ACIP recommendations for RSV vaccination in pregnancy. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023;72(41):1115-1122.

总体信心：**高** — 基于一项大规模、设计严谨的 3 期 RCT (MATISSE)，其效力评估一致，已获得监管批准并有持续的安全性监测；早产信号较小且无统计学意义，但值得继续监测。In pregnant women at 32–36 weeks gestation, the RSVpreF vaccine (Abrysvo, bivalent prefusion F protein) significantly reduces severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness (LRTI) in infants within the first 6 months of life.

**Efficacy**
In the pivotal phase 3 MATISSE trial (NCT04424316), vaccine efficacy against severe RSV LRTI (defined as hypoxia or tachypnea requiring medical intervention) in infants from birth to 6 months was **81.8% (95% CI 40.6–96.3)** [1]. Against any medically attended RSV LRTI, efficacy was **57.1% (95% CI 14.7–79.8)** [1]. Efficacy was consistent across 90 days and 180 days post-birth.

**Safety**
Maternal and neonatal safety profiles were favorable. In MATISSE, rates of serious adverse events (SAEs) were similar between vaccine (13.8%) and placebo (13.5%) groups [1]. Preterm birth rates were numerically higher in the vaccine arm (5.7% vs 4.7% placebo), but this difference was not statistically significant (risk ratio 1.21, 95% CI 0.95–1.55) [1]. No increase in maternal hypertensive disorders or fetal growth restriction was observed. A post-hoc analysis suggested a slight imbalance in very preterm births (<34 weeks) with vaccine (0.8% vs 0.4%), warranting ongoing surveillance [2].

**Regulatory Status**
Abrysvo is approved by the FDA (August 2023) for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks gestation to prevent RSV LRTI in infants from birth to 6 months [3]. EMA approval followed in August 2023 for the same indication [4]. CDC/ACIP recommends a single dose of RSVpreF (Abrysvo) for pregnant persons at 32–36 weeks gestation during September–January in the US [5].

**References**
1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent prefusion F vaccine in pregnancy to prevent RSV illness in infants. N Engl J Med. 2023;388(16):1451-1464. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa2216480
2. Simões EAF, Center KJ, Tita ATN, et al. Preterm birth risk and maternal RSVpreF vaccine. Lancet. 2024;403(10423):331-332. doi:10.1016/S0140-6736(23)02756-8
3. US Food and Drug Administration. FDA approves first vaccine for pregnant individuals to prevent RSV in infants. August 21, 2023.
4. European Medicines Agency. Abrysvo summary of product characteristics. August 2023.
5. Centers for Disease Control and Prevention. ACIP recommendations for RSV vaccination in pregnancy. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023;72(41):1115-1122.

Overall confidence: **High** — based on a large, well-conducted phase 3 RCT (MATISSE) with consistent efficacy estimates, regulatory approvals, and ongoing safety surveillance; the preterm birth signal is small and not statistically significant but warrants continued monitoring.

对于妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇，母体 RSVpreF 疫苗（Abrysvo，双价融合前 F 蛋白）可显著降低婴儿出生后 6 个月内严重 RSV 下呼吸道感染（LRTI）的发生。

有效性
在关键性 3 期 MATISSE 试验（NCT04424316）中，疫苗对婴儿出生至 6 个月内严重 RSV LRTI（定义为需要医疗干预的低氧血症或呼吸急促）的疫苗效力（VE）为 81.8%（95% CI 40.6–96.3）[1]。对任何需要医疗干预的 RSV LRTI，VE 为 57.1%（95% CI 14.7–79.8）[1]。MATISSE 最终分析（Ob/Gyn 2025，n=7420）显示，婴儿 180 天内严重 MA-RSV-LRTI 的 VE 为 70.0%（50.6–82.5）[2]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价（n=17991）汇总显示，婴儿 RSV 住院风险降低（HR 0.50，95% CI 0.31–0.82），严重 LRTI 90 天 VE 为 74%（44–88），评为高质量证据 [3]。

安全性
母体和新生儿安全性良好。MATISSE 中，疫苗组（13.8%）和安慰剂组（13.5%）的严重不良事件（SAE）发生率相似 [1]。疫苗组早产率数值略高（5.7% vs 4.7%），但差异无统计学意义（风险比 1.21，95% CI 0.95–1.55）[1]。未观察到母体高血压疾病或胎儿生长受限增加。事后分析提示极早产（<34 周）存在轻微不平衡（0.8% vs 0.4%），需持续监测 [4]。作为对照，GSK 的 RSVPreF3-Mat 疫苗（Dieussaert NEJM 2024，PMID 38477988）因早产风险信号而停止开发，这凸显了 Abrysvo 在 32–36 周窗口的合理性 [5]。法国真实世界研究（Gabet Ob/Gyn 2025，n=24891 匹配）显示早产发生率无增加（IRR 0.97，0.89–1.06），提供了额外的安全性证据 [6]。

总结
Abrysvo 已获 FDA（2023 年 8 月）批准用于妊娠 32–36 周母体免疫，以预防婴儿出生至 6 个月的 RSV LRTI [7]。EMA 于 2023 年 8 月批准相同适应症 [8]。美国 CDC/ACIP 建议在 9 月至 1 月期间为妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇接种单剂 RSVpreF（Abrysvo）[9]。总体证据质量：基于单一大型 RCT（MATISSE，RoB-2：低偏倚风险）和 Cochrane 高质量证据，但早产信号尚未完全排除，需持续药物警戒。In pregnant women at 32–36 weeks gestation, the RSVpreF vaccine (Abrysvo, bivalent prefusion F protein) significantly reduces severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness (LRTI) in infants within the first 6 months of life.

Efficacy
In the pivotal phase 3 MATISSE trial (NCT04424316), vaccine efficacy against severe RSV LRTI (defined as hypoxia or tachypnea requiring medical intervention) in infants from birth to 6 months was 81.8% (95% CI 40.6–96.3) [1]. Against any medically attended RSV LRTI, efficacy was 57.1% (95% CI 14.7–79.8) [1]. The MATISSE final analysis (Ob/Gyn 2025, n=7420) reported VE of 70.0% (50.6–82.5) for severe MA-RSV-LRTI at 180 days [2]. The Cochrane 2024 systematic review (n=17991) pooled infant RSV hospitalization HR 0.50 (0.31–0.82) and severe 90-day VE 74% (44–88), rated as high-certainty evidence [3].

Safety
Maternal and neonatal safety profiles were favorable. In MATISSE, rates of serious adverse events (SAEs) were similar between vaccine (13.8%) and placebo (13.5%) groups [1]. Preterm birth rates were numerically higher in the vaccine arm (5.7% vs 4.7% placebo), but this difference was not statistically significant (risk ratio 1.21, 95% CI 0.95–1.55) [1]. No increase in maternal hypertensive disorders or fetal growth restriction was observed. A post-hoc analysis suggested a slight imbalance in very preterm births (<34 weeks) with vaccine (0.8% vs 0.4%), warranting ongoing surveillance [4]. As a comparator, the GSK RSVPreF3-Mat vaccine (Dieussaert NEJM 2024, PMID 38477988) was halted due to a preterm risk signal, underscoring the rationale for Abrysvo's 32–36 week window [5]. A French real-world study (Gabet Ob/Gyn 2025, n=24891 matched) showed no increased preterm risk (IRR 0.97, 0.89–1.06), providing additional reassurance [6].

Summary
Abrysvo is approved by the FDA (August 2023) for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks gestation to prevent RSV LRTI in infants from birth to 6 months [7]. EMA approval followed in August 2023 for the same indication [8]. CDC/ACIP recommends a single dose of RSVpreF (Abrysvo) for pregnant persons at 32–36 weeks gestation during September–January in the US [9]. Overall evidence quality: based on a single large RCT (MATISSE, RoB-2: Low risk of bias) and Cochrane high-certainty evidence, though the preterm signal is not fully resolved and ongoing pharmacovigilance is warranted.

Efficacy
In the pivotal phase 3 MATISSE trial (NCT04424316), vaccine efficacy against severe RSV LRTI (defined as hypoxia or tachypnea requiring medical intervention) in infants from birth to 6 months was 81.8% (95% CI 40.6–96.3) [1]. Against any medically attended RSV LRTI, efficacy was 57.1% (95% CI 14.7–79.8) [1]. The MATISSE final analysis (Ob/Gyn 2025, n=7420) reported VE of 70.0% (50.6–82.5) for severe MA-RSV-LRTI at 180 days [2]. Summary
Abrysvo is approved by the FDA (August 2023) for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks gestation to prevent RSV LRTI in infants from birth to 6 months [7]. EMA approval followed in August 2023 for the same indication [8]. CDC/ACIP recommends a single dose of RSVpreF (Abrysvo) for pregnant persons at 32–36 weeks gestation during September–January in the US [9]. 💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • Anchor 校正补充了 MATISSE 最终分析（180 天 VE 70.0%）和 Cochrane 2024 系统评价（高质量证据），增强了疗效估计的精确性和稳健性。
  • Anchor 校正增加了 GSK RSVPreF3-Mat 疫苗因早产风险停止开发的关键对照信息，以及法国真实世界研究（无早产风险增加）的安全性证据，提供了更全面的安全性背景。
  • LLM 自评 High confidence 被校正为基于单一 RCT（RoB-2 低偏倚风险）和 Cochrane 高质量证据的 Moderate confidence，反映了早产信号的不确定性。
  • Anchor correction adds MATISSE final analysis (180-day VE 70.0%) and Cochrane 2024 systematic review (高质量证据 evidence), improving precision and robustness of efficacy estimates.
  • Anchor correction adds the key comparator of GSK RSVPreF3-Mat vaccine halted for preterm risk, and French real-world study (no increased preterm risk), providing a more comprehensive safety context.
  • LLM self-rated High confidence corrected to Moderate confidence based on single RCT (RoB-2 Low risk of bias) and Cochrane 高质量证据 evidence, reflecting unresolved preterm signal uncertainty.
🤖 OpenEvidence · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**Q：** 在妊娠 32-36 周的孕妇中，接种母体呼吸道合胞病毒（RSV）疫苗（如 RSVpreF / Abrysvo）对于预防婴儿出生后前 6 个月严重 RSV 下呼吸道疾病的有效性和安全性如何？请包括疫苗效力（% 及 95% CI）、关键安全信号和当前的监管状态。

---

在妊娠 32-36 周接种 RSVpreF (Abrysvo) 母体 RSV 疫苗可高效预防婴儿出生后前 6 个月内严重的 RSV 下呼吸道疾病。在关键的 MATISSE 试验中，针对需要医疗干预的严重 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病，接种后 90 天的疫苗效力为 82% (99.5% CI, 40.6–96.3)，180 天时为 69% (97.58% CI, 44.3–84.1)。[1] 一项 Cochrane 系统评价确认，对于 90 天内的严重疾病，疫苗效力为 74% (95% CI, 44–88)。[2]

**预防住院的效力：**

RSVpreF 可减少 RSV 相关住院，在出生后前 6 个月内的疫苗效力为 54% (95% CI, 高 certainty 证据)。[2] 来自阿根廷的真实世界数据显示，3 个月以下婴儿的 RSV 住院率降低了 79%，6 个月以下婴儿降低了 77%。[3]

**安全性概况：**

最常见的不良反应为注射部位疼痛、头痛、肌痛和恶心。[4] 在 MATISSE 试验中，未检测到母亲或婴儿出现严重不良事件的安全信号。[1]

**关键安全信号 - 早产：**

关于早产的潜在安全性担忧已经出现，但证据仍不确定：

- MATISSE 试验（完整接种间隔 24–36 周）：疫苗接种组的早产率为 5.7%，而安慰剂组为 4.7% (RR 1.20, 95% CI, 0.98–1.46) [5]
- 限制性接种间隔（32–36 周）：疫苗组的早产率为 4.2%，安慰剂组为 3.7% [4]
- Cochrane 系统评价：RR 1.16 (95% CI, 0.99–1.36；极低 certainty 证据) [6]
- 最近法国的真实世界数据（n=24,891 对匹配样本）：未见风险增加 (weighted IRR 0.97, 95% CI, 0.89–1.06) [7]

**正在调查的其他安全信号：**

最近的上市后监测（13,619 例接种 RSVpreF 的妊娠病例）检测到妊娠相关高血压疾病 (ARR 1.14–1.29)、胎膜早破 (ARR 1.09–1.14) 和早产前膜早破 (ARR 1.18) 的风险可能升高。[8] 然而，未观察到先天畸形、宫内生长受限或死胎的风险增加。[6][9]

**监管状态：**

- FDA 批准：2023 年 8 月，用于妊娠 32–36 周的母体免疫
- EMA 批准：2023 年
- ACIP 建议（2023 年 9 月）：在妊娠 32–36 周进行季节性接种（美国大部分地区为 9 月至次年 1 月）[4]

ACIP 认为，当接种限制在妊娠 32–36 周时，获益超过潜在风险，因为与 24–36 周的广泛试验间隔相比，这降低了早产风险。[4] 效力的 GRADE 确定性为高；由于结果不一致且精确度不足，早产损害的确定性为极低。[4][6]

**参考文献：**

1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent Prefusion F Vaccine in Pregnancy to Prevent RSV Illness in Infants. The New England Journal of Medicine. 2023. (RCT)
2. Saif-Ur-Rahman KM, King C, Whelan SO, et al. Efficacy and Safety of Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccines. The Cochrane Database of Systematic Reviews. 2025. (NewSR)
3. Jabagi MJ, Bertrand M, Gabet A, et al. Nirsevimab vs RSVpreF Vaccine for Respiratory Syncytial Virus–Related Hospitalization in Newborns. The Journal of the American Medical Association. 2025. (NewObservational)
4. Fleming-Dutra KE, Jones JM, Roper LE, et al. Use of the Pfizer Respiratory Syncytial Virus Vaccine During Pregnancy for the Prevention of Respiratory Syncytial Virus-Associated Lower Respiratory Tract Disease in Infants: Recommendations of the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices - United States, 2023. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023. (Guideline)

(RCT)Maternal RSV vaccination with RSVpreF (Abrysvo) administered at 32–36 weeks' gestation is highly effective in preventing severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness in infants during the first 6 months of life, with vaccine efficacy of 82% (99.5% CI, 40.6–96.3) at 90 days and 69% (97.58% CI, 44.3–84.1) at 180 days against medically attended severe RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness in the pivotal MATISSE trial. [1] A Cochrane meta-analysis confirmed vaccine efficacy of 74% (95% CI, 44–88) for severe illness up to 90 days. [2]

**Efficacy Against Hospitalization:**

RSVpreF reduces RSV-associated hospitalizations with vaccine efficacy of 54% (95% CI, high-certainty evidence) in the first 6 months of life. [2] Real-world data from Argentina demonstrated 79% reduction in RSV hospitalizations in infants <3 months and 77% reduction in those <6 months. [3]

**Safety Profile:**

The most common adverse reactions are injection site pain, headache, myalgia, and nausea. [4] No safety signals were detected in the MATISSE trial for serious adverse events in mothers or infants. [1]

**Key Safety Signal - Preterm Birth:**

A potential safety concern regarding preterm birth has emerged, though evidence remains uncertain:

- MATISSE trial (full dosing interval 24–36 weeks): Preterm birth occurred in 5.7% of vaccine recipients vs. 4.7% of placebo recipients (RR 1.20, 95% CI, 0.98–1.46) [5]
- Restricted dosing interval (32–36 weeks): Preterm birth rate was 4.2% in vaccine group vs. 3.7% in placebo group [4]
- Cochrane meta-analysis: RR 1.16 (95% CI, 0.99–1.36; very low-certainty evidence) [6]
- Recent French real-world data (n=24,891 matched pairs): No increased risk (weighted IRR 0.97, 95% CI, 0.89–1.06) [7]

**Other Safety Signals Under Investigation:**

Recent postlicensure surveillance (13,619 RSVpreF-exposed pregnancies) detected potential elevated risks for pregnancy-associated hypertensive disorders (ARR 1.14–1.29), premature rupture of membranes (ARR 1.09–1.14), and preterm PROM (ARR 1.18). [8] However, no increased risk of congenital abnormalities, intrauterine growth restriction, or stillbirth has been observed. [6][9]

**Regulatory Status:**

- FDA approved: August 2023 for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks' gestation
- EMA approved: 2023
- ACIP recommendation (September 2023): Seasonal administration (September–January in most of US) at 32–36 weeks' gestation [4]

The ACIP judged benefits to outweigh potential risks when vaccination is restricted to 32–36 weeks' gestation, which reduces preterm birth risk compared to the broader 24–36 week trial interval. [4] GRADE certainty for efficacy is high; for preterm birth harm is very low due to inconsistent findings and imprecision. [4][6]
