母体 RSV 疫苗 RSVpreF(Abrysvo)在妊娠 32–36 周接种,对预防婴儿出生后 6 个月内严重 RSV 下呼吸道感染高度有效。关键 MATISSE 注册试验(大型 RCT,偏倚风险低)显示:90 天疫苗效力(VE)为 81.8%(99.5% CI 40.6–96.3),180 天 VE 为 70.0%(97.58% CI 50.6–82.5)[1]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价(纳入 17991 例)汇总显示:严重 RSV 下呼吸道感染 90 天 VE 为 74%(95% CI 44–88),RSV 相关住院风险比(HR)为 0.50(95% CI 0.31–0.82),均为高质量证据[2]。RSVpreF 还减少 RSV 相关住院,VE 为 54%(95% CI,高质量证据)[2]。阿根廷真实世界数据显示,婴儿<3 月龄 RSV 住院减少 79%,<6 月龄减少 77%[3]。

安全性方面,最常见不良反应为注射部位疼痛、头痛、肌痛和恶心[4]。MATISSE 试验未发现母亲或婴儿严重不良事件的安全信号[1]。早产风险是主要关注点:MATISSE 完整接种窗(24–36 周)中,疫苗组早产率 5.7% vs 安慰剂组 4.7%(相对风险 RR 1.20,95% CI 0.98–1.46)[5];限制接种窗(32–36 周)后,疫苗组早产率 4.2% vs 3.7%[4]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价汇总 RR 1.16(95% CI 0.99–1.36,极低质量证据,因不一致性和不精确性降级)[2]。法国近期真实世界数据(24891 对匹配)未发现早产风险增加(加权发病率比 IRR 0.97,95% CI 0.89–1.06)[7]。上市后监测(13619 例 RSVpreF 暴露妊娠)发现妊娠期高血压疾病(调整后风险比 ARR 1.14–1.29)、胎膜早破(ARR 1.09–1.14)和早产胎膜早破(ARR 1.18)的潜在风险升高[8];但未观察到先天性异常、宫内生长受限或死产风险增加[6][9]。

总结:RSVpreF(Abrysvo)在妊娠 32–36 周接种,对预防婴儿出生后 6 个月内严重 RSV 下呼吸道感染高效(180 天 VE 70.0%,高质量证据),并减少 RSV 相关住院。早产风险信号在限制接种窗后减弱,法国真实世界数据提供进一步 reassurance。美国 FDA 于 2023 年 8 月批准,EMA 于 2023 年批准,ACIP 推荐季节性使用(美国 9 月–1 月)于 32–36 周接种[4]。ACIP 判断,当接种限制于 32–36 周时,获益超过潜在风险[4]。Maternal RSV vaccination with RSVpreF (Abrysvo) administered at 32–36 weeks' gestation is highly effective in preventing severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness in infants during the first 6 months of life. The pivotal MATISSE trial (large RCT, low risk of bias) reported vaccine efficacy (VE) of 81.8% (99.5% CI, 40.6–96.3) at 90 days and 70.0% (97.58% CI, 50.6–82.5) at 180 days against medically attended severe RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness [1]. A Cochrane 2024 systematic review (n=17,991) confirmed VE of 74% (95% CI, 44–88) for severe illness up to 90 days and a hazard ratio (HR) of 0.50 (95% CI, 0.31–0.82) for RSV-related hospitalization, both high-certainty evidence [2]. RSVpreF also reduces RSV-associated hospitalizations with VE of 54% (95% CI, high-certainty evidence) [2]. Real-world data from Argentina demonstrated a 79% reduction in RSV hospitalizations in infants <3 months and 77% reduction in those <6 months [3].

Regarding safety, the most common adverse reactions are injection site pain, headache, myalgia, and nausea [4]. No safety signals were detected in the MATISSE trial for serious adverse events in mothers or infants [1]. Preterm birth is the key concern: in the MATISSE full dosing interval (24–36 weeks), preterm birth occurred in 5.7% of vaccine recipients vs. 4.7% of placebo recipients (relative risk [RR] 1.20, 95% CI, 0.98–1.46) [5]; with the restricted dosing interval (32–36 weeks), the rate was 4.2% vs. 3.7% [4]. The Cochrane 2024 meta-analysis reported a pooled RR of 1.16 (95% CI, 0.99–1.36; very low-certainty evidence, downgraded for inconsistency and imprecision) [2]. Recent French real-world data (24,891 matched pairs) found no increased risk (weighted incidence rate ratio [IRR] 0.97, 95% CI, 0.89–1.06) [7]. Postlicensure surveillance (13,619 RSVpreF-exposed pregnancies) detected potential elevated risks for pregnancy-associated hypertensive disorders (adjusted risk ratio [ARR] 1.14–1.29), premature rupture of membranes (ARR 1.09–1.14), and preterm PROM (ARR 1.18) [8]; however, no increased risk of congenital abnormalities, intrauterine growth restriction, or stillbirth has been observed [6][9].

In summary, RSVpreF (Abrysvo) administered at 32–36 weeks' gestation is highly effective in preventing severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness in infants in the first 6 months of life (VE 70.0% at 180 days, high-certainty evidence) and reduces RSV-related hospitalizations. The preterm birth signal is attenuated with the restricted dosing window, and French real-world data provide further reassurance. The FDA approved in August 2023, EMA in 2023, and ACIP recommends seasonal use (September–January in most of the US) at 32–36 weeks' gestation [4]. ACIP judged that benefits outweigh potential risks when vaccination is restricted to 32–36 weeks [4].

The pivotal MATISSE trial (large RCT, low risk of bias) reported vaccine efficacy (VE) of 81.8% (99.5% CI, 40.6–96.3) at 90 days and 70.0% (97.58% CI, 50.6–82.5) at 180 days against medically attended severe RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness [1]. A Cochrane 2024 systematic review (n=17,991) confirmed VE of 74% (95% CI, 44–88) for severe illness up to 90 days and a hazard ratio (HR) of 0.50 (95% CI, 0.31–0.82) for RSV-related hospitalization, both high-certainty evidence [2]. RSVpreF also reduces RSV-associated hospitalizations with VE of 54% (95% CI, high-certainty evidence) [2]. Real-world data from Argentina demonstrated a 79% reduction in RSV hospitalizations in infants <3 months and 77% reduction in those <6 months [3].

Regarding safety, the most common adverse reactions are injection site pain, headache, myalgia, and nausea [4]. No safety signals were detected in the MATISSE trial for serious adverse events in mothers or infants [1]. Preterm birth is the key concern: in the MATISSE full dosing interval (24–36 weeks), preterm birth occurred in 5.7% of vaccine recipients vs. 4.7% of placebo recipients (relative risk [RR] 1.20, 95% CI, 0.98–1.46) [5]; with the restricted dosing interval (32–36 weeks), the rate was 4.2% vs. 3.7% [4]. The Cochrane 2024 meta-analysis reported a pooled RR of 1.16 (95% CI, 0.99–1.36; very low-certainty evidence, downgraded for inconsistency and imprecision) [2]. Recent French real-world data (24,891 matched pairs) found no increased risk (weighted incidence rate ratio [IRR] 0.97, 95% CI, 0.89–1.06) [7]. Postlicensure surveillance (13,619 RSVpreF-exposed pregnancies) detected potential elevated risks for pregnancy-associated hypertensive disorders (adjusted risk ratio [ARR] 1.14–1.29), premature rupture of membranes (ARR 1.09–1.14), and preterm PROM (ARR 1.18) [8]; however, no increased risk of congenital abnormalities, intrauterine growth restriction, or stillbirth has been observed [6][9].

In summary, RSVpreF (Abrysvo) administered at 32–36 weeks' gestation is highly effective in preventing severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness in infants in the first 6 months of life (VE 70.0% at 180 days, high-certainty evidence) and reduces RSV-related hospitalizations. The preterm birth signal is attenuated with the restricted dosing window, and French real-world data provide further reassurance. The FDA approved in August 2023, EMA in 2023, and ACIP recommends seasonal use (September–January in most of the US) at 32–36 weeks' gestation [4]. ACIP judged that benefits outweigh potential risks when vaccination is restricted to 32–36 weeks [4].

  • 早产风险信号在限制接种窗(32–36 周)后减弱,法国真实世界数据未发现风险增加,支持 ACIP 推荐。
  • Anchor 校正未改变 LLM 原始答案的核心结论,但通过引用 Cochrane 系统评价增强了疗效和安全性证据的稳健性。

🤖 ChatGPT 5.5 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
**问题：** 在妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇中，接种母体呼吸道合胞病毒 (RSV) 疫苗（如 RSVpreF / Abrysvo）预防婴儿出生后前 6 个月发生严重 RSV 下呼吸道疾病的有效性和安全性如何？请包括疫苗效力 (% 及 95% CI)、关键安全信号以及目前的监管状态。

---

在孕妇中，证据支持在妊娠 32–36 周时肌肉注射一剂 120 μg 的 RSVpreF / Abrysvo 以预防婴儿 RSV 下呼吸道疾病；在大多数情况下，它是婴儿接种 nirsevimab 的替代方案，通常不具有叠加效应 [1,2]。

在关键的 MATISSE 3 期 RCT 研究中，母体接种 RSVpreF 降低了婴儿发生严重且需就医的 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病的风险：90 天内的疫苗效力为 81.8%，99.5% CI 40.6–96.3；180 天内为 69.4%，97.58% CI 44.3–84.1；GRADE：中等-高，因为这是一项具有显著效应的大规模 RCT，但严重事件数量相对较少 [1]。该疫苗还降低了需就医的 RSV 下呼吸道疾病风险，但精准度较低：90 天内的疫苗效力为 57.1%，99.5% CI 14.7–79.8；180 天内为 51.3%，97.58% CI 29.4–66.8；GRADE：中等 [1]。

安全性总体令人放心，但 RSVpreF 组在早产率上存在数值上的不平衡：约为 5.7% vs 4.7%，RR 约为 1.2，95% CI 跨越 1；GRADE：低，因为这并非确定的因果信号，但影响了标签说明 [1,2]。母亲和婴儿的严重不良事件在两组之间大致相似；反应原性大多为轻至中度，包括注射部位疼痛、头痛、肌痛和疲劳 [1]。早产担忧在临床上具有重要意义，因为另一个使用 RSVPreF3 的母体 RSV 疫苗计划因早产安全信号而停止，因此这仍然是一个需要药物警戒的领域，而非可以忽略的发现 [3]。

监管状态：Abrysvo 在美国获批用于妊娠 32–36 周的母体免疫，以预防婴儿从出生到 6 个月期间的 RSV 下呼吸道疾病；ACIP 建议季节性使用，在美国大陆大部分地区通常为 9 月至次年 1 月 [2]。

**参考文献**

1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent prefusion F vaccine in pregnancy to prevent RSV illness in infants. N Engl J Med. 2023;388(16):1451-1464. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa2216480.
2. Fleming-Dutra KE, Jones JM, Roper LE, et al. Use of the Pfizer respiratory syncytial virus vaccine during pregnancy for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus-associated lower respiratory tract disease in infants: recommendations of the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices — United States, 2023. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023;72:1115-1122.
3. Dieussaert I, Hyung Kim J, Luik S, et al. RSV prefusion F protein-based maternal vaccine — preterm birth and other outcomes. N Engl J Med. 2024;390:1009-1021.

**总体信心：** 中等 —— 一项大规模 3 期 RCT 显示其对严重婴儿 RSV 疾病具有显著保护作用，但严重事件计数以及尚未解决的早产安全信号限制了确定性。In pregnant women, the evidence supports one dose of RSVpreF / Abrysvo 120 μg intramuscularly at 32–36 weeks' gestation to prevent infant RSV lower respiratory tract disease; it is an alternative to infant nirsevimab in most settings, not usually additive [1,2].

In the pivotal MATISSE phase 3 RCT, maternal RSVpreF reduced severe medically attended RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness in infants: vaccine efficacy 81.8%, 99.5% CI 40.6–96.3 through 90 days, and 69.4%, 97.58% CI 44.3–84.1 through 180 days; GRADE: moderate–high because this is a large RCT with a large effect, but relatively few severe events [1]. It also reduced medically attended RSV lower respiratory tract illness, but with less robust precision: vaccine efficacy 57.1%, 99.5% CI 14.7–79.8 through 90 days and 51.3%, 97.58% CI 29.4–66.8 through 180 days; GRADE: moderate [1].

Safety was generally reassuring, but there was a numerical imbalance in preterm birth in the RSVpreF arm: approximately 5.7% vs 4.7%, RR about 1.2, 95% CI crossing 1; GRADE: low because this was not a definitive causal signal but influenced labeling [1,2]. Serious adverse events in mothers and infants were broadly similar between groups; reactogenicity was mostly mild to moderate, including injection-site pain, headache, myalgia, and fatigue [1]. The preterm birth concern is clinically important because a different maternal RSV vaccine programme, using RSVPreF3, was stopped for a preterm birth safety signal, so this remains an area for pharmacovigilance rather than a dismissed finding [3].

Regulatory status: Abrysvo is approved in the US for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks' gestation to prevent RSV lower respiratory tract disease in infants from birth through 6 months; ACIP recommends seasonal use, generally September–January in most of the continental US [2].

**References**

1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent prefusion F vaccine in pregnancy to prevent RSV illness in infants. N Engl J Med. 2023;388(16):1451-1464. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa2216480.
2. Fleming-Dutra KE, Jones JM, Roper LE, et al. Use of the Pfizer respiratory syncytial virus vaccine during pregnancy for the prevention of respiratory syncytial virus-associated lower respiratory tract disease in infants: recommendations of the Advisory Committee on Immunization Practices — United States, 2023. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023;72:1115-1122.
3. Dieussaert I, Hyung Kim J, Luik S, et al. RSV prefusion F protein-based maternal vaccine — preterm birth and other outcomes. N Engl J Med. 2024;390:1009-1021.

**Overall confidence:** Moderate — one large phase 3 RCT shows substantial protection against severe infant RSV disease, but severe-event counts and the unresolved preterm-birth safety signal limit certainty.

图例(Word 修订风格):Legend (Word track-changes style): Anchor 删除 / LLM 原文错处Anchor-deleted / LLM original wrong Anchor 新增 / 替换内容Anchor-added / replacement （旁白 · 切题外插入）(aside · tangential) （证据旁注 · 无 MID/GRADE 等）(evidence caveat · no MID/GRADE …) 未标 = LLM 原话保留(minimal-diff 自证)unmarked = kept verbatim from LLM (proves minimal-diff)
对于妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇，母体接种一剂 RSVpreF / Abrysvo（120 μg 肌注）可预防婴儿出生后头 6 个月的 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病，在多数情况下可作为婴儿尼塞韦单抗（nirsevimab）的替代方案而非叠加使用 [1,2]。

在关键 MATISSE 3 期 RCT（n=7420）中，母体 RSVpreF 降低婴儿严重医疗相关 RSV 下呼吸道感染（MA-RSV-LRTI）的疫苗效力（VE）在 90 天时为 81.8%（99.5% CI 40.6–96.3），在 180 天时为 69.4%（97.58% CI 44.3–84.1）[1]；该 RCT 偏倚风险低（RoB-2: Low），（不适用 GRADE 评级）。Cochrane 2024 系统评价（Phijffer, n=17991）汇总显示婴儿 RSV 住院风险降低（HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.31–0.82），严重 RSV-LRTI 90 天 VE 74%（95% CI 44–88），评为高质量证据 [3]。法国真实世界研究（Gabet, n=24891）进一步支持其有效性 [4]。

安全性方面，MATISSE 试验中早产发生率在疫苗组为 5.7%，安慰剂组为 4.7%（RR 约 1.2, 95% CI 跨 1），该信号未达统计学显著性但影响说明书 [1,2]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价将早产安全性评为极低质量证据（因事件数少且 CI 宽）[3]；法国真实世界研究（n=24891）显示早产发生率无增加（IRR 0.97, 95% CI 0.89–1.06），提供了重要的安全性 reassurance [4]。母体与婴儿的严重不良事件在两组间相似，反应原性多为轻中度（注射部位疼痛、头痛、肌痛、疲劳）[1]。需注意，另一款母体 RSV 疫苗（RSVPreF3）因早产风险信号已终止研发，因此 Abrysvo 的早产信号仍需持续药物警戒 [5]。

监管状态：Abrysvo 已获美国 FDA 批准用于妊娠 32–36 周母体免疫，以预防婴儿从出生至 6 个月的 RSV 下呼吸道疾病；ACIP 推荐季节性使用（美国大陆多数地区为 9 月至次年 1 月）[2]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价（高质量证据）确认了 Abrysvo 在 32–36 周窗口的有效性，但早产安全性证据质量极低，需更多真实世界数据 [3]。In pregnant women at 32–36 weeks' gestation, a single dose of RSVpreF / Abrysvo (120 μg intramuscularly) reduces infant RSV lower respiratory tract disease and is an alternative to infant nirsevimab in most settings, not usually additive [1,2].

In the pivotal MATISSE phase 3 RCT (n=7420), maternal RSVpreF reduced severe medically attended RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness (MA-RSV-LRTI) in infants: vaccine efficacy (VE) 81.8% (99.5% CI 40.6–96.3) through 90 days and 69.4% (97.58% CI 44.3–84.1) through 180 days [1]; this single RCT is assessed as low risk of bias (RoB-2: Low), not GRADE-rated. The Cochrane 2024 systematic review (Phijffer, n=17991) pooled infant RSV hospitalization HR 0.50 (95% CI 0.31–0.82) and severe RSV-LRTI 90-day VE 74% (95% CI 44–88), rated as high-certainty evidence [3]. A French real-world study (Gabet, n=24891) further supports effectiveness [4].

Regarding safety, in MATISSE preterm birth occurred in 5.7% of the vaccine group vs 4.7% of placebo (RR approximately 1.2, 95% CI crossing 1); this signal was not statistically significant but influenced labeling [1,2]. The Cochrane 2024 review rated preterm safety as very low certainty (due to few events and wide CI) [3]; the French real-world study (n=24891) found no increased risk (IRR 0.97, 95% CI 0.89–1.06), providing important reassurance [4]. Serious adverse events in mothers and infants were similar between groups; reactogenicity was mostly mild to moderate (injection-site pain, headache, myalgia, fatigue) [1]. Notably, another maternal RSV vaccine (RSVPreF3) was halted due to a preterm birth signal, so the Abrysvo signal warrants ongoing pharmacovigilance [5].

Regulatory status: Abrysvo is approved in the US for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks' gestation to prevent RSV lower respiratory tract disease in infants from birth through 6 months; ACIP recommends seasonal use (generally September–January in most of the continental US) [2]. The Cochrane 2024 review (high-certainty evidence) confirms Abrysvo efficacy in the 32–36 week window, but preterm safety evidence is very low certainty, requiring more real-world data [3].

对于妊娠 32–36 周的孕妇，母体接种一剂 RSVpreF / Abrysvo（120 μg 肌注）可预防婴儿出生后头 6 个月的 RSV 相关下呼吸道疾病，在多数情况下可作为婴儿尼塞韦单抗（nirsevimab）的替代方案而非叠加使用 [1,2]。

在关键 MATISSE 3 期 RCT（n=7420）中，母体 RSVpreF 降低婴儿严重医疗相关 RSV 下呼吸道感染（MA-RSV-LRTI）的疫苗效力（VE）在 90 天时为 81.8%（99.5% CI 40.6–96.3），在 180 天时为 69.4%（97.58% CI 44.3–84.1）[1]；该 RCT 偏倚风险低（RoB-2: Low），（不适用 GRADE 评级）。Cochrane 2024 系统评价（Phijffer, n=17991）汇总显示婴儿 RSV 住院风险降低（HR 0.50, 95% CI 0.31–0.82），严重 RSV-LRTI 90 天 VE 74%（95% CI 44–88），评为高质量证据 [3]。法国真实世界研究（Gabet, n=24891）进一步支持其有效性 [4]。

安全性方面，MATISSE 试验中早产发生率在疫苗组为 5.7%，安慰剂组为 4.7%（RR 约 1.2, 95% CI 跨 1），该信号未达统计学显著性但影响说明书 [1,2]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价将早产安全性评为极低质量证据（因事件数少且 CI 宽）[3]；法国真实世界研究（n=24891）显示早产发生率无增加（IRR 0.97, 95% CI 0.89–1.06），提供了重要的安全性 reassurance [4]。母体与婴儿的严重不良事件在两组间相似，反应原性多为轻中度（注射部位疼痛、头痛、肌痛、疲劳）[1]。需注意，另一款母体 RSV 疫苗（RSVPreF3）因早产风险信号已终止研发，因此 Abrysvo 的早产信号仍需持续药物警戒 [5]。

监管状态：Abrysvo 已获美国 FDA 批准用于妊娠 32–36 周母体免疫，以预防婴儿从出生至 6 个月的 RSV 下呼吸道疾病；ACIP 推荐季节性使用（美国大陆多数地区为 9 月至次年 1 月）[2]。Cochrane 2024 系统评价（高质量证据）确认了 Abrysvo 在 32–36 周窗口的有效性，但早产安全性证据质量极低，需更多真实世界数据 [3]。In pregnant women at 32–36 weeks' gestation, a single dose of RSVpreF / Abrysvo (120 μg intramuscularly) reduces infant RSV lower respiratory tract disease and is an alternative to infant nirsevimab in most settings, not usually additive [1,2].

In the pivotal MATISSE phase 3 RCT (n=7420), maternal RSVpreF reduced severe medically attended RSV-associated lower respiratory tract illness (MA-RSV-LRTI) in infants: vaccine efficacy (VE) 81.8% (99.5% CI 40.6–96.3) through 90 days and 69.4% (97.58% CI 44.3–84.1) through 180 days [1]; this single RCT is assessed as low risk of bias (RoB-2: Low), not GRADE-rated. The Cochrane 2024 systematic review (Phijffer, n=17991) pooled infant RSV hospitalization HR 0.50 (95% CI 0.31–0.82) and severe RSV-LRTI 90-day VE 74% (95% CI 44–88), rated as high-certainty evidence [3]. A French real-world study (Gabet, n=24891) further supports effectiveness [4].

Regarding safety, in MATISSE preterm birth occurred in 5.7% of the vaccine group vs 4.7% of placebo (RR approximately 1.2, 95% CI crossing 1); this signal was not statistically significant but influenced labeling [1,2]. The Cochrane 2024 review rated preterm safety as very low certainty (due to few events and wide CI) [3]; the French real-world study (n=24891) found no increased risk (IRR 0.97, 95% CI 0.89–1.06), providing important reassurance [4]. Serious adverse events in mothers and infants were similar between groups; reactogenicity was mostly mild to moderate (injection-site pain, headache, myalgia, fatigue) [1]. Notably, another maternal RSV vaccine (RSVPreF3) was halted due to a preterm birth signal, so the Abrysvo signal warrants ongoing pharmacovigilance [5].

Regulatory status: Abrysvo is approved in the US for maternal immunization at 32–36 weeks' gestation to prevent RSV lower respiratory tract disease in infants from birth through 6 months; ACIP recommends seasonal use (generally September–January in most of the continental US) [2]. The Cochrane 2024 review (high-certainty evidence) confirms Abrysvo efficacy in the 32–36 week window, but preterm safety evidence is very low certainty, requiring more real-world data [3].

💡 Anchor 校正要点总结(读者快速版)Anchor correction · Key takeaways (executive summary)
  • Anchor 校正补充了 Cochrane 2024 系统评价（高质量证据）和法国真实世界研究（n=24891），确认了 Abrysvo 在 32–36 周窗口的有效性。
  • 早产安全性信号在 Cochrane 2024 中评为极低质量证据，但法国真实世界研究显示无增加风险（IRR 0.97），提供了重要的 reassurance。
  • LLM 对单一 RCT 误用 GRADE 评级，Anchor 校正为 RoB-2 Low（单一 RCT 不评 GRADE）。
  • Anchor correction adds Cochrane 2024 SR (高质量证据 evidence) and French RWE (n=24891), confirming Abrysvo efficacy in the 32–36 week window.
  • Preterm safety signal rated very low certainty by Cochrane 2024, but French RWE shows no increased risk (IRR 0.97), providing important reassurance.
  • LLM incorrectly applied GRADE to a single RCT; Anchor corrected to RoB-2 Low (single RCT not GRADE-rated).
🤖 Claude 4.7 Thinking · LLM 原文逐字(纯原文,无 Anchor 标记;Anchor 的校正在下方 ③ 校正卡 + ④ 校正版)· LLM verbatim (pure, no Anchor marks; corrections in ③ cards + ④ corrected block below)
在孕期接种母体 RSVpreF（Abrysvo，二价融合前 F 疫苗，单次 120 µg IM 剂量）可有效预防婴儿早期的严重 RSV 下呼吸道感染 (LRTI) [1]。

**有效性 (MATISSE trial; Kampmann et al., NEJM 2023) [1]**
在这项 3 期 RCT 中（约 7,400 例母体接种，疫苗在妊娠 24-36 周时接种），针对婴儿出现需就医的严重 RSV 相关 LRTI 这一主要终点：
- 90 天时：疫苗有效性为 81.8% (99.5% CI 40.6–96.3) [1]
- 180 天时：69.4% (97.58% CI 44.3–84.1) [1]
对于任何（不仅是严重）需就医的 RSV LRTI，有效性较低且未达到预设的统计学成功标准（90 天时约 57%，180 天时约 51%）[1]。保护作用是通过母体抗 RSV F 抗体的胎盘转移实现的，因此在滴度最高的最初几个月内获益最大。

**安全性 [1,2]**
局部/全身反应原性略高于安慰剂，但通常为轻度。重要的是，观察到早产率存在数值上的不平衡（MATISSE 中疫苗组约 5.7% vs 安慰剂组 4.7%；无统计学显著差异）[1]。这一信号——在早先的 GSK 母体 RSV 疫苗计划中被放大（该计划显示出具有统计学显著性的早产增加并被终止）——促使相关建议将 Abrysvo 的接种时间限定在妊娠 32 至 36 周之间，比试验中的 24-36 周窗口期更窄，以减轻任何理论上的早产风险 [2,3]。未出现其他明显的母体/新生儿严重不良事件信号。

**监管/建议状态 [2,3]**
FDA 已批准母体接种 Abrysvo（2023 年 8 月），接种时间为妊娠 32-36 周；ACIP 建议在季节性期间进行母体给药（美国大部分地区为 9 月至次年 1 月）[2]。作为替代方案，婴儿可接种 nirsevimab（长效单克隆抗体）；通常采用母体疫苗接种或婴儿 nirsevimab 其中之一，而非常规两者同时使用 [2]。

总体信心：高 — 有效性和安全性评估源自一项单一的大规模、执行良好的关键 RCT (MATISSE) 并得到了监管审查的证实，主要剩余的不确定性是决定妊娠周数窗口的早产信号。

References
1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent prefusion F vaccine in pregnancy to prevent RSV illness in infants. N Engl J Med. 2023;388(16):1451–1464. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa2216480
2. Fleming-Dutra KE, Jones JM, Roper LE, et al. Use of the Pfizer respiratory syncytial virus vaccine during pregnancy for the prevention of RSV-associated lower respiratory tract disease in infants: recommendations of the ACIP — United States, 2023. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023;72(41):1115–1122.
3. Healy SA, et al. (US FDA Abrysvo prescribing information / approval, 2023).Maternal RSVpreF (Abrysvo, bivalent prefusion F vaccine, single 120 µg IM dose) given in pregnancy is efficacious at preventing severe RSV lower respiratory tract illness (LRTI) in young infants [1].

Efficacy (MATISSE trial; Kampmann et al., NEJM 2023) [1]
In this phase 3 RCT (~7,400 maternal vaccinations, vaccine given at 24–36 weeks gestation), against the primary endpoint of medically attended severe RSV-associated LRTI in infants:
- At 90 days: vaccine efficacy 81.8% (99.5% CI 40.6–96.3) [1]
- At 180 days: 69.4% (97.58% CI 44.3–84.1) [1]
For any (not just severe) medically attended RSV LRTI, efficacy was lower and did not meet the prespecified statistical success criterion (~57% at 90 days, ~51% at 180 days) [1]. Protection is conferred via transplacental transfer of maternal anti-RSV F antibodies, so benefit is greatest in the first months when titers are highest.

Safety [1,2]
Local/systemic reactogenicity was modestly higher than placebo but generally mild. Importantly, a numerical imbalance in preterm birth was observed (~5.7% vaccine vs 4.7% placebo in MATISSE; not statistically significant) [1]. This signal—amplified by the earlier maternal GSK RSV vaccine program (which showed a statistically significant preterm excess and was halted)—drove the recommendation to administer Abrysvo only between 32 and 36 weeks gestation, narrower than the trial's 24–36 week window, to mitigate any theoretical prematurity risk [2,3]. No clear signal for other serious maternal/neonatal adverse events emerged.

Regulatory/recommendation status [2,3]
FDA approved maternal Abrysvo (Aug 2023) for administration at 32–36 weeks gestation; ACIP recommends seasonal maternal dosing (Sep–Jan in most of the US) [2]. As an alternative, infants may instead receive nirsevimab (long-acting monoclonal); generally either maternal vaccination or infant nirsevimab is used, not both routinely [2].

Overall confidence: High — efficacy and safety estimates derive from a single large, well-conducted pivotal RCT (MATISSE) corroborated by regulatory review, with the main residual uncertainty being the preterm-birth signal that shaped the gestational-age window.

References
1. Kampmann B, Madhi SA, Munjal I, et al. Bivalent prefusion F vaccine in pregnancy to prevent RSV illness in infants. N Engl J Med. 2023;388(16):1451–1464. doi:10.1056/NEJMoa2216480
2. Fleming-Dutra KE, Jones JM, Roper LE, et al. Use of the Pfizer respiratory syncytial virus vaccine during pregnancy for the prevention of RSV-associated lower respiratory tract disease in infants: recommendations of the ACIP — United States, 2023. MMWR Morb Mortal Wkly Rep. 2023;72(41):1115–1122.
3. Healy SA, et al. (US FDA Abrysvo prescribing information / approval, 2023).

▸临床落地建议 + 框架合规Clinical action + framework compliance基于校正后的临床决策 + framework v1.9 自检post-correction clinical decision + framework v1.9 self-check

✅ 临床落地(基于本案 Anchor 校正后,你的临床决策应这样做)✅ Clinical take-home (after Anchor correction, your clinical decision should be)

  • 推荐 32–36 周孕妇接种 Abrysvo / RSVpreF(VE 70% severe RSV LRTI at 180d,FDA Aug 2023 批准,ACIP 2023 推荐季节性接种 9 月–1 月)—— 为新生儿提供出生后头 6 个月被动保护。
  • 不要混淆 Abrysvo(Pfizer)和 GSK RSVPreF3-Mat —— 后者因 preterm 信号停止开发(Dieussaert NEJM 2024)。3 LLM 中只 ChatGPT 引此对照,DS / OE 漏 —— 这是 Anchor 关键校正点。
  • Preterm 信号持续监测:MATISSE 24–36 周 RR 1.20(0.98–1.46);32–36 周 restricted 4.2% vs 3.7%;法国真实世界(n=24891)IRR 0.97(0.89–1.06)reassurance;但其他妊娠并发症信号(高血压 / PROM)in surveillance 中,临床应告知患者风险-获益。
  • Recommend Abrysvo / RSVpreF at 32–36 周 gestation (VE 70% severe RSV LRTI at 180d, FDA Aug 2023 approval, ACIP 2023 seasonal Sep–Jan) — provides infants ~6 months of passive protection.
  • Do NOT confuse Abrysvo (Pfizer) with GSK RSVPreF3-Mat — the latter was halted for preterm signal (Dieussaert NEJM 2024). Only ChatGPT cites this comparator; DS / OE miss it — a key Anchor correction.
  • Continued preterm signal monitoring: MATISSE 24–36 wk RR 1.20 (0.98–1.46); 32–36 wk restricted 4.2% vs 3.7%; French RWE (n=24,891) IRR 0.97 (0.89–1.06) reassurance; but other pregnancy complication signals (HTN / PROM) in surveillance — clinicians should transparently discuss risk-benefit.

▸Anchor 的价值主张(投资人视角)Value proposition (investor view)
我们不和 LLM 比谁更聪明 —— 我们让任何 LLM 在受监管医疗里可部署We do not compete with LLMs on intelligence — we make any LLM deployable in regulated medicine
🔍
可核验Verifiable
每条主张逐条 PubMed 核验 —— 抓出伪造引用(本 demo 里 DeepSeek/OpenEvidence 每 case 伪造 3–7 条)Every claim checked against PubMed — catches fabricated citations (DeepSeek/OpenEvidence fabricated 3–7 per case here)
📋
可审计Auditable
PMID → 受控 KB → GRADE → 审核留痕,向 FDA/NMPA 可辩护;黑盒 LLM 的「相信我」无法审计PMID → curated KB → GRADE → reviewer trail, defensible to FDA/NMPA; a black-box 'trust me' cannot be audited
🎯
接地(填召回)Grounded (fills recall)
当最佳 LLM 也弃答时(Claude:「无法核实该试验」),AnchorAI 用当前核验证据给出答案 + PMID + 效应量When even the best LLM abstains (Claude: 'I can't verify that trial'), AnchorAI answers with current verified evidence + PMID + effect size
🔌
模型无关Model-agnostic
核验任何 LLM(客户的栈往往不是 Claude);LLM 越强,AnchorAI 越低成本确认+加审计链 —— 对 LLM 进步反脆弱Verifies any LLM (the customer's stack is rarely Claude); the better the LLM, the cheaper AnchorAI validates + audits — anti-fragile to LLM progress
护城河不是核验逻辑,而是当前、受控、可审计的证据库(5,568 证据 · 30 病种 · 含 2025–26 最新试验)—— LLM 变强不会帮你建出它。The moat is not the verification logic — it is the current, curated, auditable evidence base (5,568 evidence · 30 diseases · incl. 2025–26 trials). A stronger LLM does not build that for you.
